# 六爻 · 疾病 · 症候

*Liu Yao (six-line coin divination) · Illness · Symptoms: which organ or part is ill, by phase, trigram, position and spirit*

49 条。原文逐字核对过 `functions/_lib/doctrine/books/` 下的文本,行号是那份整理本的行号,不是原刻本的页码。「规则(整理)」是本库的归纳,引用时引原文。

## LY.ILL.BSQS.01 六位取象表·疾病：初爻足、二爻腿肢、五爻心肺、六爻头脑(三四两格原文空缺)

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卜筮全书的「六爻诸占定位」表里，疾病一行按初爻到六爻的次序是：足、腿肢、（空缺）、（空缺）、心肺、头脑。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the table of the position of the six lines for the various divinations in the Bushi Quanshu, the row for illness runs, from the first line to the sixth: the feet, legs and limbs, (missing), (missing), heart and lungs, head and brain.

**《卜筮全书·卜筮全书歌诀·二十九、六爻诸占定位》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3253–3258 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Verses of the Bushi Quanshu, no. 29: the position of each of the six lines for the various divinations", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 3253–3258*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 初爻 | 初爻 | the first line |
| 二爻 | 二爻 | the second line |
| 三爻 | 三爻 | the third line |
| 四爻 | 四爻 | the fourth line |
| 五爻 | 五爻 | the fifth line |
| 六爻 | 六爻 | the sixth line |

**《卜筮全书·卜筮全书歌诀·二十九、六爻诸占定位》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3308–3309 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Verses of the Bushi Quanshu, no. 29: the position of each of the six lines for the various divinations", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 3308–3309*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 疾病 | 疾病（这一行） | illness (this row) |
| 足 | 足（初爻） | the feet (the first line) |

**《卜筮全书·卜筮全书歌诀·二十九、六爻诸占定位》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3310 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Verses of the Bushi Quanshu, no. 29: the position of each of the six lines for the various divinations", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3310*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 腿肢 | 腿和肢（二爻） | the legs and limbs (the second line) |

**《卜筮全书·卜筮全书歌诀·二十九、六爻诸占定位》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3313–3314 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Verses of the Bushi Quanshu, no. 29: the position of each of the six lines for the various divinations", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 3313–3314*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 心肺 | 心和肺（五爻） | the heart and lungs (the fifth line) |
| 头脑 | 头和脑（六爻） | the head and brain (the sixth line) |

> **按**:这是一张表，原书竖排，这个文件里改成横排：表头是初爻到六爻，下面每一行是一种占问（天时、家宅、身命、国事、鬼神、求谋、疾病、六畜……），按位置一格对一爻。疾病一行有两格原文模糊、无法辨认，这里没有补，只收读得出的四格：足（初爻）、腿肢（二爻）、心肺（五爻）、头脑（六爻）；「一格对一爻」是按表里的位置读的，书里没有另写说明。表前的说明（今人加的）说这一表大约是从《鬼谷分爻》辑录来的，那段说明不收。这一行和易隐引鬼谷分爻（LY.ILL.YY.02）、断易天机的鬼谷辨爻法（LY.ILL.DYTJ.01）是同一类说法，「初足、二腿、五心、六头」几家一致，三、四爻各有各的说法，见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.11 的「打架」。
>
> **Note:** This is a table, written vertically in the original and turned horizontal in this file: the head of the table is the first to sixth lines, and each row below is one kind of divination (the weather, the house, the person and life, affairs of state, spirits, seeking and planning, illness, livestock, and so on), one cell to a line by position. In the row for illness two cells are blurred in the original and cannot be made out; they are not filled in here and only the four readable cells are taken: the feet (first line), the legs and limbs (second line), the heart and lungs (fifth line) and the head and brain (sixth line); 'one cell to a line' is read by position in the table, and the book does not say so separately. The explanation before the table (added by a modern hand) says that this table seems to have been collected from 'Guigu's division of the lines', and that explanation is not quoted. This row is of the same kind as the Yi Yin's quotation of Guigu's division of the lines (LY.ILL.YY.02) and the Duanyi Tianji's table under Guigu's name (LY.ILL.DYTJ.01); 'first the feet, second the legs, fifth the heart, sixth the head' is agreed by them, and the third and fourth lines are put differently in each, see the dispute in LY.ILL.DYTJ.11.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.11、LY.ILL.DYTJ.01、LY.ILL.YY.02、LY.ILL.BSQS.02

## LY.ILL.BSQS.26 柳神经·鬼与病：心气病火鬼动、脾胃灾土鬼摇；青龙六位巽宫家有头风之疾、玄武初爻鬼动必生脚湿之人；初位杀空小儿难养、六爻木鬼老者中风、上透金爻终年眼暗、五爻火鬼痨瘵缠身

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:柳神经里把鬼爻的五行、所在的位置和病挂在一起：火鬼动是心气病，土鬼摇是脾胃病；青龙在六位、巽宫，家里有头风；玄武在初爻、鬼动，生脚湿的人；初爻的杀落空，小孩难养；六爻木鬼，老人中风；上面透出金爻，终年眼暗；五爻火鬼，痨瘵缠身。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Liushen Jing ties the element of a ghost line, its place and an illness together: a Fire ghost moving is illness of the heart qi, an Earth ghost stirring is trouble of the spleen and stomach; the Azure Dragon in the sixth place and the Xun palace gives the household head-wind; the Dark Tortoise on the first line with the ghost moving gives someone with damp feet; a Void killing on the first place makes a small child hard to raise; a Wood ghost on the sixth line, an old person has a stroke; a Metal line showing through above, dim eyes all the year; a Fire ghost on the fifth line, consumption clings to the body.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·五、柳神经》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9664 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 5: the Liushen Jing", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9664*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 心气病，火鬼动； | 心气的病，是火鬼发动； | Illness of the heart qi: a Fire ghost moves; |
| 脾胃灾，土鬼摇。 | 脾胃的灾，是土鬼摇动。 | trouble of the spleen and stomach: an Earth ghost stirs. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·五、柳神经》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9657 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 5: the Liushen Jing", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9657*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 青龙六位巽宫， | 青龙在六位、巽宫， | With the Azure Dragon in the sixth place and the Xun palace, |
| 家有头风之疾； | 家里有头风的病； | the household has head-wind (chronic headache); |
| 玄武初爻鬼动， | 玄武在初爻、鬼爻发动， | with the Dark Tortoise on the first line and the ghost moving, |
| 必生脚湿之人。 | 一定生出脚湿的人。 | someone with damp feet is sure to appear. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·五、柳神经》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9665 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 5: the Liushen Jing", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9665*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 初位杀空， | 初位杀空， | With a killing spirit Void in the first place, |
| 小儿难养； | 小孩难养； | a small child is hard to raise; |
| 六爻木鬼， | 六爻木鬼， | a Wood ghost on the sixth line, |
| 老者中风。 | 老人中风； | an old person has a stroke; |
| 上透金爻， | 上面透出金爻， | a Metal line showing through above, |
| 终年眼暗； | 终年眼睛昏暗； | the eyes are dim all the year; |
| 五爻火鬼， | 五爻火鬼， | a Fire ghost on the fifth line, |
| 痨瘵缠身。 | 痨瘵缠身。 | consumption clings to the body. |

> **按**:柳神经是阐幽精要里占家宅的一篇，前后各联讲房屋、牲畜、神鬼，这里收的几联把鬼爻和病挂上：心气病配火鬼、脾胃灾配土鬼（本篇疾病论里也是这样，见 LY.ILL.BSQS.32、LY.ILL.BSQS.43）。「头风」是长年头痛；「脚湿」是脚上湿病（脚气一类）；「痨瘵」是肺痨；「中风」「眼暗」照字面。「初位杀空」「上透金爻」两处文意不全（杀是哪一种杀、金爻透在哪里），书里没有注，译文照字面。
>
> **Note:** The Liushen Jing is an essay on divining a house in the Chanyou Jingyao; its couplets run through buildings, livestock and spirits, and the ones taken here tie a ghost line to an illness: a heart-qi illness goes with a Fire ghost and trouble of the spleen and stomach with an Earth ghost (so also in this book's chapter on illness, see LY.ILL.BSQS.32 and LY.ILL.BSQS.43). 'Head-wind' is a long-standing headache; 'damp feet' is a damp disease of the feet (of the beriberi kind); 'consumption' is pulmonary consumption; 'stroke' and 'dim eyes' are literal. In 'a killing spirit Void in the first place' and 'a Metal line showing through above' the sense is incomplete (which killing spirit, and where the Metal line shows through); the book gives no note and the translation keeps to the letter.

相关:LY.ILL.BSQS.32、LY.ILL.BSQS.43

## LY.ILL.BSQS.28 参玄赋·家宅占的病句：父爻入墓家多疾病之人；兄克金财妻有病；日克火爻当生眼疾、日冲坎位定主耳聋

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:参玄赋里讲家宅时提到病的几句：父爻入了墓，家里多有生病的人；兄爻克金财，妻子有病；日辰克火爻，要生眼病；日辰冲坎位，一定主耳聋。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The lines on illness in the Canxuan Fu's discussion of a house: with the Resource line entering a tomb, the household has many sick people; with the brother line controlling a Metal Wealth line, the wife is ill; when the Day controls the Fire line, eye disease will arise; when the Day clashes the Kan place, deafness is sure.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·六、参玄赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9671 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 6: the Canxuan Fu", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9671*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父爻入墓， | 父爻入了墓， | With the Resource line entering a tomb, |
| 家多疾病之人； | 家里多有生病的人； | the household has many sick people; |
| 财位临空， | 财位落空， | with the Wealth place Void, |
| 宅有产亡之鬼。 | 宅里有难产而死的鬼。 | the house has the ghost of one who died in childbirth. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·六、参玄赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9684 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 6: the Canxuan Fu", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9684*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄克金财妻有病， | 兄爻克金财，妻子有病， | With the brother line controlling a Metal Wealth line, the wife is ill, |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·六、参玄赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9686 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 6: the Canxuan Fu", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9686*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 日克火爻， | 日辰克火爻， | When the Day controls the Fire line, |
| 当生眼疾； | 就要生眼病； | eye disease will arise; |
| 日冲坎位， | 日辰冲坎位， | when the Day clashes the Kan place, |
| 定主耳聋。 | 一定主耳聋。 | deafness is sure. |

> **按**:参玄赋是阐幽精要里的一篇赋，占家宅。「兄克金财妻有病」的「兄」是兄爻（兄弟爻），「金财」是五行属金的妻财爻，妻子看财爻，所以是妻子有病；这一句后面接的「弟临火应友多非」讲朋友，不收。「日克火爻」「日冲坎位」的日是日辰。「宅有产亡之鬼」是宅里有难产而死的人的鬼魂，不是病，但和前半句同在一联，所以带着。
>
> **Note:** The Canxuan Fu is a fu in the Chanyou Jingyao, on divining a house. In 'the brother line controls a Metal Wealth line: the wife is ill' the brother line is the Peer line and the Metal Wealth line is the Wealth line of the Metal element; a wife is read from the Wealth line, so it is the wife who is ill; the next clause, about friends, is not taken. In 'the Day controls the Fire line' and 'the Day clashes the Kan place' the Day is the Day branch. 'The ghost of one who died in childbirth' is not illness, but it is in the same couplet as the first half and is carried with it.

相关:LY.ILL.BSQS.27

## LY.ILL.BSQS.32 疾病论·五行与病位：金木动骨骼疼酸、水火兴皮肤寒热；丑未伤脾、戌辰破腹；土伏土爻生肿毒、金藏金位动心胞；妻在土兴呕吐；木下木多寒燠、水伏水多冷淋

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:疾病论里按五行和地支配病：金、木动，骨骼疼酸；水、火兴，皮肤寒热；丑、未交重必伤脾；戌、辰发动是因为腹部的病；土伏在土爻下，生肿毒；金藏在金位，动心包；妻财在土上兴旺，呕吐；木下伏木、木多，忽冷忽热；水伏在水下、水多，冷淋。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The chapter on illness matches illnesses to the five elements and branches: Metal or Wood moving, bones and joints ache; Water or Fire rising, the skin has chills and fever; Chou or Wei moving is sure to harm the spleen; Xu or Chen moving comes from trouble of the belly; Earth hidden under an Earth line gives swollen toxin; Metal hidden in a Metal place stirs the pericardium; Wealth on Earth and rising, vomiting; Wood under Wood, with much Wood, blowing hot and cold; Water hidden under Water, with much Water, cold strangury.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9771 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9771*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金木动， | 金、木发动， | With Metal or Wood moving, |
| 骨骼疼酸； | 骨头关节酸痛； | the bones and joints ache; |
| 水火兴， | 水、火兴旺， | with Water or Fire rising, |
| 皮肤寒热。 | 皮肤发冷发热。 | the skin has chills and fever. |
| 交重丑未必伤脾， | 丑、未交重，必定伤脾； | With Chou or Wei moving, the spleen is sure to be harmed; |
| 发动戌辰因破腹。 | 戌、辰发动，是因为腹部的病。 | with Xu or Chen moving, it comes from trouble of the belly. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9773 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9773*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土伏土爻生肿毒， | 土伏在土爻下，生肿毒； | Earth hidden under an Earth line gives swollen toxin; |
| 金藏金位动心胞。 | 金藏在金位，动心包。 | Metal hidden in a Metal place stirs the pericardium. |
| 妻在土兴呕吐， | 妻财在土上兴旺，呕吐； | Wealth on Earth and rising, vomiting; |
| 官临土动迟疑； | 官鬼临在土上发动，拖延迟疑； | a Pressure line on Earth and moving, delay and doubt; |
| 木下木多寒燠， | 木下伏木、木多，忽冷忽热； | Wood hidden under Wood, with much Wood, blowing hot and cold; |
| 水伏水多冷淋。 | 水伏水下、水多，冷淋。 | Water hidden under Water, with much Water, cold strangury. |

> **按**:这几联把五行（金木水火土）或地支（丑未、戌辰）配上身体部位和症状。「破腹」字面是腹破，这里按腹泻、腹痛一类的肚腹病读，书里没有另注。「迟疑」讲的是官鬼临土发动的结果，字面不像症状，可能是病拖着不好，译文照字面写作「拖延迟疑」。「寒燠」是忽冷忽热，「冷淋」是小便涩痛而冷的淋病，「心胞」是心包。「土伏土爻」「木下木多」按字面是伏神五行同于飞神（或卦中某五行多）的意思。这一组和本书的五行论病歌（LY.ILL.BSQS.43）、柳神经的火鬼动心气病（LY.ILL.BSQS.26）配法大体相通。
>
> **Note:** These couplets match the five elements (Metal, Wood, Water, Fire, Earth) or branches (Chou and Wei, Xu and Chen) to parts of the body and to symptoms. 'Broken belly' is literally a belly that has burst, and is read here as an upset belly or pain of the abdomen; the book gives no note. 'Hesitation' is the result of a Pressure line on Earth moving; as a symptom it reads oddly and may mean the illness drags on, so the translation keeps it as 'delay and doubt'. 'Blowing hot and cold' is alternating cold and heat; 'cold strangury' is a strangury, a painful and dribbling urination, with cold; the 'pericardium' is the heart's envelope. 'Earth hidden under an Earth line' and 'Wood under Wood' mean, literally, a hidden line of the same element as the flying line (or much of one element in the hexagram). This group is in general akin to the verse on illness by the five elements in this book (LY.ILL.BSQS.43) and to the Liushen Jing's 'heart-qi illness: a Fire ghost moves' (LY.ILL.BSQS.26).

相关:LY.ILL.BSQS.43、LY.ILL.BSQS.26、LY.ILL.CA.02

## LY.ILL.BSQS.33 疾病论·八卦鬼与病位：巽鬼股痛、震官足疾不瘳；离宫疮痍、火鬼心目之灾；兑官面病、坎鬼腹中灾；乾官头痛、坤鬼肚疼；坎水耳疾、艮土手灾

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:疾病论里按八卦配病位：巽宫鬼爻兴旺，大腿痛；震宫官鬼旺动，脚病不愈；离宫旺相、内卦发动，是疮疡一类的病；火鬼休囚、外卦交重，是心和眼睛的灾；兑宫官鬼发动，是面部的病；坎宫鬼爻交重，是腹中的灾；乾宫官鬼旺相，是头痛；坤宫鬼爻兴旺，是肚子疼；坎卦水爻兴旺，只因为耳病；艮宫土爻发动，一定是手的灾。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The chapter on illness matches the trigrams to the place of illness: the ghost of Xun thriving, pain in the thigh; the Pressure line of Zhen strong and moving, a disease of the foot that does not heal; Li strong and its inner lines moving, a disease of sores and wounds; a Fire ghost resting or confined with the outer lines moving, trouble of the heart and eyes; the Pressure line of Dui moving, illness of the face; a Kan ghost moving, trouble within the belly; the Pressure line of Qian strong, headache; the Kun ghost thriving, belly pain; the Water line of Kan rising, only an ear disease; the Earth line of Gen moving, sure to be trouble of the hand.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9771 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9771*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 巽鬼兴隆， | 巽宫的鬼爻兴旺， | With the ghost of the Xun palace thriving, |
| 乃为股痛； | 就是大腿痛； | it is pain in the thigh; |
| 震官旺动， | 震宫的官鬼旺相发动， | with the Pressure line of the Zhen palace strong and moving, |
| 足疾不瘳。 | 脚上的病不能痊愈。 | a disease of the foot does not heal. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9772 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9772*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 离宫旺相， | 离宫旺相， | With the Li palace strong, |
| 内爻发动， | 内卦发动， | and its inner lines moving, |
| 疮痍之疾； | 是疮疡创伤一类的病； | it is a disease of sores and wounds; |
| 火鬼休囚， | 火鬼休囚， | with a Fire ghost resting or confined, |
| 外象交重， | 外卦交重， | and the outer lines moving, |
| 心目之灾。 | 是心和眼睛的灾。 | it is trouble of the heart and eyes. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9773 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9773*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 发动兑官因面病， | 兑宫的官鬼发动，是因为面部的病； | With the Pressure line of the Dui palace moving, it is from illness of the face; |
| 交重坎鬼腹中灾。 | 坎宫的鬼爻交重，是腹中的灾。 | with a Kan ghost moving, it is trouble within the belly. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9774 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9774*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 乾官旺相因头痛， | 乾宫的官鬼旺相，是因为头痛； | With the Pressure line of the Qian palace strong, it is from headache; |
| 坤鬼兴隆为肚疼。 | 坤宫的鬼爻兴旺，是肚子疼。 | with the Kun ghost thriving, it is belly pain. |
| 坎卦水兴只缘耳疾， | 坎卦水爻兴旺，只因为耳病； | with the Water line of the Kan trigram rising, it is only from an ear disease; |
| 艮宫土发必为手灾。 | 艮宫土爻发动，一定是手的灾。 | with the Earth line of the Gen palace moving, it is sure to be trouble of the hand. |

> **打架**:八卦各管身体哪一处，几处说法不同。疾病论这一段是「乾头、坤肚腹、坎耳（另一联又说腹中）、艮手、震足、巽股、离疮痍和心目、兑面」；卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.12）是「乾头、坤腹、坎耳、离目、兑口、艮手、震足、巽股」，和这一段在巽股、艮手上一致；孙膑断疾病歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.03）是巽肠、艮手；阐奥歌章（LY.ILL.CA.02）是巽手、艮鼻。不同的地方有三处：兑，这里是面，别处是口；坎，这一段本身就有两说，一联说耳、一联说腹中；离，这里多了疮痍和心，别处只有目。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The books differ on which part of the body each trigram governs. This passage of the chapter on illness gives 'Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ear (and in another couplet the inside of the belly), Gen the hand, Zhen the foot, Xun the thigh, Li sores and heart and eyes, Dui the face'. The Bushi Yuangui passage (LY.ILL.DYTJ.12) gives 'Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ear, Li the eye, Dui the mouth, Gen the hand, Zhen the foot, Xun the thigh', which agrees with this passage on Xun the thigh and Gen the hand; Sun Bin's verse (LY.ILL.DYTJ.03) has Xun the intestines and Gen the hand; the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.02) has Xun the hand and Gen the nose. There are three places of difference: Dui is the face here and the mouth elsewhere; Kan has two readings within this one passage, the ear in one couplet and the inside of the belly in another; and Li has sores and the heart added here, where elsewhere there is only the eye. The books do not decide.

> **按**:这一段里的「官」是官鬼的简称（「兑官」「乾官」「震官」），「鬼」同指鬼爻。「乾官旺相因头痛」等句的主语是某一宫里的官鬼爻。「内爻发动」「外象交重」是内卦、外卦发动。「疮痍」是疮疡创伤。坎在这一段里出现两次，一次说腹中灾，一次说耳疾，书里没有解释哪一种对。
>
> **Note:** In this passage 'official' is a short form of Pressure ('the Dui official', 'the Qian official', 'the Zhen official'), and 'ghost' likewise means the ghost line. The subject of lines such as 'the Qian official strong: from headache' is the Pressure line in that palace. 'Inner lines moving' and 'outer image moving' are the inner and outer trigrams moving. 'Sores and wounds' means ulcers and injuries. Kan appears twice in this passage, once as trouble within the belly and once as an ear disease, and the book does not say which is right.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.12、LY.ILL.DYTJ.03、LY.ILL.CA.02

## LY.ILL.BSQS.43 五行论病歌：金鬼牙与头、更兼白虎血脓流；木鬼风疾头眩眼暗；水兴寒热多因疟、痢疾伤风；火鬼虚劳弱症眼疼；土鬼脾胃虚黄肿、间动膈病

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:五行论病歌：金鬼兴隆，病在牙和头，再加白虎，血和脓流；木鬼发动是风病，头晕眼花；水兴是发冷发热，多半是疟疾，痢疾、伤风不容易治；火鬼是虚劳加上虚弱，眼睛疼痛也有来由；土鬼是脾胃虚、黄肿，间爻动要担心膈的病。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The verse on illness by the five elements: a Metal ghost thriving, the teeth and the head, with the White Tiger as well, blood and pus flow; a Wood ghost moving, a wind illness, with dizziness and dim eyes; Water rising, chills and fever mostly from malaria, and dysentery and wind-damage are not easily treated; a Fire ghost, consumption and weakness, and eye pain has its cause too; an Earth ghost, weak spleen and stomach and yellow swelling, and if the between lines move, worry about a diaphragm illness.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十二、五行论病歌》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9805 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 12: verse on illness by the five elements", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9805*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金鬼兴隆牙与头， | 金鬼兴旺，病在牙和头， | A Metal ghost thriving: the teeth and the head; |
| 更兼白虎血脓流。 | 再加白虎，血和脓流出来。 | with the White Tiger as well, blood and pus flow. |
| 木鬼动时风疾症， | 木鬼发动的时候，是风病一类， | A Wood ghost moving: a wind illness; |
| 头眩眼暗几时休。 | 头晕眼花，不知什么时候才好。 | dizziness and dim eyes, when will it end? |
| 水兴寒热多因疟， | 水鬼兴起，发冷发热，多半是疟疾； | Water rising: chills and fever, mostly from malaria; |
| 痢疾伤风未易疗。 | 痢疾、伤风不容易治。 | dysentery and wind-damage are not easily treated. |
| 火鬼虚劳兼弱症， | 火鬼，是虚劳加上虚弱的病， | A Fire ghost: consumption and weakness; |
| 眼目疼痛有来由。 | 眼睛疼痛也有它的来由。 | eye pain has its cause too. |
| 土鬼脾胃虚黄肿， | 土鬼，脾胃虚、黄肿； | An Earth ghost: weak spleen and stomach, yellow swelling; |
| 间动须当膈病愁。 | 间爻动，要担心膈的病。 | if the between lines move, worry about a diaphragm illness. |

> **打架**:五行的鬼各主什么病，几家说法不同。这一首是「金鬼牙与头（加白虎血脓）、木鬼风疾头眩眼暗、水寒热疟痢、火鬼虚劳眼痛、土鬼脾胃虚黄肿」；阐奥歌章（LY.ILL.CA.02）是水腰肾、金肺、火心经、木肝、土脾胃；卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.12）是木鬼身体疼痛、水土鬼痈肿、金鬼牙骨痛；同一本书的疾病论（LY.ILL.BSQS.32）是金木动骨骼疼酸、水火兴皮肤寒热、丑未伤脾。土配脾胃、金配牙，几家相近；火配心（阐奥歌章）、配虚劳眼痛（这一首）、配皮肤寒热（疾病论），木配肝（阐奥歌章）、配风疾头眩（这一首）、配身体疼痛（卜筮元龟），各不相同。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The books differ on what illness each element's ghost governs. This verse has 'Metal ghost teeth and head (with the White Tiger, blood and pus), Wood ghost wind illness, dizziness and dim eyes, Water chills and fever with malaria and dysentery, Fire ghost consumption and eye pain, Earth ghost weak spleen and stomach with yellow swelling'; the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.02) has Water the waist and kidneys, Metal the lungs, Fire the heart channel, Wood the liver, Earth the spleen and stomach; the Bushi Yuangui passage (LY.ILL.DYTJ.12) has a Wood ghost bodily pain, a Water or Earth ghost swollen abscesses, a Metal ghost pain in the teeth and bones; and the chapter on illness in the same book (LY.ILL.BSQS.32) has Metal or Wood moving, aching bones and joints, Water or Fire rising, chills and fever of the skin, Chou or Wei moving, harm to the spleen. Earth matched to the spleen and stomach and Metal to the teeth are close in several; Fire is matched to the heart (Chan'ao Gezhang), to consumption and eye pain (this verse) and to chills and fever of the skin (the chapter on illness), and Wood to the liver (Chan'ao Gezhang), to wind illness and dizziness (this verse) and to bodily pain (Bushi Yuangui), all different. The books do not decide.

> **按**:这是阐幽精要里一首十句的歌，每一句配一种五行的鬼爻和它主的病。「风疾」是风病，头晕、抽搐、中风一类；「虚劳」是体虚而劳损，近于痨病；「膈病」是胸膈的病，噎膈一类；「间动」是间爻（世应之间的爻）发动。书里的病名按字面译，括号里不加现代病名。
>
> **Note:** This is a ten-line verse in the Chanyou Jingyao, each line matching one element's ghost line to the illness it governs. 'Wind illness' is an illness of wind, dizziness, convulsions, strokes and the like; 'consumption' is deficiency and wasting, close to a consumptive illness; 'diaphragm illness' is illness of the chest and diaphragm, of the choking-obstruction kind; 'the between lines move' is the lines between the Self and Other lines moving. The names of illnesses are translated to the letter, without modern disease names added in brackets.

相关:LY.ILL.CA.02、LY.ILL.DYTJ.12、LY.ILL.BSQS.32

## LY.ILL.CA.02 五行脏腑与八卦部位：水腰肾、金肺、火心经、木肝，土爻脾胃休逢杀；乾首坤腹巽手震足兑口艮鼻坎耳离目；一宫杀旺一般灾、子动龙兴保平复；虎鬼同兴应哭泣、龙孙并旺保平安

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:水是腰和肾，金是肺，火主心经，木主肝；土爻是脾胃，不要遇上杀；发动、交重时要仔细看。虎和鬼一起兴动，应该哭泣；龙和子孙一起旺，保平安。乾是头，坤是腹，巽是手，震是足，兑是口，艮是鼻，坎主耳，离主目。哪一宫杀旺，就是那一处的灾；子孙动、青龙兴，保平复。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Water is the waist and kidneys, Metal the lungs; Fire governs the heart channel, Wood the liver; the Earth line is the spleen and stomach and must not meet a killing; if it moves or crosses heavily, look carefully. The Tiger and the ghost rising together: there will surely be weeping; the Dragon and Output both strong: peace is assured. Qian is the head and Kun the belly, Xun the hands and Zhen the feet, Dui the mouth and Gen the nose, Kan governs the ears and Li the eyes. Where a killing is strong in one palace, the calamity is the same there; with Output moving and the Dragon rising, recovery is assured.

**《阐奥歌章·阐奥歌章·二十二、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3986 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Chan'ao Gezhang, chapter 22: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3986*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水为腰肾金为肺， | 水是腰和肾，金是肺， | Water is the waist and kidneys, Metal the lungs; |
| 火主心经木主肝。 | 火主心经，木主肝。 | Fire governs the heart channel, Wood the liver. |
| 土爻脾胃休逢杀， | 土爻是脾胃，不要遇上杀； | The Earth line is the spleen and stomach and must not meet a killing; |
| 发动交重仔细观。 | 发动、交重，要仔细看。 | if it moves or crosses heavily, look carefully. |
| 虎鬼同兴应哭泣， | 虎和鬼一起兴动，应该哭泣； | The Tiger and the ghost rising together: there will surely be weeping; |
| 龙孙并旺保平安。 | 龙和子孙一起旺，保平安。 | the Dragon and Output both strong: peace is assured. |
| 乾为首兮坤为腹， | 乾是头啊坤是腹， | Qian is the head and Kun the belly, |
| 巽为手兮震为足。 | 巽是手啊震是足， | Xun the hands and Zhen the feet, |
| 兑为口兮艮为鼻， | 兑是口啊艮是鼻， | Dui the mouth and Gen the nose, |
| 坎主耳兮离主目。 | 坎主耳啊离主目。 | Kan governs the ears and Li the eyes. |
| 一宫杀旺一般灾， | 哪一宫杀旺，就是同样的灾； | Where a killing is strong in one palace, the calamity is the same there; |
| 子动龙兴保平复。 | 子动龙兴，保平复。 | with Output moving and the Dragon rising, recovery is assured. |

> **打架**:八卦各管身体哪一处，几处说法不同。阐奥歌章这一首（LY.ILL.CA.02）是「乾首、坤腹、巽手、震足、兑口、艮鼻、坎耳、离目」，巽是手、艮是鼻；断易天机的孙膑断疾病歌（见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.03）是「乾首、坤腹、坎耳、震足、巽肠、艮手、兑口、离目」，巽是肠、艮是手；断易天机的卜筮元龟一段（见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.12）是「乾头、坤腹、坎耳、离目、兑口、艮手、震足、巽股」，巽是股、艮是手；卜筮正宗的痘疹章（LY.ILL.BSZZ.23）也是「乾头面、坤腹、震足、巽股、艮手、离目、坎耳、兑口」。乾、坤、坎、离、兑、震六个卦几家一致，不同的只是巽（手、肠或股）和艮（鼻或手）。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which part of the body each of the eight trigrams governs is put differently in several places. This poem of the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.02) has 'Qian the head, Kun the belly, Xun the hands, Zhen the feet, Dui the mouth, Gen the nose, Kan the ears, Li the eyes', with Xun the hands and Gen the nose; Sun Bin's song in the Duanyi Tianji (see LY.ILL.DYTJ.03) has 'Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Zhen the feet, Xun the bowels, Gen the hands, Dui the mouth, Li the eyes', with Xun the bowels and Gen the hands; the Bushi Yuangui passage in the Duanyi Tianji (see LY.ILL.DYTJ.12) has 'Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Dui the mouth, Gen the hands, Zhen the feet, Xun the thighs', with Xun the thighs and Gen the hands; and the smallpox chapter of the Bushi Zhengzong (LY.ILL.BSZZ.23) likewise has 'Qian the head and face, Kun the belly, Zhen the feet, Xun the thighs, Gen the hands, Li the eyes, Kan the ears, Dui the mouth'. Six of the trigrams (Qian, Kun, Kan, Li, Dui, Zhen) are agreed; the difference is only in Xun (the hands, bowels or thighs) and Gen (the nose or the hands). The books do not settle it.

> **按**:这是第一首的中间十二句：五行配脏腑（水腰肾、金肺、火心、木肝、土脾胃），八卦配部位，和虎鬼同兴、龙孙并旺的判语。「龙孙」是青龙和子孙。「虎鬼同兴」的「虎」是白虎。「土爻脾胃休逢杀」按字面译作「the Earth line is the spleen and stomach and must not meet a killing」。「一宫杀旺一般灾」按字面译，意思是杀在哪一个卦宫旺，就是那一个卦所管的部位遭灾。前面的「水为腰肾金为肺」和黄金策、易隐的五行脏腑大体同一说（LY.ILL.YY.11、HJC.16 一带），只是这里水是「腰肾」、火是「心经」，没有小肠。
>
> **Note:** These are the middle twelve lines of the first poem: the five phases matched with the viscera (Water the waist and kidneys, Metal the lungs, Fire the heart, Wood the liver, Earth the spleen and stomach), the eight trigrams matched with body parts, and the verdicts 'the Tiger and the ghost rising together' and 'the Dragon and Output both strong'. 'The Dragon and Output' are the Azure Dragon and the Output line. The 'Tiger' of 'the Tiger and the ghost rising together' is the White Tiger. 'The Earth line is the spleen and stomach and must not meet a killing' is rendered literally. 'Where a killing is strong in one palace, the calamity is the same' says that where a killing is strong in a given palace, the part that trigram governs meets calamity. The earlier 'Water the waist and kidneys, Metal the lungs' is broadly the same as the five phases and viscera of the Huangjin Ce and the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.11, HJC.16 and around), only that here Water is 'waist and kidneys' and Fire 'the heart channel', without the small intestine. 

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 165 行:乾为首，坤为腹
- 《周易尚占》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 784 行:水为腰肾金属肺;异文(相似 0.86) 第 784 行:火主心胸木主肝;异文(相似 0.86) 第 785 行:土爻脾位休逢杀;同文 第 785 行:发动交重仔细观;同文 第 785 行:虎鬼同兴应哭泣;同文 第 785 行:龙孙并旺保平安;同文 第 786 行:乾为首兮坤为腹;同文 第 786 行:巽为手兮震为足;同文 第 786 行:兑为口兮艮为鼻;同文 第 786 行:坎主耳兮离主目;同文 第 787 行:一宫杀旺一般灾;同文 第 787 行:子动龙兴保平复
- 《易冒》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:乾为头兮坤为腹;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:巽为腰兮震为足;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:兑为口兮艮为
- 《易林补遗》:同文 第 469 行:水为腰肾，金为肺;异文(相似 0.86) 第 469 行:火主心胸，木主肝;异文(相似 0.86) 第 469 行:土爻脾胃，休逢
- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 2999 行:乾为首，坤为腹
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1360 行:水为腰肾金为肺;同文 第 1360 行:火主心经木主肝;同文 第 1360 行:土爻脾胃休逢杀;同文 第 1360 行:发动交重仔细观;同文 第 1360 行:虎鬼同兴应哭泣;同文 第 1360 行:龙孙并旺保平安;同文 第 1360 行:乾为首兮坤为腹;同文 第 1360 行:巽为手兮震为足;同文 第 1360 行:兑为口兮艮为鼻;同文 第 1360 行:坎主耳兮离主目;同文 第 1360 行:一宫杀旺一般灾;同文 第 1360 行:子动龙兴保平复

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.01 鬼谷辨爻法·占疾病：初爻足、二爻腿股、三爻腰、四爻脊、五爻心、六爻头

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼谷子辨爻法在占疾病一章的表：初爻管足，二爻管腿和股，三爻管腰，四爻管脊，五爻管心，六爻管头。表的第一行另有丧门、吊客、哭声、棺木、行兆、坟墓六个名目，按六列的次序对着初爻到六爻。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Guigu's table of the lines for the chapter on illness: the first line governs the feet, the second the legs and thighs, the third the waist, the fourth the spine, the fifth the heart and the sixth the head. The table's first row also gives six names, Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, weeping, the coffin, Xingzhao and the grave, which by the order of the six columns stand over the first to the sixth lines.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·鬼谷辨爻法》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1587–1591 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Guigu's method of telling the lines apart", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 1587–1591*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼谷辨爻法 | 鬼谷子辨别各爻的办法 | Guigu's method of telling the lines apart |
| 丧门吊客哭声棺木行兆坟墓 | 丧门、吊客、哭声、棺木、行兆、坟墓（六个名目） | Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, weeping, the coffin, Xingzhao and the grave (six names) |
| 初爻二爻三爻四爻五爻六爻 | 初爻、二爻、三爻、四爻、五爻、六爻 | the first, second, third, fourth, fifth and sixth lines |
| 足腿股腰脊心头 | 足、腿和股、腰、脊、心、头 | the feet, the legs and thighs, the waist, the spine, the heart, the head |

> **打架**:哪一爻管身体哪一处，几本书差得很远。这张表是「初足、二腿股、三腰、四脊、五心、六头」；同一章孙膑断疾病歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.03）是「一五脏、二皮肉、三体骨腰并足、四肺心经、五六头」，和阐奥歌章（LY.ILL.CA.04）同；同一章卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.11）是「初两足、二双腿、三腰背、四心腹肚肠、五肾脏、六头」；易隐引鬼谷分爻（LY.ILL.YY.02）是「初足、二腿股、三肝肾、四脾脏、五心肺、六头脑」。头在六爻、足在初爻，几家一致；中间三爻各说各的。同一本书里，鬼谷辨爻法的表和孙膑歌就对不上。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which line governs which part of the body is put very differently in several books. This table has 'first the feet, second the legs and thighs, third the waist, fourth the spine, fifth the heart, sixth the head'; Sun Bin's song in the same chapter (LY.ILL.DYTJ.03) has 'the first the five viscera, the second skin and flesh, the third body, bones, waist and feet, the fourth lungs and heart channel, the fifth and sixth the head', as in the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.04); the Bushi Yuangui passage in the same chapter (LY.ILL.DYTJ.11) has 'first the two feet, second both legs, third waist and back, fourth heart, belly and bowels, fifth the kidneys, sixth the head'; and the Yi Yin, quoting Guigu's division of the lines (LY.ILL.YY.02), has 'first the feet, second the legs and thighs, third liver and kidneys, fourth the spleen, fifth heart and lungs, sixth the head and brain'. The head at the sixth line and the feet at the first are agreed; the three lines between are put differently in each. Even within this one book the table under Guigu's name and Sun Bin's song do not agree. The books do not settle it.

> **按**:这是一张三行的表，原书排成六列：第一行六个名目，第二行初爻到六爻，第三行六个部位。第一行在原文里连着写、没有标点，这里按六列的次序把丧门配初爻、吊客配二爻、哭声配三爻、棺木配四爻、行兆配五爻、坟墓配六爻；这是照表的列序读的，书里没有另写解释。「行兆」的意思书里没有说，「兆」有墓地的意思，可能是出殡去墓地的行列，这里不能确定，英文只给拼音。同一书的每一章开头都有一张「鬼谷辨爻法」的表，占六畜章那张是初爻鸡鸭、二爻猫犬、三爻猪、四爻羊、五爻牛、六爻马，所以这一行是把六个爻位配给这一章要看的东西。
>
> **Note:** This is a table of three rows, laid out in the book as six columns: the first row six names, the second the first to sixth lines, the third six parts of the body. The first row is written in the original run together without punctuation; here Mourning Gate is taken with the first line, Guest of Condolence with the second, weeping with the third, the coffin with the fourth, Xingzhao with the fifth and the grave with the sixth, which is reading by the order of the columns, and the book does not explain it separately. The book does not say what 'Xingzhao' means; 'zhao' can mean a burial ground and it may be the procession to the grave, but this cannot be settled here, so the English gives only the pinyin. Every chapter of this book opens with a table headed 'Guigu's method of telling the lines apart'; the one for the chapter on livestock runs first line fowl, second cats and dogs, third pigs, fourth sheep, fifth cattle, sixth horses, so this row assigns the six line positions to what that chapter is about.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 443 行:初爻二爻三爻四爻五爻六爻

相关:LY.ILL.CA.04、LY.ILL.DYTJ.03、LY.ILL.DYTJ.11、LY.ILL.YY.02

---

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.03 孙膑断疾病歌(二)：一爻五脏、二皮肉、三体骨腰并足、四肺心经、五六头；游魂恍惚如神触、归魂渐向安；乾首坤腹、坎耳震足巽肠、艮手兑口离目，鬼在其中即患伤

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:一爻管五脏，二爻管皮肉，三爻管体骨、腰和足，四爻管肺和心经，五、六爻管头。游魂卦人恍惚，像被神鬼触到；只有归魂卦渐渐向安。再看八卦：乾是头，坤是腹，坎是耳，震是足，巽是肠，艮是手，兑是口，离是目；鬼在哪一卦里，那一处就生病受伤，时下的术士要仔细推它落在哪里。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The first line governs the five viscera, the second skin and flesh, the third body, bones, waist and feet, the fourth the lungs and heart channel, the fifth and sixth the head. In a Wandering Soul hexagram the person is dazed, as if touched by spirits; only a Returning Soul hexagram gradually turns to peace. Then the trigrams: Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Zhen the feet, Xun the bowels, Gen the hands, Dui the mouth and Li the eyes; whichever of them the ghost is in, that part is ill and injured, and the diviners of the day must carefully work out where it comes to rest.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·孙膑断疾病歌》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1595 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Sun Bin's song on judging illness", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1595*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 一为五脏二皮肉， | 第一爻是五脏，第二爻是皮肉， | the first line is the five viscera, the second skin and flesh, |
| 三为体骨腰并足。 | 第三爻是体骨、腰和足； | the third body, bones, waist and feet; |
| 四肺心经五六头， | 第四爻是肺和心经，第五、第六爻是头； | the fourth is the lungs and heart channel, the fifth and sixth the head; |
| 游魂恍惚如神触， | 游魂卦恍惚，像被神鬼触到； | the Wandering Soul hexagram: dazed, as if touched by spirits; |
| 唯有归魂渐向安， | 只有归魂卦，渐渐向安； | only the Returning Soul gradually turns to peace; |
| 更寻乾首并坤腹。 | 再看乾是头、坤是腹， | look further: Qian is the head and Kun the belly, |
| 坎耳震足并巽肠， | 坎是耳、震是足，再加巽是肠， | Kan the ears, Zhen the feet and Xun the bowels, |
| 艮手兑口兼离目， | 艮是手、兑是口，再加离是目； | Gen the hands, Dui the mouth and Li the eyes; |
| 鬼在其中即患伤， | 鬼在哪一卦里，那一处就生病受伤， | the ghost being in one of them, that part is ill and injured, |
| 时师仔细推归宿。 | 时下的术士要仔细推它落在哪里。 | and the diviners of the day must carefully work out where it comes to rest. |

> **打架**:八卦各管身体哪一处，几处说法不同。孙膑歌这里是「乾首、坤腹、坎耳、震足、巽肠、艮手、兑口、离目」；阐奥歌章（LY.ILL.CA.02）是巽手、艮鼻，其余同；同一章卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.12）是巽股、艮手；卜筮正宗痘疹章（LY.ILL.BSZZ.23）也是巽股、艮手。乾首、坤腹、坎耳、离目、兑口、震足几家一致；巽有手、肠、股三说，艮有鼻、手两说。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which part of the body each of the eight trigrams governs is put differently in several places. Here Sun Bin's song has 'Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Zhen the feet, Xun the bowels, Gen the hands, Dui the mouth, Li the eyes'; the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.02) has Xun the hands and Gen the nose, the rest the same; the Bushi Yuangui passage in the same chapter (LY.ILL.DYTJ.12) has Xun the thighs and Gen the hands; and the smallpox chapter of the Bushi Zhengzong (LY.ILL.BSZZ.23) likewise has Xun the thighs and Gen the hands. Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Dui the mouth and Zhen the feet are agreed; Xun has three readings (hands, bowels, thighs) and Gen two (nose, hands). The books do not settle it.

> **按**:头三句和阐奥歌章的第二首疾病歌（LY.ILL.CA.04）一字不差。「游魂」「归魂」是卦的名称（游魂卦、归魂卦），「恍惚」是神志不清。「时师」按「时下的术士」译。这里的八卦部位是乾头、坤腹、坎耳、震足、巽肠、艮手、兑口、离目，巽配的是肠；别处巽配手或配股，见「打架」。
>
> **Note:** The first three lines are word for word the same as the second illness poem of the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.04). 'Wandering Soul' and 'Returning Soul' are names of hexagrams (the Wandering Soul hexagram and the Returning Soul hexagram), and 'dazed' is clouded consciousness. 'Shi shi' is rendered 'the diviners of the day'. The trigram parts here are Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Zhen the feet, Xun the bowels, Gen the hands, Dui the mouth and Li the eyes, with Xun matched to the bowels; elsewhere Xun is matched to the hands or the thighs, see the dispute.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3991 行:一为五脏二皮肉;同文 第 3991 行:三为体骨腰并足;同文 第 3992 行:四肺心经五六头;同文 第 3992 行:游魂恍惚如神触;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3992 行:惟有归魂渐向安
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1360 行:一为五脏二皮肉;同文 第 1360 行:三为体骨腰并足;同文 第 1360 行:四肺心经五六头;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1360 行:惟有归魂渐向安

相关:LY.ILL.CA.02、LY.ILL.CA.04、LY.ILL.DYTJ.01、LY.ILL.DYTJ.12、LY.ILL.BSZZ.23、LY.ILL.DYTJ.02、LY.ILL.DYTJ.04

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.04 孙膑断疾病歌(三)：木主酸痛火主疮、水土虚黄并肿毒、金为牙齿痛非常；外克内应克世有药频频泻肚肠、内克外世克应无鬼爻多呕吐伤；更看五鬼配五脏病源有短长

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:木主酸痛，火主疮，水和土主虚黄和肿毒，金是牙齿痛得厉害，务必仔细推详。外卦克内卦、应克世，有药也频频泻肚肠；内卦克外卦、世克应，没有鬼爻的话多是呕吐所伤。还要看五鬼配五脏，病源是长是短就在这里。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Wood governs aching pain and Fire governs sores; Water and Earth govern deficiency with yellowing and swollen toxins; Metal is toothache beyond the ordinary; work it out carefully. The outer trigram controlling the inner, the Other controlling the Self: there is medicine, yet he purges from the bowels again and again; the inner controlling the outer, the Self controlling the Other: with no Pressure line it is mostly injury by vomiting. Look further at the five ghosts matched to the five viscera: there is where the source of the illness, short or long, is found.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·孙膑断疾病歌》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1595 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Sun Bin's song on judging illness", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1595*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 木主酸痛火主疮， | 木主酸痛，火主疮； | Wood governs aching pain, Fire governs sores; |
| 水土虚黄并肿毒， | 水、土主虚黄和肿毒； | Water and Earth govern deficiency with yellowing and swollen toxins; |
| 金为牙齿痛非常， | 金是牙齿痛得厉害， | Metal is toothache beyond the ordinary; |
| 切须仔细与推详。 | 务必仔细推详。 | you must work it out carefully. |
| 外克内兮应克世， | 外卦克内卦、应克世， | The outer trigram controlling the inner, the Other controlling the Self: |
| 有药频频泻肚肠， | 有药，却频频泻肚肠； | there is medicine, yet he purges from the bowels again and again; |
| 内克外兮世克应， | 内卦克外卦、世克应， | the inner controlling the outer, the Self controlling the Other: |
| 无鬼爻多呕吐伤。 | 没有鬼爻的话，多是呕吐所伤。 | with no Pressure line it is mostly injury by vomiting. |
| 更看五鬼配五脏， | 还要看五鬼配五脏， | Look further at the five ghosts matched to the five viscera: |
| 的是病源有短长， | 病源是长是短就在这里。 | there is where the source of the illness, short or long, is found. |

> **按**:「五鬼」是五行各自的鬼（金鬼、木鬼、水鬼、火鬼、土鬼）；「五鬼配五脏」是鬼属哪一行、就配哪一脏来看病源，歌里没有把配法列出来。「有药频频泻肚肠」可以读成服了药反而频频泻，也可以读成有药也止不住泻，这里按字面译。「无鬼爻多呕吐伤」是说内克外、世克应而卦里没有鬼爻时，多是呕吐；洞林秘诀一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.16）有同样的一对：我克他先呕逆、他克我泻为伤。「虚黄」按「虚弱发黄」译。
>
> **Note:** The 'five ghosts' are the ghost of each of the five phases (the Metal, Wood, Water, Fire and Earth ghost); 'the five ghosts matched to the five viscera' means looking at the source of the illness by which viscus the phase of the ghost matches, and the song does not list the matching. 'There is medicine, yet he purges again and again' can be read as purging the more after taking medicine, or as medicine unable to stop the purging, and it is rendered literally here. 'With no Pressure line, mostly injury by vomiting' says that when the inner controls the outer and the Self controls the Other and the hexagram has no Pressure line it is mostly vomiting; the Dongling Mijue passage (LY.ILL.DYTJ.16) has the same pair: when I control him there is vomiting first, when he controls me the purging is the harm. 'Xu huang' is rendered 'deficiency with yellowing'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3610 行:外克内兮应克世;同文 第 3610 行:内克外兮世克应
- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 582 行:外克内兮应克世;同文 第 583 行:内克外兮世克应
- 《易林补遗》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1350 行:内克外、及世克应
- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 2225 行:外克内，应克世;异文(相似 0.86) 第 2225 行:内克外，世克应
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1305 行:外克内兮应克世;同文 第 1149 行:内克外兮世克应

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.03、LY.ILL.DYTJ.16、LY.ILL.TXF.03、LY.ILL.HDY.05、LY.ILL.DYTJ.12

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.10 卜筮元龟·占疾病症候：鬼爻持世身则听人语生嗔、眼慌不能视、狂言乱语；酉鬼伤眼、土鬼肿胀、金鬼伤骨节、木鬼体酸痛；鬼爻入墓兼休囚杀气、本宫白虎入墓凶、子不动难救

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼爻持世临身，一听见人说话就生气，眼睛发慌看不见，狂言乱语失了精神，吃喝也难；四墓持世也一样。鬼是酉爻，眼睛遭殃；土鬼肿胀气虚；金鬼持世，伤骨节；木鬼，身体酸痛，头闷心郁结。卦里鬼爻入了墓，又带休囚和杀气，或本宫的白虎入墓，就凶；子孙不动，难救。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With the Pressure line holding the Self and the Body, at the first sound of a voice he grows angry, his eyes are flurried and cannot see, he raves and loses his wits, and food and drink are hard; the Four Tombs holding the Self are the same. A ghost on the You line: the eyes suffer; an Earth ghost: swelling and deficient qi; a Metal ghost holding the Self injures the bone joints; a Wood ghost: aching in the body, a heavy head and a knotted heart. With the Pressure line entering a tomb in the hexagram, together with resting or trapped and a killing aura, or the White Tiger of the hexagram's own palace entering a tomb, it is ominous; if Output does not move he can hardly be saved.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·卜筮元龟》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1677–1678 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Bushi Yuangui", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 1677–1678*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占疾病症候云： | 占疾病的症候，歌诀说： | On the symptoms of illness it says: |
| 凡是鬼爻持世身， | 凡是鬼爻持世、临身， | Whenever the Pressure line holds the Self and the Body, |
| 一闻人语便生嗔， | 一听见人说话就生气， | at the first sound of a person's voice he grows angry, |
| 眼目慌忙不能视， | 眼睛发慌，看不见东西， | the eyes flurried and unable to see, |
| 狂言乱语失精神。 | 狂言乱语，失了精神。 | he raves and speaks wildly and loses his wits. |
| 饮食何能加进啜， | 饮食怎么还能多吃多喝？ | how could he take more food and drink? |
| 四墓持世同上说（3）， | 四墓持世，和上面说的一样； | the Four Tombs holding the Self, the same as said above; |
| 酉爻为鬼眼遭殃， | 酉爻是鬼，眼睛遭殃； | a ghost on the You line: the eyes suffer; |
| 其字两丁穿目穴。 | 这个字像两个「丁」穿过眼窝。 | the character looks like two 'ding' piercing the eye socket. |
| 土爻肿胀气还虚， | 土爻，肿胀，气也虚； | an Earth line: swelling and distension, and the qi deficient; |
| 金爻持世伤骨节， | 金爻持世，伤骨节； | a Metal line holding the Self injures the bone joints; |
| 木爻为鬼体酸痛， | 木爻是鬼，身体酸痛， | a Wood line as the ghost: aching in the body, |
| 脑闷气来心郁结。 | 头脑发闷，气上来，心里郁结。 | the head oppressed, the qi rising, the heart knotted up. |
| 卦象鬼爻入墓里， | 卦象里鬼爻入了墓， | In the hexagram, the Pressure line enters a tomb, |
| 兼带休囚并杀气（4）， | 又带着休囚和杀气， | together with resting or trapped and a killing aura; |
| 本宫白虎入墓凶， | 本宫的白虎入墓，凶； | the White Tiger of the hexagram's own palace entering a tomb is ominous; |
| 子不动兮难救矣。 | 子孙不动，就难救了。 | if Output does not move he can hardly be saved. |

> **按**:「四墓」是辰戌丑未四个墓爻。「其字两丁穿目穴」说的是「酉」字的字形（像两个丁字穿透眼窝），用来解释为什么酉鬼伤眼，是拆字的取象。「饮食何能加进啜」是反问，意思是饮食进不去。「休囚」是休、囚两种衰弱的状态，英文按「resting or trapped」译。「本宫白虎」的「本宫」是这一卦所属的宫，白虎是六神里的白虎。文中的「（3）」「（4）」是今人加的注号，那两条注不收。这一块和天玄赋的「五行鬼」症候（LY.ILL.TXF.03）、海底眼的症候（LY.ILL.HDY.05）是同一类，但这里多了一个酉鬼伤眼、四墓持世的说法。
>
> **Note:** The 'Four Tombs' are the four tomb lines Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei. 'The character looks like two ding piercing the eye socket' speaks of the shape of the character You (a rooster), which looks like two 'ding' piercing the eye socket, and is used to explain why a You ghost injures the eyes; it is taking the image from the shape of a character. 'How could he take more food and drink' is a rhetorical question and means food and drink will not go down. 'Resting or trapped' translates the two weak states of resting and being trapped. The 'own palace' of 'the White Tiger of the own palace' is the palace the hexagram belongs to, and the White Tiger is the one among the six spirits. The '(3)' and '(4)' in the text are modern annotation marks and those annotations are not quoted. This block is of the same kind as the symptoms by the five phases in the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.03) and in the Haidi Yan (LY.ILL.HDY.05), but adds a You ghost injuring the eyes and the Four Tombs holding the Self.

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.03、LY.ILL.HDY.05、LY.ILL.DYTJ.11、LY.ILL.DYTJ.12、LY.ILL.DYTJ.13

---

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.11 卜筮元龟·占病在何处(爻位)：鬼在初爻两足、二爻双腿、三爻腰背、四爻心腹肚肠、五爻肾脏、六爻头

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼在初爻，两足受伤；在二爻，两腿的病不同寻常；在三爻，腰背常发软；在四爻，心、腹和肚肠；在五爻，肾脏，多气胀；在六爻，头上的病是灾殃。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The ghost on the first line: both feet are injured; on the second: an unusual trouble in both legs; on the third: the waist and back are often limp; on the fourth: the heart, belly and bowels; on the fifth: the kidneys, often with distension of qi; on the sixth: trouble in the head is the calamity.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·卜筮元龟》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1679–1680 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Bushi Yuangui", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 1679–1680*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病在何处云： | 占病在什么部位，歌诀说： | On where the illness is, it says: |
| 鬼在初爻两足伤， | 鬼在初爻，两足受伤； | The ghost on the first line: both feet are injured; |
| 二爻双腿患非常， | 二爻，两腿的病不同寻常； | on the second line: an unusual trouble in both legs; |
| 三爻腰背常轻软， | 三爻，腰背常发软； | on the third: the waist and back are often limp; |
| 四爻心腹及肚肠， | 四爻，心、腹和肚肠； | on the fourth: the heart, belly and bowels; |
| 五爻肾脏多气胀， | 五爻，肾脏，多气胀； | on the fifth: the kidneys, often with distension of qi; |
| 六爻头上患为殃。 | 六爻，头上的病是灾殃。 | on the sixth: trouble in the head is the calamity. |

> **打架**:哪一爻管身体哪一处，几本书差得很远。这一块是「初两足、二双腿、三腰背、四心腹肚肠、五肾脏、六头」；同一章鬼谷辨爻法的表（LY.ILL.DYTJ.01）是「初足、二腿股、三腰、四脊、五心、六头」；孙膑断疾病歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.03）和阐奥歌章（LY.ILL.CA.04）是「一五脏、二皮肉、三体骨腰并足、四肺心经、五六头」；易隐引鬼谷分爻（LY.ILL.YY.02）是「初足、二腿股、三肝肾、四脾脏、五心肺、六头脑」。初爻管足、上爻管头，几家一致；中间几爻各说各的，而孙膑歌和阐奥歌章那一说连初爻也不管足。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which line governs which part of the body is put very differently in several books. This block has 'first the two feet, second both legs, third waist and back, fourth heart, belly and bowels, fifth the kidneys, sixth the head'; Guigu's table in the same chapter (LY.ILL.DYTJ.01) has 'first the feet, second the legs and thighs, third the waist, fourth the spine, fifth the heart, sixth the head'; Sun Bin's song (LY.ILL.DYTJ.03) and the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.04) have 'the first the five viscera, the second skin and flesh, the third body, bones, waist and feet, the fourth lungs and heart channel, the fifth and sixth the head'; and the Yi Yin, quoting Guigu's division of the lines (LY.ILL.YY.02), has 'first the feet, second the legs and thighs, third liver and kidneys, fourth the spleen, fifth heart and lungs, sixth the head and brain'. The first line for the feet and the top line for the head are agreed by most of them; the lines between are put differently in each, and the reading of Sun Bin's song and the Chan'ao Gezhang does not even put the feet at the first line. The books do not settle it.

> **按**:「三爻腰背常轻软」的「轻软」按「发软」译。这一块和同一章鬼谷辨爻法的表（LY.ILL.DYTJ.01）、孙膑断疾病歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.03）一样，都是把六个爻位配给身体的六个部位，各家的配法见「打架」。
>
> **Note:** The 'light and soft' of 'the waist and back are often light and soft' is rendered 'limp'. This block, like Guigu's table in the same chapter (LY.ILL.DYTJ.01) and Sun Bin's song (LY.ILL.DYTJ.03), matches the six line positions to six parts of the body; how the books match them is in the dispute.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易冒》:同文 第 1229 行:鬼在初爻两足伤;同文 第 1229 行:二爻双腿患非常;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:三爻腰股常轻软;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:六爻头上患为

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.01、LY.ILL.DYTJ.03、LY.ILL.CA.04、LY.ILL.YY.02、LY.ILL.DYTJ.12

---

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.12 卜筮元龟·占病在何处(八卦五行)：鬼在乾头坤腹、坎耳离目、兑口艮手、震足巽股；木鬼体疼痛、水土鬼痈肿、金鬼牙骨痛

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼在乾是头，在坤病在腹；坎要患耳，离伤目；兑要忧口，艮在手上；震要防足，巽患在股。木爻是鬼，身体疼痛；水、土爻持着，必生痈肿；克象若说是金鬼，以此类推，是牙和骨头痛。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The ghost in Qian is the head, in Kun the illness is in the belly; Kan must mean the ears, Li injures the eyes; Dui: worry for the mouth; Gen: the hands; Zhen: guard the feet; Xun: trouble in the thighs. A Wood line as the ghost: pain in the body; Water or Earth holding it: there will be abscess and swelling; if the controlling image is a Metal ghost, by analogy it is pain in the teeth and bones.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·卜筮元龟》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1680 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Bushi Yuangui", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1680*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在乾头坤患腹， | 鬼在乾是头，在坤病在腹； | The ghost in Qian is the head, in Kun the illness is in the belly; |
| 坎须患耳离伤目， | 坎要患耳，离伤目； | Kan must mean the ears, Li injures the eyes; |
| 兑口须忧艮手间， | 兑要忧口，艮在手上； | Dui: worry for the mouth; Gen: the hands; |
| 震足犹防巽患股。 | 震要防足，巽患在股。 | Zhen: guard the feet; Xun: trouble in the thighs. |
| 木爻为鬼体疼痛， | 木爻是鬼，身体疼痛； | A Wood line as the ghost: pain in the body; |
| 水土持之必痈肿， | 水、土爻持着，必生痈肿； | Water or Earth holding it: there will be abscess and swelling; |
| 克象若言是金鬼， | 克象如果说是金鬼， | if the controlling image is said to be a Metal ghost, |
| 以类推之牙骨痛。 | 以此类推，是牙和骨头痛。 | by analogy it is pain in the teeth and bones. |

> **打架**:八卦各管身体哪一处，几处说法不同。这一块是「乾头、坤腹、坎耳、离目、兑口、艮手、震足、巽股」；孙膑断疾病歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.03）是巽肠、艮手，其余同；阐奥歌章（LY.ILL.CA.02）是巽手、艮鼻，其余同；卜筮正宗痘疹章（LY.ILL.BSZZ.23）是巽股、艮手，同这一块。乾头、坤腹、坎耳、离目、兑口、震足几家一致；巽有股、肠、手三说，艮有手、鼻两说。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which part of the body each of the eight trigrams governs is put differently in several places. This block has 'Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Dui the mouth, Gen the hands, Zhen the feet, Xun the thighs'; Sun Bin's song (LY.ILL.DYTJ.03) has Xun the bowels and Gen the hands, the rest the same; the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.02) has Xun the hands and Gen the nose, the rest the same; and the smallpox chapter of the Bushi Zhengzong (LY.ILL.BSZZ.23) has Xun the thighs and Gen the hands, as here. Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Dui the mouth and Zhen the feet are agreed; Xun has three readings (thighs, bowels, hands) and Gen two (hands, nose). The books do not settle it.

> **按**:前四句按八卦配身体：乾头、坤腹、坎耳、离目、兑口、艮手、震足、巽股。后四句是五行配症候：木鬼疼痛、水土鬼痈肿、金鬼牙骨痛。「克象若言是金鬼」的「克象」不确定所指，按字面译；意思大概是说到金鬼时，照这个类推。五行症候这一半和孙膑断疾病歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.04）、天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.03）是同一类，各家说法大同小异。
>
> **Note:** The first four lines match the trigrams to the body: Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Dui the mouth, Gen the hands, Zhen the feet, Xun the thighs. The last four lines match the five phases to symptoms: a Wood ghost pain, Water and Earth ghosts abscess and swelling, a Metal ghost pain in teeth and bones. What the 'controlling image' of 'if the controlling image is said to be a Metal ghost' points to is not certain and it is rendered literally; it probably means that when a Metal ghost is spoken of, one reasons by analogy. The half on the five phases and symptoms is of the same kind as Sun Bin's song (LY.ILL.DYTJ.04) and the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.03), and the books are much alike.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.03、LY.ILL.CA.02、LY.ILL.BSZZ.23、LY.ILL.DYTJ.04、LY.ILL.TXF.03、LY.ILL.DYTJ.11

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.13 卜筮元龟·占疾病卦例：内戒伤五脏、外戒伤皮肤、骸骨羸瘦、棺椁心惊、血脉疮痍、游魂恍惚、归魂精魄暗扶身

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:内戒卦，病伤五脏；外戒卦，病伤皮肤；骸骨卦，羸弱消瘦；棺椁卦，心惊多恐惧；血脉卦，疮痍；游魂卦，恍惚不安宁；归魂卦，精魄暗中扶着身子，气息沉静，懒得说话。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** An Inner Warning hexagram: the illness injures the five viscera; an Outer Warning hexagram: it injures the skin; a Bones hexagram: frail and wasted; a Coffin hexagram: a startled heart and much fear; a Blood Vessels hexagram: sores; a Wandering Soul hexagram: dazed and not at rest; a Returning Soul hexagram: the spirit-souls quietly support the body, the breath is still and the heart too listless to speak.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·卜筮元龟》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1681–1682 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Bushi Yuangui", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 1681–1682*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占疾病卦例云： | 占疾病的卦例，歌诀说： | On the types of hexagram in divining illness it says: |
| 内戒病云伤五脏， | 内戒卦，病是伤了五脏； | In an Inner Warning hexagram the illness is said to injure the five viscera; |
| 外戒伤害病皮肤， | 外戒卦，伤害的病在皮肤； | in an Outer Warning hexagram the harm is illness of the skin; |
| 骸骨困羸消瘦尽， | 骸骨卦，困顿羸弱，瘦得尽了； | in a Bones hexagram: worn and frail, wasted to nothing; |
| 棺椁心惊多恐忌。 | 棺椁卦，心惊，多有恐惧忌讳。 | in a Coffin hexagram: a startled heart and much fear and dread. |
| 血脉疮痍有无虑， | 血脉卦，疮痍；有没有忧虑（意思不明）； | in a Blood Vessels hexagram: sores and wounds; whether there is anything to worry about (the sense is unclear); |
| 游魂恍惚不安居， | 游魂卦，恍惚，不安宁； | in a Wandering Soul hexagram: dazed, not at rest; |
| 归魂精魄暗扶身， | 归魂卦，精魄暗中扶着身子， | in a Returning Soul hexagram: the spirit-souls quietly support the body, |
| 气息沉静心懒语。 | 气息沉静，心里懒得说话。 | the breath quiet and still and the heart too listless to speak. |

> **按**:内戒、外戒、骸骨、棺椁、血脉、游魂、归魂都是卦的名称：易隐引京房的「十六变卦」，每一宫的十六个变卦里依次有游魂、外戒、内戒、归魂、绝命、血脉、肌肉、骸骨、棺槨、坟墓这些名字（乾宫是晋为游魂、旅为外戒、鼎为内戒、大有为归魂、离为绝命、噬嗑为血脉、颐为肌肉、益为骸骨、无妄为棺槨、同人为坟墓），见 LY.ILL.YY.36。「戒」字按 Warning 译，「棺椁」按 Coffin 译，这是这本库里的译法，原文没有解释。「血疾有无虑」原文作「血脉疮痍有无虑」，「有无虑」意思读不清，按字面译并标出不明。「游魂恍惚」和天玄赋、阐奥歌章说的游魂恍惚是同一个说法（LY.ILL.CA.04、LY.ILL.DYTJ.03）。
>
> **Note:** Inner Warning, Outer Warning, Bones, Coffin, Blood Vessels, Wandering Soul and Returning Soul are all names of hexagrams: the Yi Yin, quoting Jing Fang's 'sixteen changed hexagrams', has among the sixteen changed hexagrams of each palace in order the names Wandering Soul, Outer Warning, Inner Warning, Returning Soul, End of Life, Blood Vessels, Flesh, Bones, Coffin and Grave (in the Qian palace Jin is the Wandering Soul, Lü the Outer Warning, Ding the Inner Warning, Dayou the Returning Soul, Li the End of Life, Shihe the Blood Vessels, Yi (Nourishment) the Flesh, Yi (Increase) the Bones, Wuwang the Coffin and Tongren the Grave), see LY.ILL.YY.36. 'Jie' is rendered 'Warning' and 'guan guo' 'Coffin', which is the rendering used in this library, and the original does not explain them. The original line is 'blood vessels, sores and wounds, whether or not there is worry', and 'you wu lü' does not read clearly, so it is rendered literally and marked as unclear. 'The Wandering Soul dazed' is the same saying as in the Chan'ao Gezhang and Sun Bin's song (LY.ILL.CA.04, LY.ILL.DYTJ.03).

相关:LY.ILL.YY.36、LY.ILL.CA.04、LY.ILL.DYTJ.03、LY.ILL.DYTJ.10

---

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.21 海底眼(断易天机本)·又占症候：金同木四肢、感寒喘气亏羸；辰戌胃胸呕逆、丑连肚腹吐伤脾；火动三焦渴、血心眼目疮痍；水土发寒因冷得、泄泻虚劳耳肾衰

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:金和木，病在四肢，感寒、喘、气亏体弱；辰、戌，胃和胸，呕逆；丑连着肚腹，吐而伤脾；火动，势头到极点，三焦干渴，血、心、眼目有疮痍；水、土发寒，是因受冷得的，泄泻、虚劳，耳和肾衰弱。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Metal and Wood: the four limbs, catching a chill, quick panting, deficient qi and frailty; Chen and Xu: the stomach and chest, vomiting and retching; Chou links with the belly and abdomen, vomiting injures the spleen; Fire moving: at its extreme, the Triple Burner parched, and blood, heart and eyes have sores; Water and Earth cause chills, caught from cold, diarrhoea, consumption, and weakness of the ears and kidneys.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·海底眼》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1732 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Haidi Yan", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1732*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又占症候云： | 又占症候，歌诀说： | On symptoms again it says: |
| 候病金同木四肢， | 诊病：金和木，病在四肢， | In taking the illness: Metal and Wood mean the four limbs, |
| 感寒疾喘气亏羸， | 感寒，喘得急，气亏体弱； | catching a chill, quick panting, deficient qi and frailty; |
| 辰戌胃胸生呕逆， | 辰、戌是胃和胸，生出呕吐逆气； | Chen and Xu are the stomach and chest, giving rise to vomiting and retching; |
| 丑连肚腹吐伤脾。 | 丑连着肚腹，吐而伤脾； | Chou links with the belly and abdomen: vomiting injures the spleen; |
| 火动势极三焦渴， | 火动，势头到极点，三焦干渴， | Fire moving, at its extreme, the Triple Burner parched, |
| 血心眼目有疮痍， | 血、心、眼目有疮痍； | blood, heart and eyes have sores; |
| 水土发寒因冷得， | 水、土发寒，是因受冷得的， | Water and Earth cause chills, caught from cold, |
| 泄泻虚劳耳肾衰（2）。 | 泄泻、虚劳，耳和肾衰弱。 | diarrhoea, consumption, and weakness of the ears and kidneys. |

> **按**:这是海底眼「症候」一段的另一个抄本（LY.ILL.HDY.05 是海底眼本书的一份），字句略有不同：海底眼本书是「肺病金同木四肢，感寒痰喘气亏羸」，这里是「候病金同木四肢，感寒疾喘气亏羸」；又，海底眼本书里「水主发寒因冷得，世泻虚劳耳肾衰」，这里是「水土发寒因冷得，泄泻虚劳耳肾衰」。「（2）」是今人加的注号，那条注不收。
>
> **Note:** This is another copy of the passage on symptoms of the Haidi Yan (LY.ILL.HDY.05 is the copy in the Haidi Yan itself), with slight differences in wording: the Haidi Yan itself has 'lung illness: Metal and Wood the four limbs, catching a chill, phlegm and panting, deficient qi and frailty' where this has 'in taking the illness: Metal and Wood the four limbs, catching a chill, quick panting'; and where the Haidi Yan itself has 'Water governs chills caught from cold, the Self: diarrhoea, consumption, weakness of the ears and kidneys', this has 'Water and Earth cause chills caught from cold, diarrhoea, consumption, weakness of the ears and kidneys'. The '(2)' is a modern annotation mark and that annotation is not quoted.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易冒》:同文 第 1229 行:辰戌胃胸生呕逆;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:水鬼发寒因冷得;异文(相似 0.88) 第 1229 行:泄泻虚劳耳肾衰
- 《海底眼》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 857 行:肺病金同木四肢;同文 第 857 行:辰戌胃胸生呕逆;异文(相似 0.86) 第 859 行:水主发寒因冷得

相关:LY.ILL.HDY.05、LY.ILL.DYTJ.12、LY.ILL.DYTJ.19

## LY.ILL.EL.07 占是病不是病只论官爻：鬼交重必成此症、官不动而生旺亦断此灾、卦无鬼及落空亡或临绝处则似是而非；随官入墓助鬼伤身其祸当兴，要知病散须看子孙

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:以官鬼为主，旺衰为凭。占疾厄是假是真，官鬼交重，病一定兴起；旺相的也会遭这个患；落空、遇绝，就是虚名。随官入墓、助鬼伤身，灾就不是假的，祸一定兴起；日、月制住官鬼，病是实还是不实要看；子孙旺动，病就难成立。注说：占是不是病，只论官爻，再没有别的说法；鬼如果交重，一定成这个症；官虽不动，如果逢生旺，也断有这个灾；卦如果没有鬼、或落空亡、或临绝处，似是而非，病不真；凡是遇上随官入墓、助鬼伤身，其祸应当兴起，灾难回避；要知道病什么时候散，要看子孙，如果子旺官衰、日月和福德，卦内子爻或动而克制，病就散。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Pressure line is the main line, and strength or weakness is the ground. To divine whether an ailment is false or real: if the Pressure line moves, the illness will surely rise; strong, it too suffers this trouble; Void or meeting Extinction, it is an empty name. Following into a tomb and helping the ghost to harm the Self mean the calamity is no false one, and misfortune will surely rise; if the Day and Month control the Pressure line, it remains to be seen whether it is real; Output strong and moving, and the illness is hard to make out. The commentary says: in judging whether it is an illness or not, go by the Pressure line alone, with no other opinion; if the ghost moves, it will surely make this illness; though the Pressure line does not move, if it meets generation or strength, it too means this calamity; if the hexagram has no ghost, or it is Void, or sits at Extinction, it seems so but is not, and the illness is not real; when one meets following into a tomb or helping the ghost to harm the Self, the misfortune should rise and the calamity is hard to avoid; to know when the illness will disperse, look at Output: if Output is strong and Pressure weak, or the Day and Month and the fortune spirit are on it, or Output in the hexagram moves and controls the ghost, the illness disperses.

**《易林补遗·是病非病(章题缺,在出痘之后)》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1274 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Real illness or not (chapter title missing; after the pox)", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1274*

| --- | --- | --- |

**《易林补遗·是病非病(章题缺,在出痘之后)》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1275 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Real illness or not (chapter title missing; after the pox)", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1275*

| 旺相亦然遭此患，临空遇绝是虚名。 | 旺相的也会遭这个患，落空、遇绝，就是虚名。 | strong, it too suffers this trouble; Void or meeting Extinction, it is an empty name. |

| 鬼若交重，必成此症。 | 鬼如果交重，一定成这个症。 | If the ghost moves, it will surely make this illness. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1278 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1278*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |

| 要知病散，须看子孙。 | 要知道病什么时候散，要看子孙。 | To know when the illness will disperse, look at Output. |
| 若得子旺官衰、日月及临福德，卦内子爻或动克制。 | 如果子旺官衰、日月和福德，卦内的子爻或动而克制，病就散。 | If Output is strong and Pressure weak, or the Day and Month and the fortune spirit are on it, or Output in the hexagram moves and controls the ghost, the illness disperses. |

> **按**:这一段也没有章题。它问的是：这算不算病，是真病还是虚惊。判断只有一个：官鬼——交重必成、生旺也成、空绝就不真。第二组歌诀是把随官入墓、助鬼伤身作为「灾非假」的两个实证，而解病的方法是子孙。「灾难，回避」原文如此，大意是灾难以躲过；最后一句「子爻或动克制」没有写完（缺后半句），按上下文译作「子爻动来克制，病就散」。与易冒的「问症发真假」（LY.ILL.YM.31）相同：鬼实则是、鬼虚则非。
>
> **Note:** This passage too has no chapter title. It asks whether this counts as illness, a real illness or a false alarm. There is only one criterion: the Pressure line — moving, it surely forms; strong, it forms; Void or in Extinction, it is not real. The second verse gives following into a tomb and helping the ghost to harm the Self as two proofs that the calamity is 'no false one', and the way to dissolve the illness is Output. 'The calamity is hard to avoid' is as in the original, meaning the calamity can hardly be escaped; the last sentence, 'Output in the hexagram moves and controls', breaks off without the second half and is translated from the context as 'Output moves and controls, and the illness disperses'. It agrees with the Yi Mao on whether the outbreak of symptoms is real (LY.ILL.YM.31): when the ghost is full it is real, and when the ghost is empty it is not.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.06、LY.ILL.EL.08、LY.ILL.YM.31、LY.ILL.YM.24、LY.ILL.HJC.01、LY.ILL.HJC.41

## LY.ILL.EL.16 疾病生克论之一：百病重轻不出五行生死，万民生死难逃八卦兴衰——以水、火、土、金为主象各举其所畏所喜（水畏土喜金木、火土皆动见木反凶，土为用神水木火三动当从吉断……），查何日逢金便决何时作殡

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:百病的轻重，不出五行的生死；万民的生死，逃不出八卦的兴衰。注举了用神为水、为金、为土、为火的几种情形，一一说明畏什么、喜什么：水是主象，怕土，也怕金以外的受制；卦里土静火兴，不必畏忌；爻内火安静、土动，却不吉祥；怕遇上巳、午两个时辰，喜遇上木、金；又如火土都动，见木反而凶，得到酉、申来助水，这病才会痊愈。假如金藏在土下，飞神能生伏神就好；木爻安静，日辰忌卯、寅，木象发动，时辰忧亥、子，这就是所说的遇金则吉、遇水则凶。再拿土作用神，卦里见水、木、火三个爻同时发动，不当作凶来推，应当按吉断；遇午、申的时候，土得到生扶就吉，逢卯、巳的日子，土临死绝就被囚。又论火是用爻，只有水发动的日子遇上戌、辰、丑、未，水被土克无妨；一见酉、申就命尽，只求寅、卯就身安。如果用爻居于衰位、木化入金乡，卦里没有亥、子和父爻兴旺，一定难以救度，查哪一天逢金，就断哪个时候办丧事；倘若金空，就不这样断，节气产到亥、子，命也会回生，细究哪个爻动静，就知道哪天存亡。其中有贪生忘克、救处遭伤，盈虚要精细详审，怎么可以寻常一概而论？

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The lightness or heaviness of a hundred illnesses does not go beyond the birth and death of the five phases; the life and death of the people of ten thousand houses cannot escape the rise and fall of the eight trigrams. The commentary gives cases with the Subject as Water, Metal, Earth and Fire, and says of each what it fears and what it welcomes: with Water as the chief image, it fears Earth; if in the hexagram Earth is still and Fire thriving there is no need to fear it; if in the lines Fire is still and Earth moves, it is not auspicious; it fears meeting the hours Si and Wu, and is glad to meet Wood and Metal; again, if Fire and Earth both move, meeting Wood is on the contrary ominous, and when You and Shen come to help Water the illness will be cured. If Metal lies hidden under Earth, it is good if the flying line can generate the hidden one; if the Wood line is still, the Mao and Yin days are to be feared, and if the Wood image moves, the Hai and Zi hours are a worry: this is what is meant by 'meeting Metal is auspicious, meeting Water ominous'. Taking Earth again as the Subject, if the hexagram shows Water, Wood and Fire lines all three moving, do not judge it ominous but by the auspicious; at the hours Wu and Shen Earth gains generation and support and it is auspicious, and on Mao and Si days Earth on death or Extinction is imprisoned. Again, with Fire as the Subject line, when only Water moves on a day that meets Xu, Chen, Chou or Wei, Water is controlled by Earth and it does not matter; at the first sight of You or Shen life is at an end, and only if one hopes for Yin or Mao is the body safe. If the Subject line sits in a weak place and Wood changes into Metal's country, and the hexagram has no Hai or Zi or a thriving Resource line, rescue is hard, and to find on what day it meets Metal is to decide at what time the coffin is made; if Metal is Void, it is not judged so, and when the solar term reaches Hai and Zi life is restored, and if one examines which line moves and which is still, one knows on which day one lives or dies. Among these are those who, greedy for generation, forget control, or are harmed at the place of rescue, and fullness and emptiness must be examined finely: how can they be judged by the common general rule?

**《易林补遗·疾病生克论》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1297 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "On generation and control in illness", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1297*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |

| 万民生死，难逃八卦兴衰。 | 万民的生死，逃不出八卦的兴衰。 | the life and death of the people of ten thousand houses cannot escape the rise and fall of the eight trigrams. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1298 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1298*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |

| 卦中土静火兴，不须畏忌。 | 卦里土静火兴，不必畏忌。 | if in the hexagram Earth is still and Fire thriving, there is no need to fear it. |

| 怕逢巳午二时喜遇木金二字。 | 怕遇上巳、午两个时辰，喜欢遇上木、金两个字。 | It fears meeting the two hours Si and Wu, and is glad to meet the two, Wood and Metal. |
| 又如火土皆动，见木反凶，得酉申而助水疑此病以方痊。 | 又如火、土都动，见木反而凶，得到酉、申来助水，这病才会痊愈。 | Again, if Fire and Earth both move, meeting Wood is on the contrary ominous, and when You and Shen come to help Water the illness will be cured. |

| 时遇午申，土得生扶则吉，日逢卯巳，土临死绝则囚。 | 遇午、申的时候，土得到生扶就吉，逢卯、巳的日子，土临死绝就被囚。 | at the hours Wu and Shen Earth gains generation and support and it is auspicious, and on Mao and Si days Earth on death or Extinction is imprisoned. |
| 又论火是用爻，独逢水发日遇戌辰丑未，水遭土克无妨，一见酉申当命尽，但求寅卯必身安。 | 又论火是用爻，只有水发动的日子遇上戌、辰、丑、未，水被土克无妨；一见酉、申就命尽，只求寅、卯就身安。 | Again, with Fire as the Subject line, when only Water moves on a day that meets Xu, Chen, Chou or Wei, Water is controlled by Earth and it does not matter; at the first sight of You or Shen life is at an end, and only if one hopes for Yin or Mao is the body safe. |
| 若用居衰木化入金乡，卦无亥子父兴，必难救度，查何日逢金，便决何时作殡。 | 如果用爻居于衰位、木化入金乡，卦里没有亥、子和父爻兴旺，一定难以救度，查哪一天逢金，就断哪个时候办丧事。 | If the Subject line sits in a weak place and Wood changes into Metal's country, and the hexagram has no Hai or Zi or a thriving Resource line, rescue is hard, and to find on what day it meets Metal is to decide at what time the coffin is made. |
| 倘若金空亦非此断，节产亥子，命亦回生，细究何爻动静，便知那日存亡。 | 倘若金空，就不这样断，节气产到亥、子，命也会回生，细究哪个爻动静，就知道哪天存亡。 | If Metal is Void, it is not judged so, and when the solar term reaches Hai and Zi life is restored, and if one examines which line moves and which is still, one knows on which day one lives or dies. |
| 其中有贪生忘克，救处遭伤、盈虚却要精详，岂可寻常概论？ | 其中有贪生忘克、救处遭伤，盈虚要精细详审，怎么可以寻常一概而论？ | Among these are those who, greedy for generation, forget control, or are harmed at the place of rescue, and fullness and emptiness must be examined finely: how can they be judged by the common general rule? |

> **按**:这一段是全部五行—地支推算的例子，密度很高，原文有几处文字可疑：头一句「畏土纵金」的「纵」（放过、让金存在）按上下文译作「让金」；「疑此病以方痊」的「疑」按字面译；第五句的「假令金藏土下，飞能生伏为佳」是说金是用神、藏在土爻之下，土（飞）生金（伏）则好；「用居衰木化入金乡」是用神为木，木变入金地（被金克）；「卦无亥子父兴」的「父」是父爻；「查何日逢金，便决何时作殡」是说用神为木时，金克木，遇金的日子就是死期；这段的含意是逐个五行说清「怕什么、喜什么」，不是要背下每个时辰。「节产亥子」的「产」疑是「交」之误（节气交到亥、子），读法没有把握。
>
> **Note:** This passage is a run of cases reckoned by the five phases and the branches, and dense; a few readings in the original are doubtful: in the first line, 'fears Earth and lets Metal (zong jin)' is rendered from the context as 'lets Metal be'; 'suspects this illness will then be cured' is translated literally; in 'if Metal hides under Earth, it is good if the flying line can generate the hidden one' Metal is the Subject hidden under an Earth line, and Earth (flying) generating Metal (hidden) is good; 'the Subject sits in a weak place and Wood changes into Metal's country' means the Subject is Wood and it changes into a Metal place (controlled by Metal); the 'father' in 'no Hai or Zi or a thriving father' is the Resource line; 'to find on what day it meets Metal is to decide at what time the coffin is made' means that when the Subject is Wood, Metal controls Wood and the day it meets Metal is the day of death; the sense of the passage is to say for each phase in turn 'what it fears and what it welcomes', not to memorise every hour. The 'chan' in 'when the solar term reaches (chan) Hai and Zi' looks like an error for 'jiao' (arrives), and the reading is uncertain.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.15、LY.ILL.EL.17、LY.ILL.HJC.47、LY.ILL.HJC.48、LY.ILL.YM.14、LY.ILL.YM.16

---

## LY.ILL.HDY.05 病症按五行爻位部位断：肺病属金同木主四肢、辰戌胃胸呕逆、丑运肠肚木伤脾；火动热极三焦竭、水主发寒；土动主吐、水动主泻、金动四肢满闷；木足金头土胸腹火手水脾肾；飞为起因伏为受病；世为动爻则内下受病、应为动爻则外上受病、间爻动受阻隔；易镜云长男受病宜纯震不摇、少女染病宜兑不动

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:病症要按五行、爻位、身体部位来断：肺病属金，木主四肢；感受寒邪、痰喘、气弱消瘦；辰戌是胃和胸，生出呕吐逆气；丑运转肠肚，木克伤脾。火动热到极点，三焦枯竭，还有血、心、眼目；水主发寒，是受冷得的；世爻泻泄、虚劳、耳肾衰弱。土动主吐，水动主泻，水动发寒，火动发热，金动四肢和胀闷。木主足，金主头，土主胸腹，火主手，水主脾肾。飞爻、伏爻都旺相时，飞爻是起病的原因，伏爻（原文作「代」）是受病的。世爻是动爻，病在里面下面；应爻是动爻，病在外面上面；间爻动，受阻隔。《易镜》说：长男得病，适宜纯震不摇动；少女染病，适宜兑卦不动。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Symptoms are judged by the five phases, the line positions and the parts of the body: lung illness is Metal, with Wood governing the four limbs; catching cold, phlegm and wheezing, qi weak and emaciated; Chen and Xu are the stomach and chest, giving rise to vomiting and retching; Chou moves the bowels and belly, and Wood wounds the spleen. Fire moving: heat to the extreme, the Triple Burner exhausted, and also blood, heart and eyes; Water governs chills, got through cold; the Self line: diarrhoea, consumptive weakness, decline of ears and kidneys. Earth moving governs vomiting, Water moving governs diarrhoea, Water moving brings chills, Fire moving brings fever, Metal moving the four limbs and fullness and oppression. Wood governs the feet, Metal the head, Earth the chest and belly, Fire the hands, Water the spleen and kidneys. When the flying and hidden lines are both strong, the flying line is the cause that begins the illness and the hidden line (the text has 'substitute') is what receives it. If the Self is the moving line, the illness is received inside, below; if the Other is the moving line, outside, above; if the between line moves, there is obstruction. The Mirror of the Changes says: when the eldest son falls ill, it is fitting that the pure Zhen does not shake; when the youngest daughter is infected, that the Dui hexagram does not move.

**《海底眼·病症》原文** — 《海底眼》第 857 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Symptoms", base text; Haidi Yan, line 857*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |

| 感寒痰喘气[]羸， | 感受寒邪、痰喘，气（此处缺一字）羸弱； | catching cold, phlegm and wheezing, qi (one character is missing here) emaciated; |
| 辰戌胃胸生呕逆， | 辰、戌是胃和胸，生出呕吐逆气； | Chen and Xu are the stomach and chest, giving rise to vomiting and retching; |
| 丑运肠肚木伤脾。 | 丑运转肠肚，木克伤脾。 | Chou moves the bowels and belly, and Wood wounds the spleen. |

**《海底眼·病症》原文** — 《海底眼》第 859 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Symptoms", base text; Haidi Yan, line 859*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又曰： | 又说道： | It says again: |
| 火动热极三焦竭， | 火动是热到极点，三焦枯竭， | Fire moving: heat to the extreme, the Triple Burner exhausted; |
| 血心眼目及痦痰， | 血、心、眼目，以及痦子和痰； | blood, heart, eyes, and rash and phlegm; |
| 水主发寒因冷得， | 水主发寒，是因受冷得的； | Water governs chills, got through cold; |
| 世泻虚劳耳肾衰。 | 世爻泻泄、虚劳、耳和肾衰弱。 | the Self: diarrhoea, consumptive weakness, decline of the ears and kidneys. |

**《海底眼·病症》原文** — 《海底眼》第 861 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Symptoms", base text; Haidi Yan, line 861*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土动主吐， | 土动主吐， | Earth moving governs vomiting; |
| 水动主泻， | 水动主泻， | Water moving governs diarrhoea; |
| 水动发寒， | 水动发寒， | Water moving brings chills; |
| 火动发热， | 火动发热， | Fire moving brings fever; |
| 金动四肢及满闷。 | 金动是四肢和胀闷。 | Metal moving: the four limbs and fullness and oppression. |
| 木主足， | 木主管足， | Wood governs the feet; |
| 金主头， | 金主管头， | Metal governs the head; |
| 土主胸腹， | 土主胸腹， | Earth governs the chest and belly; |
| 火主手， | 火主管手， | Fire governs the hands; |
| 水主脾肾。 | 水主脾肾。 | Water governs the spleen and kidneys. |

**《海底眼·病症》原文** — 《海底眼》第 863 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Symptoms", base text; Haidi Yan, line 863*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 飞伏俱旺相， | 飞爻和伏爻都旺相时， | When the flying and hidden lines are both strong, |
| 飞为起因， | 飞爻是起病的原因， | the flying line is the cause that begins it, |
| 以代为受病。 | 「代」是受病的（疑是「伏」之误）。 | and the 'dai' is what receives the illness (probably a slip for 'fu', hidden). |
| 又世为动爻， | 又，世爻是动爻， | Again, if the Self is the moving line, |
| 在内下受病。 | 就在里面下面受病； | the illness is received inside, below; |
| 应为动爻， | 应爻是动爻， | if the Other is the moving line, |
| 在外上受病。 | 就在外面上面受病； | the illness is received outside, above; |
| 间爻动， | 间爻发动， | if the between line moves, |
| 受阻隔。 | 受到阻隔。 | there is obstruction. |

**《海底眼·病症》原文** — 《海底眼》第 865 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Symptoms", base text; Haidi Yan, line 865*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 易镜云： | 《易镜》这样说： | The Mirror of the Changes says: |
| 且如长男受病， | 比如长男得病， | For example, when the eldest son falls ill, |
| 宜纯震之不摇。 | 适宜纯震卦不摇动； | it is fitting that the pure Zhen does not shake; |
| 少女染病， | 少女染病， | when the youngest daughter is infected, |
| 则兑卦之不动。 | 那就适宜兑卦不动。 | then it is fitting that the Dui hexagram does not move. |

> **按**:这一节按五行、爻位、部位断病，和黄金策「五行推病」、易隐「病症以官鬼为凭」（LY.ILL.YY.11、YY.13）是同一个路子，只是这里更简、更碎，而且有几处和它们不一致：「水主脾肾」把脾也归了水；眉批「巳肠木脾」和歌诀「丑运肠肚木伤脾」的地支不同；「世泻虚劳耳肾衰」的「世泻」没有说清是世爻还是「世」上的泄泻。「气[ ]羸」里的方括号是整理本标的缺字，照抄。「痦痰」的「痦」不常见（白痦是一种皮疹），译作「rash」，不确定。「以代为受病」的「代」疑是「伏」之误：飞爻是起病之因、伏爻是受病之处，和「飞为起因」的说法对得上，这里按字面译并标出。「易镜云」是海底眼引的别的书，火珠林也引了这一句（火珠林末尾多一句「大忌申酉持世，木爻独发者，申为丧车，酉为丧服，木为棺椁耳」）；它的意思是病人是哪一卦（长男是震、少女是兑），那一卦就该纯而不动。 括号里「眉批：……」是书眉上的旁注，整理本排在正文里；这里分开收，说话人标为「海底眼眉批」。
>
> **Note:** This section judges illness by the five phases, the line positions and the body parts, on the same road as the Huangjin Ce's 'the five phases and illness' and the Yi Yin's 'symptoms by the Pressure line' (LY.ILL.YY.11, YY.13), only terser and more fragmentary, and it disagrees with them in a few places: 'Water governs the spleen and kidneys' also gives the spleen to Water; the marginal comment 'Si the bowels, Wood the spleen' has a different branch from the verse 'Chou moves the bowels and belly, Wood wounds the spleen'; and in 'the Self: diarrhoea, consumptive weakness, decline of ears and kidneys' it is not clear whether it is the Self line or a diarrhoea 'of the Self'. The square brackets in 'qi [ ] emaciated' are the edited text's mark for a missing character, and are copied. The 'wu' of 'wu tan' is uncommon (white 'wu' is a kind of rash) and is rendered 'rash', without certainty. The 'dai' in 'the dai receives the illness' is probably a slip for 'fu' (hidden): the flying line is the cause and the hidden line the place that receives the illness, which matches 'the flying line is the cause', and it is translated literally here and marked. 'The Mirror of the Changes says' is another book quoted by the Haidi Yan, and the Huozhulin quotes the same passage (with one more sentence at its end, 'most feared are Shen or You holding the Self and a Wood line moving alone: Shen is the funeral carriage, You is the mourning dress, Wood is the coffin'); its meaning is that the trigram of the sick person (the eldest son being Zhen, the youngest daughter Dui) should be pure and not moving. The bracketed 'marginal comment' is a note from the top margin that the edited text sets in the main text; it is quoted separately here, with the speaker marked as the marginal comment.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1732 行:候病金同木四肢;同文 第 1732 行:辰戌胃胸生呕逆
- 《易冒》:同文 第 1229 行:辰戌胃胸生呕逆
- 《断易天机》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1732 行:水土发寒因冷得;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1732 行:泄泻虚劳耳肾衰
- 《易冒》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:火动热极三焦;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:水鬼发寒因冷得;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1229 行:泄泻虚劳耳肾衰
- 《火珠林》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 440 行:金动四肢或满闷
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1736 行:宜纯震之不摇;异文(相似 0.83) 第 1736 行:宜兑卦之不动
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 441 行:且如长男受病;同文 第 441 行:宜纯震之不摇;同文 第 441 行:则兑卦之不动

相关:LY.ILL.HDY.04、LY.ILL.HDY.06、LY.ILL.YY.11、LY.ILL.YY.13、LY.ILL.YM.26、LY.ILL.HJC.16

## LY.ILL.HJC.02 火属心经、水归肾部、金肺木肝、土侵脾胃：鬼爻的五行定哪一脏受病；衰轻旺重，动则煎迫

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻的五行，定哪一脏受病：火是心经（发热、咽干、口燥），水是肾（怕冷、盗汗、遗精白浊），金是肺（咳嗽、虚怯、气喘），木是肝（感冒风寒、四肢不和），土是脾胃（虚黄、浮肿、瘟疫时气）。鬼爻衰弱病轻、旺相病重；静则安卧，动则煎熬躁急。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The phase of the Pressure line fixes which organ is ill: Fire is the Heart channel (fever, dry throat, dry mouth), Water the Kidney (aversion to cold, night sweats, seminal emission and cloudy urine), Metal the Lung (cough, weak timidity, panting), Wood the Liver (catching wind-cold, limbs out of harmony), Earth the Spleen and Stomach (sallow weakness, swelling, epidemic and seasonal disease). A weak Pressure line means a light illness and a strong one a heavy illness; still, the patient lies quiet, and moving, he is tormented and restless.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6766 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6766*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火属心经，发热咽干口燥； | 火对应心经，病发热、咽喉干、口燥； | Fire belongs to the Heart channel: fever, dry throat and dry mouth; |
| 水归肾部，恶寒盗汗遗精。 | 水归肾这一部，怕冷、盗汗、遗精。 | Water belongs to the Kidney: aversion to cold, night sweats and seminal emission. |
| 金肺木肝，土乃病侵脾胃； | 金对应肺，木对应肝，土就是病侵到脾胃； | Metal is the Lung and Wood the Liver; Earth means the illness invades the Spleen and Stomach; |
| 衰轻旺重，动则煎迫身躯。 | 鬼爻衰弱病轻，旺相病重，发动就煎熬逼迫身体。 | weak, it is light; strong, it is heavy; moving, it gnaws at and presses on the body. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6767 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6767*

| --- | --- | --- |

| 其病必发热，咽干口燥类。 | 这病一定是发热、咽喉干、口燥一类。 | the illness will be of the kind with fever, dry throat and dry mouth. |
| 属水，肾经受病； | 属水，病在肾经； | If Water, the Kidney channel is ill; |
| 其病必恶寒，或盗汗，或遗精白浊类。 | 这病一定是怕冷，或者盗汗，或者遗精、白浊一类。 | the illness will be of the kind with aversion to cold, or night sweats, or seminal emission and cloudy urine. |
| 属金，肺经受病； | 属金，病在肺经； | If Metal, the Lung channel is ill; |
| 其症必咳嗽虚怯，或气喘类。 | 这症一定是咳嗽、虚弱胆怯，或者气喘一类。 | the symptoms will be of the kind with cough and weak timidity, or panting. |
| 属木，肝经受病； | 属木，病在肝经； | If Wood, the Liver channel is ill; |
| 其症必感冒风寒，四肢不和类。 | 这症一定是感冒风寒、四肢不调和一类。 | the symptoms will be of the kind with wind-cold and limbs out of harmony. |
| 属土，脾经受病； | 属土，病在脾经； | If Earth, the Spleen channel is ill; |
| 必虚黄浮肿，瘟疫时气类。 | 一定是虚黄浮肿、瘟疫时气一类。 | it will be of the kind with sallow weakness and swelling, epidemic and seasonal disease. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6768 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6768*

| 旺必重。 | 旺就一定重。 | if strong, it is heavy. |

| 属土脾经受病，其症必虚黄、浮肿或时气瘟疫类。 | 属土是脾经受病，症状一定是虚黄、浮肿，或者时气瘟疫一类。 | if Earth, the Spleen channel is ill, and the symptoms will be of the kind with sallow weakness, swelling, or seasonal epidemic. |
| 或鬼爻衰弱则安卧，发动则烦躁之类也。 | 又如鬼爻衰弱就安静地躺着，发动就烦躁，一类的情形。 | Again, a weak Pressure line means lying quiet, and a moving one means restlessness, and such things. |
