## LY.ILL.HJC.03 鬼在坎宫中满血虚兼湿毒，在离卦内虚眼赤及尿黄

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻在坎宫，病是中满、血虚，兼有湿毒；在离卦，病是内虚、眼红、尿黄。旧注分得更细：坎宫属水，火鬼主血虚、水鬼主湿毒；离宫属火，火鬼主眼红、水鬼主尿黄。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With the Pressure line in the Kan palace, the illness is fullness in the middle and blood deficiency, together with damp toxin; in the Li hexagram it is inner deficiency, red eyes and yellow urine. The old commentary divides it more finely: the Kan palace belongs to Water, where a Fire Pressure means blood deficiency and a Water Pressure damp toxin; the Li palace belongs to Fire, where a Fire Pressure means red eyes and a Water Pressure yellow urine.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6770 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6770*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若在坎宫，中满血虚兼湿毒； | 鬼爻如果在坎宫，是中满、血虚，兼有湿毒； | If it is in the Kan palace: fullness in the middle, blood deficiency, together with damp toxin; |
| 如当离卦，内虚眼赤及尿黄。 | 如果在离卦，是内虚、眼红、尿黄。 | if it is in the Li hexagram: inner deficiency, red eyes and yellow urine. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6773 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6773*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 坎象中满，故主中满； | 坎的卦象中间是实的，所以主中满； | The Kan image is full in the middle, so it means fullness in the middle; |
| 离象中虚，故主内虚。 | 离的卦象中间是空的，所以主内虚。 | the Li image is empty in the middle, so it means inner deficiency. |
| 然坎宫属水，火鬼主血虚，水鬼主湿毒。 | 不过坎宫属水，火鬼主血虚，水鬼主湿毒。 | But the Kan palace belongs to Water: a Fire Pressure means blood deficiency, a Water Pressure means damp toxin. |
| 离宫属火，或火官主眼赤，水鬼主尿黄。 | 离宫属火，火鬼主眼红，水鬼主尿黄。 | The Li palace belongs to Fire: a Fire Pressure means red eyes, a Water Pressure means yellow urine. |

> **按**:这一联卜筮正宗的王洪绪注里没有。旧注里最后一句写作「或火官主眼赤」，「或」应是「火」之误，译文按上文「火鬼」对「水鬼」译。
>
> **Note:** This couplet has no note by Wang Hongxu in the Bushi Zhengzong. The last sentence of the old commentary reads 'or the Fire Pressure means red eyes'; the 'or' is probably a slip, and the translation follows the contrast between the Fire Pressure and the Water Pressure in the sentence before.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1109 行:若在坎宫，中满血虚兼湿毒;同文 第 1109 行:如当离卦，内虚眼赤及尿黄

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.04

## LY.ILL.HJC.04 坤腹乾头、兑喉风咳嗽、艮足震手、巽瘫痪肠风：鬼在八宫的病位；以鬼在坤宫为例，五行之鬼各主一病

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼爻落在哪一宫，病就在哪个部位：坤是腹、乾是头，兑是喉风咳嗽，艮和震是手足，巽是瘫痪、肠风。以鬼在坤宫为例：火鬼腹痈，水鬼腹痛，动化财爻或财化水鬼是泻痢，土鬼是癖块食积或沙胀蛊症，木鬼是绞肠痧痛或大肠有病，金鬼是胁肋痛（在上胸痛、在下腰痛）。别的卦照此推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Where the Pressure line falls in the palaces fixes where the illness is: Kun is the belly and Qian the head; Dui is throat wind and cough; Gen and Zhen are the hand and foot; Xun is paralysis and intestinal wind. Taking Pressure in the Kun palace as the example: a Fire Pressure is an abdominal abscess, a Water Pressure pain in the belly; moving and changing into a Wealth line, or Wealth changing into a Water Pressure, is diarrhea and dysentery; an Earth Pressure is abdominal masses and food accumulation, or sha bloating and gu-swelling; a Wood Pressure is twisting colic or trouble in the large intestine; a Metal Pressure is pain in the flanks (chest if high, loins if low). The other palaces follow by analogy.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6771 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6771*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 坤腹乾头，兑必喉风咳嗽； | 坤是腹、乾是头，兑必定是喉风、咳嗽； | Kun is the belly and Qian the head; Dui is surely throat wind and cough; |
| 艮足震手，巽须瘫痪肠风。 | 艮是足、震是手，巽必定是瘫痪、肠风。 | Gen is the foot and Zhen the hand; Xun is surely paralysis and intestinal wind. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6776–6777 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6776–6777*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在坤宫，腹中有病。 | 鬼爻落在坤宫，病在腹中。 | If the Pressure is in the Kun palace, the illness is in the belly. |
| 火鬼：必患腹痈。 | 火鬼：一定得腹痈。 | Fire Pressure: surely an abdominal abscess. |
| 水鬼：腹中疼痛。 | 水鬼：腹中疼痛。 | Water Pressure: pain in the belly. |
| 动化财爻，或财化水鬼，必患泻痢。 | 动了化成财爻，或者财爻化成水鬼，一定得泻痢。 | Moving and changing into a Wealth line, or Wealth changing into a Water Pressure: surely diarrhea and dysentery. |
| 土鬼：则是癖块，食积，或沙胀蛊症。 | 土鬼：是癖块、食积，或者沙胀蛊症。 | Earth Pressure: abdominal masses, food accumulation, or sha bloating and gu-swelling. |
| 木鬼：绞肠痧痛，或大肠有病。 | 木鬼：绞肠痧痛，或者大肠有病。 | Wood Pressure: twisting colic, or trouble in the large intestine. |
| 金鬼：胁肋痛。 | 金鬼：胁肋痛。 | Metal Pressure: pain in the flanks. |
| 在上，胸隔痛； | 在上，胸膈痛； | If high, pain in the chest and diaphragm; |
| 在下，腰痛。 | 在下，腰痛。 | if low, pain in the loins. |
| 此鬼在坤宫断法，其诸卦仿此推之。 | 这是鬼在坤宫的断法，其余各卦仿照这个来推。 | This is the method for Pressure in the Kun palace; the other trigrams are reasoned in the same way. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2305 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2305*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在坤宫，腹中有病，火鬼必患腹疾； | 鬼在坤宫，病在腹中，火鬼一定得腹病； | Pressure in the Kun palace: illness in the belly; a Fire Pressure surely brings a belly complaint; |
| 水鬼腹中疼痛； | 水鬼是腹中疼痛； | a Water Pressure is pain in the belly; |
| 动化财或化水鬼必泻痢； | 动了化成财爻，或者化成水鬼，一定泻痢； | moving and changing into Wealth or into a Water Pressure: surely diarrhea and dysentery; |
| 土鬼则是食积痞块或痧胀蛊症； | 土鬼是食积痞块，或者痧胀蛊症； | an Earth Pressure is food accumulation and lumps, or sha bloating and gu-swelling; |
| 木鬼绞肠痧痛，或大肠有病； | 木鬼是绞肠痧痛，或者大肠有病； | a Wood Pressure is twisting colic, or trouble in the large intestine; |
| 金鬼胁肋疼痛，在上胸痛，在下腰痛。 | 金鬼是胁肋疼痛，在上胸痛，在下腰痛。 | a Metal Pressure is pain in the flanks, in the chest if high, in the loins if low. |
| 此鬼在坤宫断，余卦类推之。 | 这是鬼在坤宫的断法，其余的卦类推。 | This is judged for Pressure in the Kun palace; the other trigrams follow by analogy. |

> **按**:经文和旧注写「艮足震手」，卜筮正宗写成「艮手、震足」；本章下面讲震宫的一条（LY.ILL.HJC.19）是「鬼在震宫，病在足」，和卜筮正宗的写法相合。卜筮大全旧注这一条的前半（「鬼在坤宫，腹中有病……土鬼：」）在前面重复抄了一次，这里只收完整的那一次。「沙胀蛊症」「绞肠痧」是旧医书里的病名，书里没有解释，译文保留拼音。
>
> **Note:** The verse and the old commentary write 'Gen the foot, Zhen the hand', while the Bushi Zhengzong writes 'Gen the hand, Zhen the foot'; the entry below in this chapter on the Zhen palace (LY.ILL.HJC.19) says 'Pressure in the Zhen palace, the illness is in the foot', which agrees with the Bushi Zhengzong. In the Bushi Daquan the first half of this commentary ('Pressure in the Kun palace, illness in the belly ... Earth Pressure:') is copied twice, and only the complete copy is taken here. 'Sha bloating and gu-swelling' and 'twisting colic (jiaochang sha)' are disease names from old medical books which the book does not explain, and the translation keeps the sounds.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2305 行:坤腹、乾头，兑必喉风咳嗽;异文(相似 0.8) 第 2305 行:艮手、震足，巽须瘫痪肠风
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1112 行:坤腹乾头，兑必喉风咳嗽;同文 第 1112 行:艮足震手，巽须瘫痪肠风

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.03、LY.ILL.HJC.19

## LY.ILL.HJC.05 六神临鬼各主一症：螣蛇心惊、青龙酒色过度、勾陈肿胀、朱雀言语颠狂、白虎损伤血崩血晕、玄武忧郁阴虚

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻临着哪个六神，就是哪一种病：螣蛇鬼坐卧不安、心神不定；青龙鬼酒色过度、瘦弱无力；勾陈鬼胸腹胀满、脾胃不和；朱雀鬼狂言乱语、身热面赤；白虎鬼跌打损伤，女人则血崩血晕、产后各症（白虎是血神）；玄武鬼色欲过度、忧闷在心，在本宫主阴虚。最后一句叫人变通着断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Which of the six spirits the Pressure line sits on fixes the kind of illness: Coiling Snake Pressure, unable to sit or lie at ease and the mind unsettled; Azure Dragon Pressure, excess of wine and lust, thin and without strength; Hook Earth Pressure, fullness and swelling, Spleen and Stomach out of harmony; Vermilion Bird Pressure, wild talk, hot body and red face; White Tiger Pressure, falls and blows and injury, and in a woman flooding and blood-faintness and the ailments after childbirth (the White Tiger is the spirit of blood); Dark Tortoise Pressure, excess of lust and gloom pent in the heart, and in the home palace yin deficiency. The last sentence tells one to judge flexibly.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6779 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6779*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 螣蛇心惊，青龙则酒色过度； | 螣蛇是心惊，青龙就是酒色过度； | The Coiling Snake means fright of the heart; the Azure Dragon means excess of wine and lust; |
| 勾陈肿胀，朱雀则言语颠狂。 | 勾陈是肿胀，朱雀就是言语颠狂。 | Hook Earth means swelling; the Vermilion Bird means raving speech. |
| 虎有损伤，女子则血崩血晕； | 白虎是损伤，女人就是血崩血晕； | The Tiger means injury, and in a woman flooding and blood-faintness; |
| 玄武忧郁，男人则阴症阴虚。 | 玄武是忧郁，男人就是阴症阴虚。 | the Dark Tortoise means gloom, and in a man yin syndrome and yin deficiency. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6780 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6780*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 螣蛇鬼：则坐卧不安，心神不定。 | 螣蛇鬼：坐也不安，躺也不安，心里定不下来。 | Coiling Snake Pressure: unable to sit or lie at ease, the mind unsettled. |
| 青龙鬼：则酒色过度，瘦弱无力。 | 青龙鬼：酒和女色过度，人瘦弱，没有力气。 | Azure Dragon Pressure: excess of wine and lust, thin and weak, without strength. |
| 勾陈鬼：饱满肿胀，脾胃不和。 | 勾陈鬼：肚腹饱胀，身体肿胀，脾胃不调和。 | Hook Earth Pressure: fullness and swelling, Spleen and Stomach out of harmony. |
| 朱雀鬼：狂言乱语，身热面赤。 | 朱雀鬼：说狂话、乱话，身上发热，脸红。 | Vermilion Bird Pressure: wild and confused talk, hot body and red face. |
| 白虎鬼：跌扑打斗，伤筋损骨； | 白虎鬼：摔跤、扑倒、打架，伤到筋骨； | White Tiger Pressure: falls, blows and fights, harm to sinews and bones; |
| 女人则血崩血晕，产后诸症； | 女人则是血崩血晕，和产后各种症候； | in a woman, flooding and blood-faintness and the various ailments after childbirth; |
| 白虎血神，故也。 | 因为白虎是血神。 | for the White Tiger is the spirit of blood. |
| 玄武鬼：色欲太过，忧闷在心； | 玄武鬼：贪恋色欲过度，忧愁郁闷积在心里； | Dark Tortoise Pressure: excess of lust, gloom pent in the heart; |
| 在本宫主阴虚； | 在本宫就是阴虚； | in the home palace it means yin deficiency; |
| 化子孙，男子必阳痿； | 化出子孙，男子一定阳痿； | changing into Output, a man surely suffers impotence; |
| 盖玄武又暗昧之神，故也。 | 因为玄武又是暗昧的神。 | for the Dark Tortoise is also the spirit of the hidden and obscure. |
| 宜通变。 | 要变通着断。 | Judge it flexibly. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2307 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2307*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 螣蛇鬼则坐卧不安，心神不定； | 螣蛇鬼是坐卧不安，心神不定； | Coiling Snake Pressure: unable to sit or lie at ease, the mind unsettled; |
| 青龙鬼则酒色过度，虚弱无力； | 青龙鬼是酒色过度，虚弱无力； | Azure Dragon Pressure: excess of wine and lust, weak and without strength; |
| 勾陈鬼胸满肿胀，脾胃不和； | 勾陈鬼是胸满肿胀，脾胃不和； | Hook Earth Pressure: fullness in the chest and swelling, Spleen and Stomach out of harmony; |
| 朱雀鬼狂言乱语，身热面赤； | 朱雀鬼是狂言乱语，身热面赤； | Vermilion Bird Pressure: wild talk, hot body and red face; |
| 白虎鬼跌打气防，伤筋损骨，女人血崩血晕，产后诸症，盖白虎血神故也； | 白虎鬼是跌打损伤，伤筋损骨，女人血崩血晕、产后各种症候，因为白虎是血神； | White Tiger Pressure: falls and blows, harm to sinews and bones, and in a woman flooding and blood-faintness and the ailments after childbirth, for the White Tiger is the spirit of blood; |
| 玄武鬼色欲太过，郁闷在心，在本宫主阴虚，化子孙男子阴症阴虚，盖玄武暗昧之神故也。 | 玄武鬼是色欲太过，郁闷在心，在本宫主阴虚，化出子孙，男子是阴症阴虚，因为玄武是暗昧的神。 | Dark Tortoise Pressure: excess of lust, gloom pent in the heart; in the home palace yin deficiency; changing into Output, a man's yin syndrome and yin deficiency, for the Dark Tortoise is the spirit of the hidden and obscure. |
| 断宜通变。 | 断的时候要变通。 | Judge it flexibly. |

> **按**:玄武鬼化子孙：旧注说男子必阳痿，王洪绪写成男子阴症阴虚，两家只差一个病名，意思都是男子的房室虚损。卜筮正宗白虎鬼一句的「跌打气防」四个字不通，大概是「跌扑打斗」之误，译文按跌打译。「阴症」是中医说的阴证（虚寒一类的病），不是阴性病。
>
> **Note:** For a Dark Tortoise Pressure changing into Output, the old commentary says a man surely suffers impotence and Wang Hongxu writes yin syndrome and yin deficiency; the two differ by one disease name and both mean a man's depletion in bedchamber matters. The four words 'die da qi fang' in the Bushi Zhengzong's White Tiger clause do not read and are probably a slip for 'falls, blows and fights'; the translation takes it as falls and blows. A 'yin syndrome' is a yin-type disorder in Chinese medicine (a cold, deficient kind of illness) and not an illness of a yin nature.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2307 行:螣蛇心惊，青龙则酒色过度;异文(相似 0.91) 第 2307 行:勾陈肿胀，朱雀则言语颠;异文(相似 0.91) 第 2307 行:虎损伤，女子则血崩血晕;同文 第 2307 行:玄武忧郁，男人则阴症阴虚
- 《增删卜易》:同文 第 13192 行:螣蛇心惊，青龙则酒色过度;同文 第 13192 行:勾陈肿胀，朱雀则言语颠狂;同文 第 13192 行:虎有损伤，女子则血崩血晕;同文 第 13192 行:玄武忧郁，男人则阴症阴虚
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1115 行:螣蛇心惊，青龙则酒色过度;同文 第 1115 行:勾陈肿胀，朱雀则言语颠狂;同文 第 1115 行:虎有损伤，女子则血崩血晕;同文 第 1115 行:玄武忧郁，男人则阴症阴虚

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.04

## LY.ILL.HJC.14 水化火火化水往来寒热：水旺火衰寒多热少，水伤火助则常热乍寒；坎宫火动内寒外热、离宫水动皮寒骨热，带日辰是疟疾

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里水爻动化火、火爻动化水，不论是不是鬼爻，只要冲犯世爻或主象的，都是忽冷忽热的症候；水、火两爻都动，也是这样。水旺火衰是冷多热少；水受伤、火得助，是常热而偶尔发冷。坎宫火动是里冷外热，离宫水动是皮冷骨热。再带上日辰，一定是疟疾。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the hexagram, a Water line moving and changing into Fire, or a Fire line changing into Water, whether or not it is the Pressure line, if it offends the Self line or the main image, is a syndrome of alternating chills and fever; if a Water line and a Fire line both move, it is the same. Water strong and Fire weak means more chill and less fever; Water harmed and Fire helped means constant heat with an occasional chill. Fire moving in the Kan palace is cold inside and hot outside; Water moving in the Li palace is cold skin and hot bones. If it also carries the Day, it is surely malaria.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6809 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6809*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水化火，火化水，往来寒热； | 水爻化火、火爻化水，是忽冷忽热； | Water changing into Fire, Fire changing into Water: alternating chills and fever; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6810–6812 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6810–6812*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦爻水动化火，火动化水； | 卦爻里水爻动化成火、火爻动化成水； | In the hexagram, a Water line moving and changing into Fire, or a Fire line moving and changing into Water; |
| 不拘鬼爻，但有干犯世爻或主象者，皆是寒热往来之症。 | 不论是不是鬼爻，只要冲犯了世爻或者主象的，都是寒热往来的症候。 | whether or not it is the Pressure line, so long as it offends the Self line or the main image, it is a syndrome of alternating chills and fever. |
| 卦有水火二爻俱动，亦然。 | 卦里水、火两爻都动，也是这样。 | If both a Water line and a Fire line move in the hexagram, it is the same. |
| 水旺火衰，寒多热少； | 水旺火衰，冷多热少； | Water strong and Fire weak: more chill and less fever; |
| 水受伤，火得助，则常热乍寒也。 | 水受伤、火得帮助，就是常常发热而偶尔发冷。 | Water harmed and Fire helped: constantly hot and suddenly cold. |
| 坎宫火动，内寒外热； | 坎宫的火爻动，是里冷外热； | Fire moving in the Kan palace: cold inside and hot outside; |
| 离宫水动，皮寒骨热。 | 离宫的水爻动，是皮冷骨热。 | Water moving in the Li palace: cold skin and hot bones. |
| 若带日辰，必是疟疾。 | 如果带着日辰，一定是疟疾。 | If it carries the Day, it is surely malaria. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2323 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2323*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水化火，火化水，不拘鬼爻，但有干犯主象者，皆是寒热往来之症，卦有水火二爻俱动亦然。 | 水化火、火化水，不论是不是鬼爻，只要冲犯了主象的，都是寒热往来的症候，卦里水、火两爻都动也是这样。 | Water changing into Fire or Fire into Water, whether or not it is the Pressure line, if it offends the main image, is a syndrome of alternating chills and fever, and so it is if both a Water line and a Fire line move in the hexagram. |
| 水旺火衰，寒多热少，倘水受伤，火得助，则常热乍寒也。 | 水旺火衰，冷多热少，如果水受伤、火得帮助，就是常常发热而偶尔发冷。 | Water strong and Fire weak means more chill and less fever; if Water is harmed and Fire helped, it is constantly hot and now and then cold. |
| 坎宫火动，内寒外热，离宫水动，皮寒骨热； | 坎宫火动是里冷外热，离宫水动是皮冷骨热； | Fire moving in the Kan palace is cold inside and hot outside, and Water moving in the Li palace is cold skin and hot bones; |
| 若带日辰，必是虐疾。 | 如果带着日辰，一定是疟疾。 | if it carries the Day, it is surely malaria. |

> **按**:卜筮正宗把「疟疾」写成「虐疾」，是同一个字的写法不同，译作疟疾（malaria）是按后世通行的说法；书里没有说明。
>
> **Note:** The Bushi Zhengzong writes 'ni ji' (cruel illness) for 'nue ji' (malaria); they are one word written two ways, and 'malaria' follows later usage, which the book does not explain.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2323 行:水化火，火化水，往来寒热
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1142 行:水化火，火化水，往来寒热

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.13、LY.ILL.HJC.15

## LY.ILL.HJC.15 上冲下下冲上，内外感伤：上下有鬼内外两感；内鬼动冲外鬼先伤后感，外鬼动冲内鬼先感后伤

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:上下卦都有鬼，病一定是里外两处感受。两个鬼都动、或者都静，是一起受病；两个鬼互相冲，刚感受了邪就受了伤。内卦的鬼动来冲外卦的鬼，是先受伤后感邪；外卦的鬼动来冲内卦的鬼，是先感邪后受伤。要分轻重，用刑、合来断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With Pressure in both the upper and lower trigrams, the illness is surely a double contagion of inside and outside. Both Pressure lines moving, or both still, means falling ill together; two Pressure lines clashing each other means hardly is the evil felt than harm is done. If the inner Pressure moves and clashes the outer, it is first harm and then contagion; if the outer Pressure moves and clashes the inner, first contagion and then harm. To tell heavy from light, judge by punishment and combination.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6814 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6814*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 上冲下，下冲上，内外感伤。 | 上冲下、下冲上，里外都感受伤害。 | Above clashing below, below clashing above: harm from both inside and outside. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6815 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6815*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 上下有鬼，病必内外两感。 | 上下都有鬼，病一定是内外两种感受。 | With Pressure above and below, the illness is surely a double contagion of inside and outside. |
| 俱动俱静，一同受病。 | 都动或者都静，是一起受病。 | Both moving or both still: they fall ill together. |
| 二鬼自相冲者，适感而遂伤也。 | 两个鬼互相冲的，是刚感受了邪就受了伤。 | Two Pressure lines clashing each other: hardly is the evil felt than harm is done. |
| 如内鬼动，冲外鬼，先伤后感； | 如果内卦的鬼动来冲外卦的鬼，是先受伤后感邪； | If the inner Pressure moves and clashes the outer, it is first harm and then contagion; |
| 外鬼动，冲内鬼，先感后伤。 | 外卦的鬼动来冲内卦的鬼，是先感邪后受伤。 | if the outer Pressure moves and clashes the inner, first contagion and then harm. |
| 要分轻重，以刑合断之，可也。 | 要分轻重，用刑、合来断，就可以了。 | To tell heavy from light, judge by punishment and combination, and that will do. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2325 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2325*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 上下有鬼，病必内外两感； | 上下有鬼，病一定是内外两处感受； | With Pressure above and below, the illness is surely a double contagion of inside and outside; |
| 俱动俱静者，一同受病； | 都动或者都静的，是一起受病； | both moving or both still, they fall ill together; |
| 二鬼自冲者，适感而适愈也。 | 两个鬼自己互相冲的，刚感受了邪就痊愈。 | two Pressure lines clashing each other of themselves: hardly is the evil felt than it is cured. |

> **打架**:两鬼互冲的结果，旧注和王洪绪相反。旧注说「适感而遂伤也」，刚感受了邪就受了伤；卜筮正宗写「适感而适愈也」，刚感受就好了。书里没有再解释，也没有别家的话可对。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The two disagree on what follows when two Pressure lines clash each other. The old commentary says 'hardly is the evil felt than harm is done', while the Bushi Zhengzong writes 'hardly is it felt than it is cured'. The book explains no further and there is no other voice to check against.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2325 行:上冲下，下冲上，内外感伤
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1145 行:上冲下，下冲上，内外感伤

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.11、LY.ILL.HJC.14

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.16 火鬼冲财上临则呕逆多吐：火鬼动克外卦财爻必呕吐，重则反胃不食；财带螣蛇吐虫，财在间爻消渴易饥

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:火的性子向上，财爻代表饮食。占病遇到火鬼动来克外卦的财爻，一定是呕吐，重的是反胃、吃不下饭。财爻带着螣蛇，吐出来的还带虫；财爻在间爻，是消渴、容易饿的症候。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Fire's nature is to flare upward and Wealth stands for food and drink. In divining illness, a Fire Pressure moving to control the outer Wealth line is surely vomiting, and when heavy, a stomach that turns and cannot take food. If Wealth carries the Coiling Snake, worms are brought up as well; if Wealth is on the between line, it is the syndrome of thirst and quick hunger.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6817 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6817*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火鬼冲财，上临则呕逆多吐； | 火鬼冲克财爻，在上卦的，就呕逆、常常呕吐； | A Fire Pressure clashing Wealth, above, brings retching and much vomiting; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6818 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6818*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火性炎上，财为饮食。 | 火的性子向上，财爻代表饮食。 | Fire's nature is to flare upward, and Wealth stands for food and drink. |
| 故占病，遇火鬼动克外财，必是呕吐，重则反胃不食。 | 所以占病，遇到火鬼动来克外卦的财爻，一定是呕吐，重的是反胃、不能进食。 | So in divining illness, a Fire Pressure moving to control the outer Wealth line is surely vomiting, and when heavy, a stomach that turns and takes no food. |
| 若财带螣蛇，吐上兼虫； | 如果财爻带着螣蛇，吐出来的还带着虫； | If Wealth carries the Coiling Snake, worms are brought up as well; |
| 财在间爻，则是消渴易饥之症。 | 财爻在间爻，就是消渴、容易饥饿的症候。 | if Wealth is on the between line, it is the syndrome of thirst and quick hunger. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2327 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2327*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火性炎上，财为饮食，故占病遇火鬼动克外财，必是呕吐，重则反胃不食。 | 火的性子向上，财爻代表饮食，所以占病遇到火鬼动来克外卦的财爻，一定是呕吐，重的是反胃、不能进食。 | Fire's nature is to flare upward and Wealth stands for food and drink, so in divining illness a Fire Pressure moving to control the outer Wealth line is surely vomiting, and when heavy, a stomach that turns and takes no food. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2327 行:火鬼冲财上临，则呕逆多吐
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1148 行:火鬼冲财，上临则呕逆多吐

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.17、LY.ILL.HJC.30

## LY.ILL.HJC.17 水官化土回头克、落在内卦是小便不通：属阴则大便也不通，阴阳错配二便俱闭；内卦水克加白虎，阳爻尿血、阴爻泻血，带刑是痔漏

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:水行的官鬼动了，变出土来回头克它，又落在本宫的内卦（下三爻）：小便不通；这一爻属阴，大便也不通；阳宫里是阴的象、阴宫里是阳的象，大小便都不通。内卦的水爻起克、又动、又加白虎：阳爻是尿血，阴爻是泻血（白虎是血神）；再带刑是痔漏。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Water Pressure line that moves and changes into Earth, which turns back to control it, and falls in the inner trigram of the home palace (the lower three lines): the urine is blocked; if the line is yin, the stool is blocked too; a yang palace with a yin image, or a yin palace with a yang image, blocks both. If the Water line in the inner trigram controls, moves and has the White Tiger added: a yang line means blood in the urine, a yin line bloody stool (the White Tiger is the spirit of blood); with punishment added it is piles and fistula.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6820 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6820*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水官化土，下值则小便不通。 | 水官化成土，落在下面（内卦），就是小便不通。 | A Water Pressure line changing into Earth, sitting below, means the urine is blocked. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6822–6823 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6822–6823*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水官化出回头土克，在本宫内卦，是小便不通。 | 水官变出土来回头克它，又在本宫的内卦，是小便不通。 | When a Water Pressure line changes out into Earth that turns back to control it, and it lies in the inner trigram of the home palace, the urine is blocked. |
| 属阴，是大便不通。 | 这一爻属阴，就是大便不通。 | If it is yin, the stool is blocked. |
| 阳宫阴象，阴宫阳象，二便俱不通。 | 阳宫里是阴的象，阴宫里是阳的象，大小便都不通。 | A yang palace with a yin image, or a yin palace with a yang image: neither urine nor stool passes. |
| 若内卦水克，动加白虎，阳爻是尿血，阴爻是泻血。 | 如果是内卦里的水爻起克，动了又加白虎，阳爻是尿血，阴爻是泻血。 | If the Water in the inner trigram is the one controlling, and it moves with the White Tiger added: a yang line means blood in the urine, a yin line means bloody stool. |
| 白虎血神，故也。 | 因为白虎是血神。 | This is because the White Tiger is the spirit of blood. |
| 带刑是痔漏。 | 再带着刑，就是痔漏。 | With punishment added it is piles and fistula. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2329 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2329*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水官化出回头克，在本宫初爻是小便不通，属阴是大便不通； | 水官变出回头克它，落在本宫的初爻，是小便不通，这一爻属阴，是大便不通； | A Water Pressure line changing out into a returning control, on the first line of the home palace: the urine is blocked; if it is yin, the stool is blocked; |
| 阳宫阴象，阴宫阳象，二便俱不通。 | 阳宫里是阴的象，阴宫里是阳的象，大小便都不通。 | a yang palace with a yin image, or a yin palace with a yang image: neither urine nor stool passes. |
| 若加白虎阳爻，是尿血，阴爻是泻血，白虎血神故也。 | 如果再加白虎，阳爻是尿血，阴爻是泻血，因为白虎是血神。 | If the White Tiger is added, a yang line means blood in the urine and a yin line bloody stool, because the White Tiger is the spirit of blood. |
| 带刑害是痔漏症。 | 带着刑、害，是痔漏。 | With punishment or harm added it is piles and fistula. |

> **打架**:水官化土在哪里才是小便不通：旧注说在「本宫内卦」（下三爻），王洪绪说在「本宫初爻」（最下面一爻）。范围一个宽一个窄，书里没有说哪个对。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Where a Water Pressure line changing into Earth means blocked urine: the old commentary says in the 'inner trigram of the home palace' (the lower three lines), Wang Hongxu says on the 'first line of the home palace' (the lowest line). One range is wide and one narrow, and the book does not say which is right.

> **按**:「属阴」书里没有说属什么阴；按上下文是这一爻是阴爻（下一句的「阳爻、阴爻」说的也是这一爻）。「阳宫阴象，阴宫阳象」是宫的阴阳和这一爻的阴阳相反。「痔漏」是痔疮和肛瘘。「回头土克」是水鬼动出来的土反过来克它自己。
>
> **Note:** The book does not say what 'belonging to yin' refers to; from the context it is that the line is a yin line (the 'yang line, yin line' of the next sentence also speaks of this line). 'A yang palace with a yin image' means that the yin or yang of the palace is the opposite of the yin or yang of this line. 'Piles and fistula' are haemorrhoids and anal fistula. 'Earth turning back to control' means the Earth that the Water Pressure line moves out into controls that line in return.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.91) 第 2329 行:水官化土下直，则小便不通
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1151 行:水官化土，下值则小便不通

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.04、LY.ILL.HJC.16、LY.ILL.HJC.30

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.18 鬼在兑宫病在口：金鬼化火或火化金鬼必患牙疔，不化火只是牙痛，金鬼逢冲牙必松动

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼爻在兑宫，病在口里。金鬼变出火、或者火爻变成金鬼，必生牙疔；没有变到火爻，只是牙痛。金鬼遇冲，牙一定松动。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line in the Dui palace puts the illness in the mouth. A Metal Pressure changing out into Fire, or a Fire line changing into a Metal Pressure, surely brings a tooth carbuncle; with no change into a Fire line it is only toothache. A Metal Pressure that meets a clash surely loosens the teeth.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6825 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6825*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若患牙疔，兑鬼金连火杀； | 如果患牙疔，是兑宫的鬼爻，金鬼连着火煞； | If one suffers a tooth carbuncle, it is a Pressure line in the Dui palace, a Metal Pressure joined to a Fire killer; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6826 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6826*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在兑宫，口中病。 | 鬼在兑宫，病在口里。 | Pressure in the Dui palace: the illness is in the mouth. |
| 若金鬼化火，或火化金鬼，必患牙疔。 | 如果金鬼变成火，或者火爻变成金鬼，一定生牙疔。 | If a Metal Pressure changes into Fire, or a Fire line changes into a Metal Pressure, one surely gets a tooth carbuncle. |
| 不化火爻，则是齿痛。 | 没有变到火爻，就只是牙痛。 | If it does not change into a Fire line, it is toothache. |
| 金鬼逢冲，齿必动摇。 | 金鬼遇上冲，牙一定松动。 | A Metal Pressure meeting a clash: the teeth surely loosen. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2331 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2331*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在兑宫，口中有病，若金鬼化忌神，或忌神化金鬼，必患牙疔，不化忌神则是齿痛。 | 鬼在兑宫，口里有病；如果金鬼变成忌神，或者忌神变成金鬼，一定生牙疔；没有变到忌神，就是牙痛。 | Pressure in the Dui palace: there is illness in the mouth; if a Metal Pressure changes into the Adversary, or the Adversary changes into a Metal Pressure, one surely gets a tooth carbuncle; if it does not change into the Adversary, it is toothache. |
| 静鬼逢冲齿必动摇。 | 静着的鬼遇上冲，牙一定松动。 | A Pressure line that is still and meets a clash: the teeth surely loosen. |

> **按**:旧注说「火」，王洪绪改说「忌神」：金的忌神是克它的火，说的是同一种变法。旧注的「金鬼逢冲」，王洪绪写成「静鬼逢冲」，限定在不动的鬼爻上。「牙疔」是牙龈上长的疔疮，小而硬、根深、来得急。经文的「火杀」，旧注解成「火」，即火爻。
>
> **Note:** Where the old commentary says 'Fire', Wang Hongxu says 'the Adversary': the Adversary of Metal is the Fire that controls it, so both speak of the same change. The old commentary's 'a Metal Pressure meeting a clash' becomes 'a still Pressure meeting a clash' in Wang Hongxu, which limits it to a Pressure line that does not move. A 'tooth carbuncle' is a boil on the gum, small, hard, deep-rooted and quick to come. The 'Fire killer' of the verse is explained by the old commentary as Fire, that is, a Fire line.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.9) 第 2331 行:若患牙疔，兑鬼金连火
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1153 行:若患牙疔，兑鬼金连火杀

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.04、LY.ILL.HJC.19

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.19 鬼在震宫病在脚：加勾陈足肿、加白虎折伤，土鬼化木是脚气，木鬼酸麻、水鬼湿气、火鬼疮毒、金鬼骨节痛或刀伤

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼在震宫，病在脚上：加勾陈，脚一定肿；加白虎，一定摔断打伤、破损。土鬼变成木，得脚气；木鬼是酸痛麻木，水鬼是湿气，火鬼主疮毒，金鬼是踝、膝疼痛和骨头痛，或者被刀刃所伤。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line in the Zhen palace puts the illness in the foot: with Hook Earth added the foot surely swells, with the White Tiger it is surely broken, wounded or torn. An Earth Pressure changing into Wood is beriberi; a Wood Pressure is aching, soreness and numbness, a Water Pressure is damp, a Fire Pressure means sores and toxin, and a Metal Pressure is pain in the ankle joints, the knees and the bones, or a wound from a blade.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6828 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6828*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如生脚气，震宫土化木星。 | 如果生了脚气，是震宫的土鬼变成木。 | If beriberi arises, it is an Earth Pressure in the Zhen palace changing into Wood. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6829 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6829*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在震宫，病在足。 | 鬼在震宫，病在脚上。 | Pressure in the Zhen palace: the illness is in the foot. |
| 加勾陈，足必肿； | 加勾陈，脚一定肿； | With Hook Earth added, the foot surely swells; |
| 加白虎，必折伤破损。 | 加白虎，一定是摔断、打伤、破损。 | with the White Tiger added, it is surely broken, wounded or torn. |
| 土鬼化木，则患脚气。 | 土鬼变成木，就得脚气。 | An Earth Pressure changing into Wood is beriberi. |
| 木鬼，酸疼麻木； | 木鬼是酸痛、麻木； | A Wood Pressure is aching, soreness and numbness; |
| 水鬼，必是湿气； | 水鬼一定是湿气； | a Water Pressure is surely damp; |
| 火鬼，必主疮毒； | 火鬼主要是疮毒； | a Fire Pressure mainly means sores and toxin; |
| 金鬼，是脚骱膝疼骨痛，或刀刃所伤。 | 金鬼是踝关节、膝盖疼和骨头痛，或者被刀刃所伤。 | a Metal Pressure is pain in the ankle joints and knees and pain in the bones, or a wound from a blade. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2333 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2333*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在震宫，病在足，加勾陈足必肿，加白虎必折伤破损。 | 鬼在震宫，病在脚上，加勾陈脚一定肿，加白虎一定摔断打伤、破损。 | Pressure in the Zhen palace: the illness is in the foot; with Hook Earth added the foot surely swells, with the White Tiger it is surely broken, wounded or torn. |
| 土鬼化木则患脚气，木鬼酸痛麻木，水鬼是湿气，火鬼必生疮毒，金鬼是脚骱膝痛、骨痛或刀刃所伤类。 | 土鬼变木得脚气，木鬼是酸痛麻木，水鬼是湿气，火鬼一定生疮毒，金鬼是踝、膝疼痛，骨痛，或刀刃所伤一类。 | An Earth Pressure changing into Wood is beriberi, a Wood Pressure is aching, soreness and numbness, a Water Pressure is damp, a Fire Pressure surely brings sores and toxin, and a Metal Pressure is pain in the ankle joints and knees, bone pain, or wounds of the blade kind. |

> **按**:这里的「脚气」是旧医书说的脚气病，多指腿脚又肿又麻、软弱无力，不是今天说的脚癣。「脚骱」是踝关节（骱是骨节相接的地方）。震宫主脚，和 LY.ILL.HJC.04 里王洪绪的写法一致。
>
> **Note:** The 'jiaoqi' here is the old medical disease of that name, which mostly means legs and feet that are swollen and numb, weak and without strength, not what is now called athlete's foot. 'Jiaojie' is the ankle joint (a 'jie' is the place where bones meet). The Zhen palace governing the foot agrees with the way Wang Hongxu writes it in LY.ILL.HJC.04.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2333 行:如生脚气，震宫土化木星
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1156 行:如生脚气，震宫土化木星

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.04、LY.ILL.HJC.20

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.20 离宫鬼化水是痰火、乾宫鬼化木是头风；震鬼遇螣蛇发动是惊悸颠狂、逢冲有逾墙上屋之患；艮鬼逢火鬼是痈疽，变出土鬼是浮肿蛊胀

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:离宫的鬼变成水（或水爻动变成鬼）是痰火的症候；乾宫的鬼变成木（或木爻动变成鬼）是头风、眩晕。震宫的鬼在外卦，不要按脚上的病断，可以说他坐卧不安、心神恍惚（震是主动的卦）；再加螣蛇发动，是癫狂、惊痫，小孩是惊风；遇冲有翻墙上屋的乱动之患。艮宫的鬼遇上火鬼，一定生痈疽；变出土鬼，可以说浮肿、蛊胀一类。其余照此类推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line of the Li palace changing into Water (or a moving Water line changing into Pressure) is the syndrome of phlegm-fire; a Pressure line of the Qian palace changing into Wood (or a moving Wood line changing into Pressure) is headwind and dizziness. A Zhen Pressure line in the outer trigram is not judged as a foot illness: say the patient cannot sit or lie at ease and his mind wanders (Zhen governs movement); with the Coiling Snake added and moving it is mania and fright-epilepsy, in a child convulsions; meeting a clash there is the danger of climbing over walls and onto roofs. A Gen Pressure line meeting a Fire Pressure surely brings carbuncles; if an Earth Pressure changes out, one can speak of swelling and drum-like distension. The rest follows by analogy.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6830 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6830*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在离宫化水，痰火何疑； | 鬼爻在离宫、变成水，是痰火，不用怀疑； | A Pressure line in the Li palace changing into Water: phlegm-fire, no doubt of it; |
| 官来乾象变木，头风有准。 | 官鬼在乾卦的象里变成木，头风是准的。 | a Pressure line in the Qian image changing into Wood: headwind is certain. |
| 震遇螣蛇仍发动，惊悸颠狂； | 震宫遇上螣蛇、又发动，是惊悸和癫狂； | Zhen meeting the Coiling Snake and still moving: fright, palpitations and mania; |
| 艮逢巳午又交重，痈疽疮毒。 | 艮宫遇上巳、午又发动，是痈疽和疮毒。 | Gen meeting Si and Wu and moving: carbuncles, deep abscesses, sores and toxin. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6831 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6831*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 离宫鬼化水爻，痰火症候； | 离宫的鬼变成水爻，是痰火的症候； | A Pressure line of the Li palace changing into a Water line: the syndrome of phlegm-fire; |
| 水动化鬼，亦然。 | 水爻动而变成鬼，也是这样。 | a moving Water line changing into Pressure is the same. |
| 乾宫鬼化木爻，头风眩晕； | 乾宫的鬼变成木爻，是头风、眩晕； | A Pressure line of the Qian palace changing into a Wood line: headwind and dizziness; |
| 木动变鬼，亦然。 | 木爻动而变成鬼，也是这样。 | a moving Wood line changing into Pressure is the same. |
| 震在外卦，勿依此断，可言其病坐卧不安，心神恍惚。 | 震在外卦，就不要照这个断，可以说他坐卧不安，心神恍惚。 | If Zhen is in the outer trigram, do not judge by this; one can say the patient cannot sit or lie at ease and his mind wanders. |
| 盖震主动，故也。 | 因为震是主动的卦。 | This is because Zhen governs movement. |
| 更加螣蛇发动，必是颠狂惊痫之症，小儿乃惊风也。 | 再加螣蛇发动，一定是癫狂、惊痫的症候，小孩子就是惊风。 | With the Coiling Snake added and moving, it is surely the syndrome of mania and fright-epilepsy, and in a child, convulsions. |
| 逢冲，则有踰墙上屋之患。 | 遇上冲，就有翻墙上房的乱动之患。 | Meeting a clash, there is the danger of climbing over walls and onto roofs. |
| 艮象为山，山高故也。 | 艮的象是山，因为山高。 | The image of Gen is the mountain, because a mountain is high. |
| 更逢火鬼，必患痈疽。 | 再遇上火鬼，一定生痈疽。 | Meeting a Fire Pressure as well, one surely gets carbuncles. |
| 若遇变出土鬼，可言浮肿蛊胀等症。 | 如果变出土鬼，可以说是浮肿、蛊胀一类的症候。 | If an Earth Pressure changes out, one can speak of the syndromes of swelling and drum-like distension. |
| 余可类推。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest can be reasoned by analogy. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2335 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2335*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 离宫鬼化水爻，痰火症候，水动化鬼亦然。 | 离宫的鬼变成水爻是痰火的症候，水爻动变成鬼也是这样。 | A Pressure line of the Li palace changing into a Water line: the syndrome of phlegm-fire; a moving Water line changing into Pressure is the same. |
| 乾宫鬼化木爻，头风眩晕，木动变鬼亦然。 | 乾宫的鬼变成木爻是头风、眩晕，木爻动变成鬼也是这样。 | A Pressure line of the Qian palace changing into a Wood line: headwind and dizziness; a moving Wood line changing into Pressure is the same. |
| 震在外卦勿以脚断，可言其病坐卧不宁，心神恍惚，盖震主动故也。 | 震在外卦，不要当脚上的病来断，可以说他坐卧不宁，心神恍惚，因为震是主动的卦。 | If Zhen is in the outer trigram, do not judge it as a foot illness; one can say the patient cannot sit or lie at rest and his mind wanders, because Zhen governs movement. |
| 更加螣蛇发动，必是颠狂惊痫之病，小儿乃惊风也。 | 再加螣蛇发动，一定是癫狂、惊痫的病，小孩子就是惊风。 | With the Coiling Snake added and moving, it is surely the illness of mania and fright-epilepsy, and in a child, convulsions. |
| 逢冲则有逾墙上屋之患。 | 遇上冲，就有翻墙上房的乱动之患。 | Meeting a clash, there is the danger of climbing over walls and onto roofs. |
| 艮逢火鬼必生痈疽，若遇变出土鬼，可言浮肿蛊胀等症。 | 艮宫遇上火鬼一定生痈疽，如果变出土鬼，可以说是浮肿、蛊胀一类的症候。 | A Gen palace meeting a Fire Pressure surely brings carbuncles; if an Earth Pressure changes out, one can speak of the syndromes of swelling and drum-like distension. |
| 余可类推。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest can be reasoned by analogy. |

> **按**:「痰火」是痰和火郁在一起的病，「头风」是反复发作的头痛，「蛊胀」是肚腹胀大如鼓的病，「踰墙上屋」是发狂时翻墙爬房的举动。旧注「震在外卦，勿依此断」的「此」没有写明，王洪绪写成「勿以脚断」，译文按他的写法理解。旧注「艮象为山，山高故也」放在「逢冲」和「更逢火鬼」之间，没有说它解释的是哪一句（前面的翻墙上屋，还是后面的痈疽）；王洪绪把这句删了。经文的「巳午」是火的两个地支，「交重」就是发动。
>
> **Note:** 'Phlegm-fire' is an illness in which phlegm and fire are bound together; 'headwind' is a headache that comes back again and again; 'gu distension' is a belly swollen like a drum; 'climbing over walls and onto roofs' is what a patient in a fit of madness does. The 'this' of the old commentary's 'if Zhen is in the outer trigram, do not judge by this' is not spelled out, and Wang Hongxu writes 'do not judge it as a foot illness'; the translation follows his wording. The old commentary puts 'the image of Gen is the mountain, because a mountain is high' between 'meeting a clash' and 'meeting a Fire Pressure as well' and does not say which of the two it explains (the climbing of walls and roofs before it, or the carbuncles after it); Wang Hongxu drops the sentence. Si and Wu in the verse are the two Fire branches, and 'jiaozhong' simply means moving.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2335 行:鬼在离宫化水，痰火何疑;同文 第 2335 行:官来乾象变木，头风有准;同文 第 2335 行:震遇螣蛇仍发动，惊悸颠狂;异文(相似 0.91) 第 2335 行:艮逢巳午又交重，痛疽疮毒
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1159 行:鬼在离宫化水，痰火何疑;同文 第 1159 行:官来乾象变木，头风有准;同文 第 1159 行:震遇螣蛇仍发动，惊悸颠狂;同文 第 1159 行:艮逢巳午又交重，痈疽疮毒

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.04、LY.ILL.HJC.19

## LY.ILL.HJC.21 卦内无财则饮食不纳：财主饮食，落空吃不下，不上卦不想吃；财动化官鬼病因酒食而得，财临身世或化财则病重也能食

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:财爻管饮食。卦里没有财爻，吃不下东西：财爻落空，吃不下；不上卦，不想吃。财爻动而变成官鬼，这病一定是吃喝来的；财爻临在身爻、世爻上，或者变出财爻，病再重也还能吃。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Wealth line governs food and drink. With no Wealth in the hexagram, food and drink are not taken: if the Wealth line falls into Void, nothing goes down; if it is absent from the hexagram, he has no wish to eat. A Wealth line that moves and changes into Pressure means the illness came from drink and food; a Wealth line sitting on the Body or the Self line, or a change into a Wealth line, means that even in a heavy illness he can still eat.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6833 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6833*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦内无财，饮食不纳； | 卦里没有财爻，吃不下东西； | With no Wealth line in the hexagram, food and drink are not taken; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6834 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6834*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财主饮食。 | 财爻管饮食。 | Wealth governs food and drink. |
| 动化官鬼，其病必因酒饮食而得。 | 财爻动而变成官鬼，这病一定是因为喝酒、吃东西得的。 | If it moves and changes into Pressure, the illness was surely got through wine, drink and food. |
| 若遇空亡，饮食不纳。 | 如果遇上空亡，吃不下东西。 | If it meets Void, food and drink are not taken. |
| 若不上卦，不思饮食。 | 如果不上卦，不想吃东西。 | If it is absent from the hexagram, he has no thought of food or drink. |
| 财临身世，或化财爻，虽病重亦能食。 | 财爻临在身爻、世爻上，或者变出财爻，病再重也能吃。 | If Wealth sits on the Body or the Self line, or a Wealth line changes out, then even in a heavy illness he can eat. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2337 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2337*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财主饮食，若遇空亡，饮食不纳，若不上卦，不思饮食。 | 财爻管饮食，遇上空亡吃不下，不上卦就不想吃。 | Wealth governs food and drink; if it meets Void, food and drink are not taken, and if it is absent from the hexagram, he has no thought of food or drink. |

> **按**:经文的「卦内无财」，旧注分成两种：有财爻但落空（吃不下），和根本不上卦（不想吃）。「身世」是身爻和世爻。旧注的「动化官鬼」和「财临身世」两句，王洪绪没有收。
>
> **Note:** The verse's 'no Wealth in the hexagram' is split by the old commentary into two cases: a Wealth line that is there but Void (food is not taken) and one that is not in the hexagram at all (no wish to eat). 'Shen shi' is the Body line and the Self line. Wang Hongxu does not take the old commentary's two sentences on 'moving and changing into Pressure' and 'Wealth on the Body or Self line'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2337 行:卦内无财，饮食不纳
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1162 行:卦内无财，饮食不纳

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.16、LY.ILL.HJC.22、LY.ILL.HJC.30

## LY.ILL.HJC.22 世应之间是病人胸膈：鬼临其上则痞塞不通，金鬼胸肋骨痛、土鬼饱闷、木鬼心痒嘈杂、水鬼痰饮填塞、火鬼心痛；化财或财化鬼是宿食未消

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻和应爻中间的位置，是病人胸膈的地方。官鬼临在这里，胸膈一定堵闷不通：金鬼是胸肋骨头痛，土鬼是胀饱憋闷、不舒畅，木鬼是心里发痒、胃里嘈杂，水鬼是痰饮堵塞，火鬼多半是心口痛。如果变出财爻，或财爻变成鬼，一定是隔夜的食物没消化，弄得胸膈不舒服。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The place between the Self and Other lines is where the patient's chest and diaphragm are. With a Pressure line on it the chest is surely stuffed and blocked: a Metal Pressure is pain in the ribs and chest bones, an Earth Pressure a full, oppressive feeling with no relief, a Wood Pressure an itching heart and a gnawing stomach, a Water Pressure phlegm and fluid choking it up, and a Fire Pressure mostly heart pain. If it changes into a Wealth line, or a Wealth line changes into Pressure, it is surely food from before left undigested, so that the chest and diaphragm are not at ease.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6836 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6836*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 间中有鬼，胸膈不宽。 | 间爻上有鬼，胸膈不舒畅。 | With Pressure in the between lines, the chest and diaphragm are not at ease. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6837 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6837*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世应中间，即病人胸膈处也。 | 世爻和应爻中间的位置，就是病人胸膈的地方。 | The place between the Self and Other lines is where the patient's chest and diaphragm are. |
| 官鬼临之，必然痞塞不通。 | 官鬼临在这里，胸膈一定堵闷不通。 | With a Pressure line on it, the chest is surely stuffed and blocked. |
| 金鬼胸肋骨痛，土鬼饱闷不宽，木鬼心痒嘈杂，水鬼痰饮填塞，火鬼多是心痛。 | 金鬼是胸肋骨头痛，土鬼是胀饱憋闷、不舒畅，木鬼是心里发痒、胃里嘈杂，水鬼是痰饮堵塞，火鬼多半是心口痛。 | A Metal Pressure is pain in the ribs and chest bones, an Earth Pressure a full, oppressive feeling with no relief, a Wood Pressure an itching heart and a gnawing stomach, a Water Pressure phlegm and fluid choking it up, and a Fire Pressure mostly heart pain. |
| 若化财爻，或财爻化鬼，必是宿食未消，以致胸膈不利。 | 如果变出财爻，或财爻变成鬼，一定是隔夜的食物没消化，弄得胸膈不舒服。 | If it changes into a Wealth line, or a Wealth line changes into Pressure, it is surely food from before left undigested, so that the chest and diaphragm are not at ease. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2339 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2339*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世应中间即病入胸膈处也，官鬼临之，必然痞塞不通。 | 世爻和应爻中间，就是病人胸膈的地方，官鬼临在这里，胸膈一定堵闷不通。 | The place between the Self and Other lines is where the patient's chest and diaphragm are; with a Pressure line on it, the chest is surely stuffed and blocked. |
| 金鬼胸隔骨痛，土官饱闷不宽，木鬼心痒嘈杂，水鬼痰饮填塞，火鬼多是心痛。 | 金鬼是胸膈骨头痛，土鬼是胀饱憋闷、不舒畅，木鬼是心里发痒、胃里嘈杂，水鬼是痰饮堵塞，火鬼多半是心口痛。 | A Metal Pressure is pain in the chest bones, an Earth Pressure a full, oppressive feeling with no relief, a Wood Pressure an itching heart and a gnawing stomach, a Water Pressure phlegm and fluid choking it up, and a Fire Pressure mostly heart pain. |
| 若化财爻或财爻化鬼，必是宿食未消，以致胸膈不利。 | 如果变出财爻，或财爻变成鬼，一定是隔夜的食物没消化，弄得胸膈不舒服。 | If it changes into a Wealth line, or a Wealth line changes into Pressure, it is surely food from before left undigested, so that the chest and diaphragm are not at ease. |

> **按**:经文的「间」是世爻和应爻之间的两爻；旧注拿它当病人胸膈的位置。王洪绪这一句写成「病入胸膈处」，大概是「病人胸膈处」之误，译文按病人。旧注写「胸肋骨痛」，王洪绪写「胸隔骨痛」；旧注「土鬼」，王洪绪写作「土官」，是同一个意思。「嘈杂」是胃里烧灼、空空乱乱的不适，「痰饮」是体内水液积聚成痰、成饮。
>
> **Note:** The 'between' of the verse means the two lines between the Self and Other lines, and the old commentary takes them as the place of the patient's chest and diaphragm. Wang Hongxu writes 'where the illness enters the chest' here, which is probably a slip for 'where the patient's chest is', and the translation follows 'patient'. The old commentary writes 'pain in the ribs and chest bones' and Wang Hongxu 'pain in the chest bones', and where the old commentary has 'Earth Pressure' Wang Hongxu has 'Earth official'; both mean the same. 'Gnawing' (caoza) is a burning, empty, disordered discomfort in the stomach, and 'phlegm and fluid' (tanyin) is water in the body collecting as phlegm and as fluid.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2339 行:间中有鬼胸膈不宽
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.88) 第 1165 行:间中有鬼，胸隔不宽

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.21、LY.ILL.HJC.29

## LY.ILL.HJC.24 世爻衰而入墓则神志昏沉：旺则懒动、衰则不言不语，怕明喜暗、不思食、爱眠懒起；再坐阴宫是阴症，用爻入墓同断

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻衰弱又落进墓里，病人昏沉困倦。世爻有气（旺相），不过是懒得动；衰弱就不说话：怕亮喜暗，不想吃东西，爱睡，怕起床，懒得睁眼。再坐在阴宫上，一定是阴症。用爻入墓也这样断；鬼爻持世入墓、鬼的墓临在用爻上，都照这个断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A weak Self line falling into the tomb makes the patient drowsy and clouded. If the Self line is strong and has force, he is only too listless to move; if it is weak, he does not speak: he shrinks from the light and likes the dark, has no wish to eat, loves to sleep, dreads getting up and is too listless even to open his eyes. If it also sits in a yin palace it is surely a yin syndrome. A Subject line entering the tomb is judged the same way; so are a Pressure line on the Self line entering the tomb and the tomb of Pressure falling on the Subject line.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6842 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6842*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世衰入墓，神思困而不清。 | 世爻衰弱、落进墓里，神志困倦不清醒。 | A weak Self line entering the tomb: the mind is drowsy and unclear. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6843–6844 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6843–6844*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世爻入墓，病必昏沉。 | 世爻入了墓，病人一定昏沉。 | The Self line entering the tomb: the patient is surely clouded and heavy. |
| 旺相有气，无非懒于行动； | 世爻旺相有气，不过是懒得动； | If it is strong and has force, he is no more than too listless to move; |
| 衰则不言不语。 | 衰弱就不说话。 | if weak, he does not speak. |
| 是怕明喜暗，不思食； | 他怕亮、喜欢暗，不想吃东西； | He shrinks from the light and likes the dark, and has no thought of food; |
| 爱眠，怕起，懒开目。 | 爱睡，怕起床，懒得睁眼。 | he loves to sleep, dreads getting up, and is too listless to open his eyes. |
| 更坐阴宫，必是阴症。 | 再坐在阴宫上，一定是阴症。 | If it also sits in a yin palace, it is surely a yin syndrome. |
| 用爻入墓亦然。 | 用爻入墓也是这样。 | A Subject line entering the tomb is the same. |
| 世鬼入墓，鬼墓临用，皆依此断。 | 鬼爻持世入墓、鬼的墓库临在用爻上，都照这个断。 | Pressure on the Self line entering the tomb, and the tomb of Pressure falling on the Subject line, are all judged by this. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2343 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2343*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世爻入墓，病必昏沉。 | 世爻入了墓，病人一定昏沉。 | The Self line entering the tomb: the patient is surely clouded and heavy. |
| 旺相有气，则懒于行动，衰则不言不语，是怕明喜暗，不思饮食，爱眠怕起，懒开目，更坐阴宫必是阴症。 | 旺相有气就懒得动，衰弱就不说话，怕亮喜暗，不想吃东西，爱睡怕起床，懒得睁眼，再坐在阴宫上一定是阴症。 | If strong and with force he is too listless to move, if weak he does not speak; he shrinks from the light and likes the dark, has no wish to eat, loves to sleep and dreads getting up, is too listless to open his eyes, and if it also sits in a yin palace it is surely a yin syndrome. |
| 用爻入墓、鬼墓临用、原神入墓，皆依此法断。 | 用爻入墓、鬼的墓库临在用爻上、原神入墓，都照这个办法断。 | A Subject line entering the tomb, the tomb of Pressure falling on the Subject line, and the Support entering the tomb are all judged by this method. |

> **按**:两家末句列的情形不完全一样：旧注是「用爻入墓」「世鬼入墓」「鬼墓临用」，王洪绪是「用爻入墓」「鬼墓临用」「原神入墓」。旧注的「世鬼入墓」，按上下文读作鬼爻持世、又入墓。「阴症」是虚寒、精神萎靡一类的症候。
>
> **Note:** The two ends differ a little in the cases they list: the old commentary gives 'a Subject line entering the tomb', 'a Pressure line on the Self line entering the tomb' and 'the tomb of Pressure falling on the Subject line', Wang Hongxu 'a Subject line entering the tomb', 'the tomb of Pressure falling on the Subject line' and 'the Support entering the tomb'. The old commentary's 'shi gui ru mu' is read from the context as a Pressure line holding the Self line and entering the tomb. A 'yin syndrome' is one of cold and deficiency, with the spirits low.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2343 行:世衰入墓，神思困而不清
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1172 行:世衰入墓，神思困而不清

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.23、LY.ILL.HJC.26

## LY.ILL.HJC.27 用爻被哪一行克就伤哪一处：金克木肢体酸痛、木克土皮骨伤残、火为仇喘咳、水来害恍惚；卦中无鬼，看谁刑冲克害用爻

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里没有鬼爻，就看谁来刑、冲、克、害用爻来断。用爻被金伤，四肢一定酸痛；用爻遭木克，皮和骨一定伤残；火是仇敌，主喘咳的灾病；水来相害，主恍惚的症候。动的金克过来，木爻受伤，四肢关节酸痛；动的木克过来，土爻受制，皮肉伤损。其余照此类推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With no Pressure line in the hexagram, judge by whatever punishes, clashes, controls or harms the Subject line. A Subject line wounded by Metal: the limbs surely ache and are sore; controlled by Wood: skin and bones are surely injured and maimed; with Fire as the enemy, the trouble of panting and cough; with Water coming to harm, a dazed, wandering state. So when a moving Metal line controls, the Wood line is wounded and the limbs and joints ache; when a moving Wood line controls, the Earth line is held down and flesh and skin are injured. The rest follows by analogy.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6851 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6851*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用受金伤，肢体必然酸痛； | 用爻被金所伤，四肢一定酸痛； | The Subject line wounded by Metal: the limbs surely ache and are sore; |
| 主遭木克，皮骨定见伤残。 | 用爻遭木克，皮和骨一定伤残。 | the Subject line controlled by Wood: skin and bones are surely injured and maimed. |
| 火为仇，则喘咳之灾； | 火是仇敌，就是喘咳的灾病； | with Fire as the enemy, it is the trouble of panting and cough; |
| 水来害，则恍惚之症。 | 水来相害，就是恍惚的症候。 | with Water coming to harm, it is the syndrome of a dazed, wandering mind. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6852 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6852*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中无鬼，以刑冲克害用爻者断。 | 卦里没有鬼爻，就拿刑、冲、克、害用爻的那一爻来断。 | If there is no Pressure line in the hexagram, judge by whatever line punishes, clashes, controls or harms the Subject line. |
| 如金动来克，则木爻受伤，肢节酸痛； | 比如金爻动了来克，木爻就受伤，四肢关节酸痛； | For example, if a Metal line moves and controls, the Wood line is wounded and the limbs and joints ache; |
| 木动来克，则土爻受制，皮肉伤损。 | 木爻动了来克，土爻就受制，皮肉受伤损坏。 | if a Wood line moves and controls, the Earth line is held down and flesh and skin are injured. |
| 余可类推。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest can be reasoned by analogy. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2350 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2350*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如金动来克，则木爻受伤，支节酸痛； | 比如金爻动了来克，木爻就受伤，四肢关节酸痛； | For example, if a Metal line moves and controls, the Wood line is wounded and the limbs and joints ache; |
| 木动来克，则土爻受制，皮骨伤损。 | 木爻动了来克，土爻就受制，皮骨受伤损坏。 | if a Wood line moves and controls, the Earth line is held down and skin and bones are injured. |
| 余可类推。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest can be reasoned by analogy. |

> **按**:经文只说「用受金伤」「主遭木克」「火为仇」「水来害」，没有写用爻本身是什么五行；旧注只解释了前两句（金克的是木爻，木克的是土爻）。「火为仇」「水来害」对应的是用爻为金被火克、用爻为火被水克，这是按五行相克推的，书里没有写出来。旧注说「皮肉伤损」，王洪绪写「皮骨伤损」；旧注「肢节」，王洪绪写「支节」。
>
> **Note:** The verse only says 'the Subject wounded by Metal', 'the Subject controlled by Wood', 'Fire as the enemy' and 'Water coming to harm', and does not say which element the Subject line itself is; the old commentary explains only the first two (what Metal controls is the Wood line, what Wood controls is the Earth line). That 'Fire as the enemy' and 'Water coming to harm' correspond to a Metal Subject controlled by Fire and a Fire Subject controlled by Water is reasoned from the controlling cycle of the five phases and is not written in the book. The old commentary says 'flesh and skin are injured' and Wang Hongxu 'skin and bones are injured'; the old commentary writes 'limb joints' with one character and Wang Hongxu with another.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2349 行:用受金伤，肢体必然酸痛;异文(相似 0.9) 第 2349 行:主遭木克，皮骨定遭伤残;同文 第 2349 行:火为仇则喘咳之灾;同文 第 2349 行:水来害则恍惚之症
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1181 行:用受金伤，肢体必然酸痛;同文 第 1181 行:主遭木克，皮骨定见伤残;同文 第 1181 行:火为仇，则喘咳之灾;同文 第 1181 行:水来害，则恍惚之症

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.29

## LY.ILL.HJC.28 第三爻主腰：无故自空是腰软，旺相而空是腰痛，不空而遇动爻日辰官鬼冲克是闪腰，鬼在此爻也主腰痛

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:第三爻管腰。第三爻无缘无故落空，是腰软；有缘故而空，或者旺相而空，是腰痛；不空，但遇到动爻、日辰、官鬼来克或冲它，是闪了腰的痛；动而空的也是这样。鬼爻就在第三爻上，也主腰痛。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The third line is the waist. If the third line is Void of itself without cause, the waist is weak; if it is Void with a cause, or Void while strong, the waist hurts; if it is not Void but a moving line, the Day or a Pressure line controls or clashes it, it is a strained waist; a moving Void line is the same. A Pressure line on the third line also means waist pain.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6854 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6854*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 空及第三，此病须知腰软； | 空亡落到第三爻，这个病要知道是腰软； | When Void reaches the third line, know that this illness is a weak waist; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6855 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6855*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第三爻无故自空，为腰软； | 第三爻无缘无故自己落空，是腰软； | If the third line is Void of itself without cause, the waist is weak; |
| 有故而空，或旺相而空，为腰痛； | 有缘故而空，或者旺相而空，是腰痛； | if it is Void with a cause, or Void while strong, the waist hurts; |
| 不空，而遇动爻日辰官鬼克冲者，乃闪腰痛也； | 不空，但遇到动爻、日辰、官鬼来克或冲它的，是闪了腰的痛； | if it is not Void but meets a moving line, the Day or a Pressure line that controls or clashes it, it is a strained waist; |
| 动空亦然。 | 动而空的也是这样。 | a moving Void line is the same. |
| 鬼在此爻者，亦主腰痛。 | 鬼爻就在这一爻上的，也主腰痛。 | A Pressure line on this line also means waist pain. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2352 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2352*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第三爻如值旬空，为腰软； | 第三爻如果碰上旬空，是腰软； | If the third line meets Void, the waist is weak; |
| 或旺相而空，为腰痛。 | 或者旺相而空，是腰痛。 | or if it is Void while strong, the waist hurts. |
| 不空而遇动爻日辰官鬼冲克者，乃闪腰痛也，动爻亦然。 | 不空，但遇到动爻、日辰、官鬼来冲克的，是闪了腰的痛，动爻也是这样。 | If it is not Void but meets a moving line, the Day or a Pressure line that clashes or controls it, it is a strained waist, and a moving line is the same. |
| 鬼在此爻者，亦主腰痛。 | 鬼爻就在这一爻上的，也主腰痛。 | A Pressure line on this line also means waist pain. |

> **按**:旧注的「无故自空」「有故而空」书里没有说「故」指什么。王洪绪把前者写成「值旬空」，后者只留「旺相而空」。旧注末尾的「动空亦然」，王洪绪写成「动爻亦然」，一个说动而空，一个说动爻本身，大概是传抄中「空」「爻」两个字的差别。
>
> **Note:** The book does not say what the 'cause' of the old commentary's 'Void without cause' and 'Void with cause' is. Wang Hongxu writes the former as 'meets Void' and keeps only 'Void while strong' of the latter. The old commentary's closing 'a moving Void line is the same' is written by Wang Hongxu as 'a moving line is the same': one means moving and Void, the other the moving line itself, probably a difference of the two characters for 'Void' and 'line' in copying.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2352 行:空及第三，此病须知腰软
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1185 行:空及第三，此病须知腰软

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.29

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.29 官鬼克到哪一处，那一处也有病：克上六头痛，克间爻或临间爻胸膈不利；被克之处和鬼所临之处都要看

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:病不只在鬼爻所坐的地方，被鬼克到的地方也有病痛：克到最上面一爻（上六），主头痛；克到间爻，或者鬼坐在间爻上，主胸膈不舒服；忌神也一样。其余照此类推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The illness is not only where the Pressure line sits; the place it wounds also has pain. Controlling the top line brings headache; controlling a between line, or sitting on one, makes the chest and diaphragm uneasy; the Adversary does the same. The rest follows by analogy.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6857 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6857*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官伤上六，斯人当主头疼。 | 官鬼伤到上六爻，这个人会头痛。 | A Pressure line wounding the top line: this person will have a headache. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6858 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6858*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 上六。 | 指最上面的第六爻。 | The top, sixth line. |
| 大抵不惟鬼爻临处受病，被伤之处亦有病痛。 | 大体上不光是鬼爻所坐的地方受病，被它伤到的地方也有病痛。 | In general, it is not only the place where the Pressure line sits that is ill: the place it wounds also has pain. |
| 如克上六，当主头痛； | 比如克到上六，主头痛； | For instance, controlling the top line means headache; |
| 克间爻，则胸隔不利。 | 克到间爻，胸膈就不舒服。 | controlling a between line, the chest and diaphragm are uneasy. |
| 余可类推。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest can be reasoned by analogy. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2354 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2354*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 不唯官鬼克伤上六而头疼，即如官鬼所临之处，亦有病也。 | 不只是官鬼克伤上六爻才头疼，官鬼所坐的地方，也有病。 | It is not only that a Pressure line controlling and wounding the top line brings headache: the place where a Pressure line sits also has illness. |
| 如官鬼克间爻，或临间爻，皆主胸膈不利，忌神亦然。 | 比如官鬼克到间爻，或者坐在间爻上，都主胸膈不舒服，忌神也是这样。 | For instance, if a Pressure line controls a between line or sits on one, the chest and diaphragm are uneasy, and the Adversary does the same. |
| 余可类推。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest can be reasoned by analogy. |

> **按**:旧注开头的「上六」两个字是给经文「上六」注的名称（最上面一爻叫上六），下面从「大抵」起才是解释。王洪绪比旧注多两处：鬼「临」间爻也算，忌神也算。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary's opening 'shang liu' is a gloss on the verse's 'shang liu' (the top line is called the sixth at the top), and the explanation begins at 'in general'. Wang Hongxu goes beyond the old commentary in two places: a Pressure line 'sitting on' a between line also counts, and so does the Adversary.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2354 行:官伤上六，斯人当主头疼
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1188 行:官伤上六，斯人当主头疼

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.22、LY.ILL.HJC.27、LY.ILL.HJC.28

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.30 财爻动在上卦主吐、动在下卦主泻：被合住则欲吐不吐、欲泻不泻，是呕逆恶心、里急后重

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:财爻在卦中动：动在上卦，主呕吐；动在下卦，主腹泻。如果被合住，就想吐吐不出、想泻泻不出，像呕逆恶心、里急后重一类。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Wealth line moves in the hexagram: moving in the upper trigram it means vomiting, and in the lower trigram diarrhoea. If it is held by a combination, he wants to vomit and cannot, wants to purge and cannot, as in retching and nausea, or urgency with straining at stool.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6860 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6860*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财动卦中，不吐则泻； | 财爻在卦中动，不是呕吐就是腹泻； | A Wealth line moving in the hexagram: if not vomiting, then diarrhoea; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6861 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6861*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财在上卦动，主吐； | 财爻在上卦动，主呕吐； | Wealth moving in the upper trigram means vomiting; |
| 在下卦动，主泻。 | 在下卦动，主腹泻。 | moving in the lower trigram, diarrhoea. |
| 若逢合住，则欲吐不吐，欲泻不泻； | 如果遇上合住，就想吐吐不出，想泻泻不出； | If it is held by a combination, he wants to vomit and cannot, wants to purge and cannot; |
| 如呕逆恶心，里急后重之例。 | 就像呕逆恶心、里急后重这一类。 | like retching and nausea, or urgency with straining at stool. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2356 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2356*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财爻动临上卦主吐，动临下卦主泻，若逢合住，则欲吐不吐、欲泻不泻。 | 财爻动在上卦主呕吐，动在下卦主腹泻，如果遇上合住，就想吐吐不出、想泻泻不出。 | A Wealth line moving in the upper trigram means vomiting and in the lower trigram diarrhoea; if it is held by a combination, he wants to vomit and cannot, wants to purge and cannot. |

> **按**:「里急后重」是肚子里急着要排、排的时候又觉得下坠不尽。旧注用它和呕逆恶心一起，说明被合住时那种「想而不出」的样子。这一条的上卦、下卦，按字面是外卦、内卦。
>
> **Note:** 'Urgency with straining' (liji houzhong) is a pressing need in the belly to go, with a feeling of sinking and incomplete passing when it does. The old commentary uses it along with retching and nausea to show the state of 'wanting and not managing' when the line is held by a combination. The upper and lower trigram of this entry are, in the letter, the outer and inner trigram.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.88) 第 2356 行:财动卦中非吐则泻
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1191 行:财动卦中，不吐则泻

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.16、LY.ILL.HJC.17、LY.ILL.HJC.21

## LY.ILL.HJC.31 木爻兴旺在世爻上，不是发痒就是疼痛：寅木主痛，卯木主痒；又在本宫内卦是气不顺

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:木爻旺在世爻上发动，病不是发痒就是发疼：寅木主痛，卯木主痒；如果又在本宫的内卦，还有气不顺的毛病。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a Wood line rises on the Self line, the illness is either itching or pain: a Yin Wood line means pain, a Mao Wood line itching; if it is also in the inner trigram of the home palace, the qi is not flowing smoothly.

**《黄金策·疾病》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6863 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6863*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 木兴世上，非痒即疼。 | 木爻兴旺在世爻上，不是发痒就是疼痛。 | Wood rising on the Self line: if not itching, then pain. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6864 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6864*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 寅卯二爻属木，寅木主痛，卯木主痒，若在本宫内卦，又是气不顺也。 | 寅、卯两爻属木，寅木主疼痛，卯木主发痒；如果在本宫的内卦，又是气不顺。 | The Yin and Mao lines belong to Wood: Yin Wood means pain, Mao Wood itching; if it is in the inner trigram of the home palace, the qi is also not flowing smoothly. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2358 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2358*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 寅卯二爻属木，寅木主痛，卯主痒。 | 寅、卯两爻属木，寅木主痛，卯木主痒。 | The Yin and Mao lines belong to Wood: Yin Wood means pain, Mao Wood itching. |

> **按**:旧注末尾「若在本宫内卦，又是气不顺也」，王洪绪没有收。
>
> **Note:** Wang Hongxu does not take the old commentary's closing 'if it is in the inner trigram of the home palace, the qi is also not flowing smoothly'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2358 行:木兴世上，非痒即疼
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1194 行:木兴世上，非痒即疼

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.05

## LY.ILL.HJC.46 兄弟持世饮食减省：兄弟持世饮食必减，病也因贪食而得；旧注：兄弟不动又无气、财福旺则不是这样，是气短力弱之症难愈

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:兄弟爻持世，饮食一定比平时少，这病也是因为吃得太多得的。旧注补充：如果兄弟不动、又没有气，而卦里财爻、子孙爻旺，就不是这样，那是气短力弱的症候，不容易痊愈。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a Peer line holds the Self line the patient surely eats less than usual, and the illness was also got through eating too much. The old commentary adds: if the Peer line does not move and has no force, while Wealth and Output are strong in the hexagram, it is not so; it is then a syndrome of short breath and weak strength, not easily healed.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6925 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6925*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世挠兄弟，饮食减省于平时。 | 兄弟扰着世爻，饮食比平时减少。 | A Peer line troubling the Self line: eating is less than at ordinary times. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6926 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6926*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄弟持世，饮食必减。 | 兄弟持着世爻，饮食一定减少。 | A Peer line holding the Self line: eating is surely reduced. |
| 其病亦是贪食而得。 | 这病也是因为贪吃得来的。 | The illness too was got through gluttony. |
| 若不动又无气，而卦中财福爻旺者，则不然。 | 如果兄弟不动、又没有气，而卦里财爻、福爻旺的，就不是这样。 | If it does not move and has no force, while the Wealth and Output lines in the hexagram are strong, it is not so. |
| 乃是气短力弱之症，不易痊可。 | 那是气短力弱的症候，不容易痊愈。 | It is then a syndrome of short breath and weak strength, not easily healed. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2390 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2390*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄弟持世，饮食必减，其病亦因多食而得。 | 兄弟持世，饮食一定减少，这病也是因为吃得多得来的。 | A Peer line holding the Self line: eating is surely reduced, and the illness too was got through eating too much. |

> **按**:经文写「世挠兄弟」，王洪绪引的经文作「世持兄弟」；两家的注讲的都是兄弟持世。旧注用「贪食」，王洪绪用「多食」，是同一个意思。
>
> **Note:** The verse reads 'the Self troubled by a Peer' where Wang Hongxu quotes it as 'the Self holding a Peer'; both commentaries speak of a Peer holding the Self line. The old commentary says 'gluttony' and Wang Hongxu 'eating much', which come to the same thing.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.91) 第 2390 行:世持兄弟，饮食减省于平时
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1239 行:世挠兄弟，饮食减省于平时

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.21、LY.ILL.HJC.38
