## LY.ILL.HZL.03 占疾病·土动主泻、木动发寒、金动四肢满闷、火动发热；木足金头土胸腹火手目水耳肾；飞为化因伏为受病；世动在内下受病、应动在外上受病、间爻动胸膈病；《易镜》长男受病宜纯震不摇；大忌申酉持世木爻独发(申丧车酉丧服木棺椁)

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:土动主泻，木动发寒，金动主四肢或满闷，火动发热。木主足，金主头，土主胸腹，火主手目，水主耳肾。飞伏都旺相时，飞是化出病的原因，以伏为受病的地方。又：世是动爻，在内则下部受病；应是动爻，在外则上部受病；间爻动，主胸膈的病。《易镜》说：长男受病，宜纯震卦不动；小女染病，宜兑卦不动。大忌申、酉持世，木爻独发，因为申是丧车，酉是丧服，木是棺椁。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Earth moving means diarrhoea, Wood moving means chills, Metal moving means the four limbs or oppression, Fire moving means fever. Wood governs the feet, Metal the head, Earth the chest and belly, Fire the hands and eyes, Water the ears and kidneys. When the flying and the hidden are both strong, the flying is the cause the illness turns on and the hidden is where the illness is received. Again, the Self as the moving line, inside, the lower part receives the illness; the Other as the moving line, outside, the upper part receives it; a between line moving means an illness of the chest and diaphragm. The Yijing says: for a person of the eldest son to fall ill it is fitting that the pure Zhen hexagram does not move, and for the youngest daughter to be infected that the Dui hexagram does not move. It is greatly feared that Shen and You hold the Self and a Wood line moves alone, for Shen is the mourning cart, You the mourning dress and Wood the coffin.

**《火珠林·占疾病》原文** — 《火珠林》第 440 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining illness", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 440*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土动主泻，木动发寒，金动四肢或满闷，火动发热。 | 土动主泻，木动发寒，金动主四肢或满闷，火动发热。 | Earth moving means diarrhoea, Wood moving means chills, Metal moving means the four limbs or oppression, Fire moving means fever. |
| 木主足，金主头、土主胸腹，火主手目，水主耳肾。 | 木主足，金主头，土主胸腹，火主手目，水主耳肾。 | Wood governs the feet, Metal the head, Earth the chest and belly, Fire the hands and eyes, Water the ears and kidneys. |
| 飞伏俱旺相，飞为化因，以伏为受病。 | 飞伏都旺相时，飞是化出病的原因，以伏为受病的地方。 | When the flying and the hidden are both strong, the flying is the cause the illness turns on and the hidden is where the illness is received. |
| 又世为动爻，在内下受病，应为动爻，在外上受病，间爻动，主胸膈病症。 | 又：世是动爻，在内则下部受病；应是动爻，在外则上部受病；间爻动，主胸膈的病。 | Again: the Self as the moving line, inside, the lower part receives the illness; the Other as the moving line, outside, the upper part receives it; a between line moving means an illness of the chest and diaphragm. |

**《火珠林·占疾病》原文** — 《火珠林》第 441 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining illness", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 441*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 《易镜》云：且如长男受病，宜纯震之不摇，小女染病，则兑卦之不动。 | 《易镜》说：长男受病，宜纯震卦不动；小女染病，宜兑卦不动。 | The Yijing says: for a person of the eldest son to fall ill it is fitting that the pure Zhen hexagram does not move; for the youngest daughter to be infected that the Dui hexagram does not move. |
| 大忌申酉持世，木爻独发者，申为丧车，酉为丧服，木为棺椁耳。 | 大忌申、酉持世，木爻独发，因为申是丧车，酉是丧服，木是棺椁。 | It is greatly feared that Shen and You hold the Self and a Wood line moves alone, for Shen is the mourning cart, You the mourning dress and Wood the coffin. |

> **按**:这一段和海底眼的（LY.ILL.HDY.05）几乎一字不差：「土动主吐水动主泻……木主足金主头土主胸腹火主手水主脾肾，飞伏俱旺相飞为起因……」，以及「易镜云：且如长男受病，宜纯震之不摇，小女染病，则兑卦之不动」。两本书里哪一本抄哪一本，书里没有说。这里和海底眼有几处字不同：火珠林是「土动主泻」、海底眼是「土动主吐」；火珠林是「火主手目」、海底眼是「火主手」；火珠林是「水主耳肾」、海底眼是「水主脾肾」；火珠林是「飞为化因，以伏为受病」、海底眼是「飞为起因，以代为受病」。「化因」「起因」都按「病由之而起的原因」译。「《易镜》」是一部书名，这里译作 Yijing，不是《周易》。「长男」是震，「少女」（原文「小女」）是兑。申为丧车、酉为丧服、木为棺椁，是拿字面取象。
>
> **Note:** This passage is almost word for word the same as the Haidi Yan's (LY.ILL.HDY.05): 'Earth moving means vomiting, Water moving means diarrhoea ... Wood governs the feet, Metal the head, Earth the chest and belly, Fire the hands, Water the spleen and kidneys; the flying and the hidden both strong, the flying is the origin ...', and 'the Yijing says: for a person of the eldest son to fall ill it is fitting that the pure Zhen hexagram does not move; for the youngest daughter to be infected that the Dui hexagram does not move'. Which of the two books copied the other, the books do not say. There are a few differences in characters from the Haidi Yan: the Huozhu Lin has 'Earth moving means diarrhoea' where the Haidi Yan has 'Earth moving means vomiting'; 'Fire the hands and eyes' where the Haidi Yan has 'Fire the hands'; 'Water the ears and kidneys' where the Haidi Yan has 'Water the spleen and kidneys'; and 'the flying is the cause, the hidden is where the illness is received' where the Haidi Yan has 'the flying is the origin, the substitute is where the illness is received'. Both 'hua yin' and 'qi yin' are rendered 'the cause the illness arises from'. 'Yijing' is the title of a book, here rendered Yijing, and is not the Zhouyi. The eldest son is Zhen and the youngest daughter (in the original 'the small daughter') is Dui. Shen as the mourning cart, You as the mourning dress and Wood as the coffin are images taken from the wording.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《海底眼》:异文(相似 0.89) 第 861 行:木主足，金主头，土主胸腹，火主手，水主脾肾;异文(相似 0.86) 第 863 行:飞伏俱旺相，飞为起因，以代为受病;异文(相似 0.81) 第 863 行:又世为动爻，在内下受病。应为动爻，在外上受病。间爻动
- 《海底眼》:异文(相似 0.96) 第 865 行:易镜云：且如长男受病，宜纯震之不摇。少女染病，则兑卦之不动

相关:LY.ILL.HDY.05、LY.ILL.HZL.02、LY.ILL.DYTJ.12、LY.ILL.TXF.08

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.20 (十八)占疾病：壬午火鬼正值九四爻、火鬼主热；若占父母九二木财发动必伤、幸九五金一制其病可痊但牵连未脱

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:（十八）占疾病：壬午火鬼，正好在九四爻，火鬼主热。若占父母，九二的木财发动必然伤（克父母），幸好九五的金一制，他的病可以痊愈，只是牵连着还没有脱身。其余照这个类推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** (18) Divining illness: the Ren Wu Fire ghost falls exactly on the ninth in the fourth place, and a Fire ghost means heat. If a parent is divined for, the Wood Wealth on the ninth in the second place moving will surely injure (control Resource), but luckily the Metal on the ninth in the fifth place checks it, and his illness can be cured, only it is still tangled and has not yet shaken free. The rest follow by analogy.

**《火珠林·占卦例·(十八)占疾病》原文** — 《火珠林》第 777–778 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Worked applications: (18) divining illness", base text; Huozhu Lin, lines 777–778*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （十八）占疾病 | （十八）占疾病 | (18) Divining illness |
| 壬午火鬼，正值九四爻，火鬼主热。 | 壬午火鬼，正好在九四爻，火鬼主热。 | the Ren Wu Fire ghost falls exactly on the ninth in the fourth place, and a Fire ghost means heat. |
| 若占父母，其九二木财发动必伤，幸九五金一制，其病可痊，但牵连末脱。 | 若占父母，九二的木财发动必然伤，幸好九五的金一制，他的病可以痊愈，只是牵连着还没有脱身。 | If a parent (the Resource line) is divined for, the Wood Wealth on the ninth in the second place moving will surely injure, but luckily the Metal on the ninth in the fifth place checks it, and his illness can be cured, only it is still tangled and has not yet shaken free. |
| 余类推。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow by analogy. |

> **按**:这一节是火珠林后面「占卦例」一部分里的一条：那一部分用同一个卦逐项演示（十七占蚕、十八占疾病、十九占姓字……），所以「壬午火鬼」「九四爻」「九二木财」「九五金」指的是那一个卦上的爻（卦的排盘在这一节前面，原书用表格排，文件里排乱了，这里不收）。这一条本身能读出来的规矩是：火鬼主热；占父母病时，财爻发动克父母必伤，若有金爻制住木财，病就可以痊愈，但牵连着还没有脱身。「木财」是属木的财爻，「金一制」是金制木。「（十八）」是原文的编号。和「妻财独发，生鬼伤父；问病难瘳」（LY.ILL.HZL.18）是同一个道理，只是这里多了「有金制财」这一救。
>
> **Note:** This section is one entry in the part of the Huozhu Lin near the end on 'worked applications': that part works through item after item on one and the same hexagram (17 divining silkworms, 18 divining illness, 19 divining a name ...), and so 'Ren Wu Fire ghost', 'the ninth in the fourth place', 'the Wood Wealth on the ninth in the second place' and 'the Metal on the ninth in the fifth place' refer to the lines of that one hexagram (its layout is set out before this section, arranged as a table in the original and scrambled in the file, and is not taken here). What can be read out of this entry as a rule is: a Fire ghost means heat; divining a parent's illness, the Wealth line moving controls Resource and will surely injure, but if a Metal line checks the Wood Wealth the illness can be cured, only it is still tangled and has not shaken free. 'Wood Wealth' is a Wealth line belonging to Wood, and 'Metal checks it' is Metal controlling Wood. The '(18)' is the numbering of the original. It is the same principle as 'Wealth moving alone generates the ghost and injures the father; asking about illness it is hard to cure' (LY.ILL.HZL.18), only with the rescue of 'Metal checking Wealth' added here.

相关:LY.ILL.HZL.18、LY.ILL.HZL.07

---

## LY.ILL.TXF.03 欲定症候须看鬼临之五行：金鬼肺腑病咳嗽气喘或疮疖血光筋骨疼痛；木鬼四肢不利、风气肝胆、左瘫右痪口眼歪斜、头疼发热三焦口渴，加朱雀狂言乱语阳症伤寒；水鬼沉寒痼冷遗精白浊淋沥吐泻呕逆；火鬼寒热往来脾寒疟疾；土鬼脾胃发瘴水肿虚浮瘟疫时气；前卦火鬼后卦水鬼则服冷药过多变阴症

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:要定症候，必须看鬼临着的五行。金鬼：肺腑的病，咳嗽气喘，或者疮疖、血光，或者筋骨疼痛。木鬼：四肢不利，风气、肝胆病，左瘫右痪，口眼歪斜，头疼发热，三焦口渴；加朱雀，说胡话、乱语，是阳症伤寒。水鬼：沉寒痼冷，遗精白浊，小便淋沥，吐泻呕逆。火鬼：寒热往来，脾寒疟疾。土鬼：脾胃发瘴，水肿虚浮，瘟疫时气。如果前一卦是火鬼、后一卦是水鬼，前面的症候一定是阳症，服冷药过多，变成了阴症；如果午时以前占，遇到这两个鬼，应当说不要服药；过了头，以后必有变化。这说的是在火旺的月日占卦的情形。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To fix the symptoms, one must look at the phase the ghost sits on. A Metal ghost: illness of the lungs and viscera, coughing and wheezing, or sores and boils, or bleeding, or aching of the sinews and bones. A Wood ghost: the four limbs not free, wind-qi and illness of the liver and gall, left and right palsy, mouth and eyes awry, headache and fever, thirst in the Triple Burner; with the Vermilion Bird added, wild and confused speech, a yang cold-damage illness. A Water ghost: deep cold and chronic chill, seminal emission and turbid urine, dribbling urination, vomiting, diarrhoea and retching. A Fire ghost: alternate chills and fever, spleen chill and malaria. An Earth ghost: spleen and stomach miasma, water swelling and puffy weakness, epidemic and seasonal qi. If the earlier hexagram is a Fire ghost and the later a Water ghost, the earlier symptoms were surely yang, and taking too much cold medicine has turned them into a yin condition; if the divination is made before noon and these two ghosts are met, one should say do not take medicine; if it is overdone, there will surely be a change afterwards. This speaks of divining in a month or day when Fire is strong.

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4965 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4965*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 欲定症候，须看鬼临之五行。 | 要断定症候，必须看鬼临着的五行。 | To fix the symptoms, one must look at the phase the ghost sits on. |
| 若金鬼： | 如果是金鬼： | A Metal ghost: |
| 肺腑病， | 肺腑的病， | illness of the lungs and viscera, |
| 咳嗽气， | 咳嗽气喘， | coughing and wheezing, |
| 或疮疖、血光， | 或者是疮疖、血光， | or sores and boils, or bleeding, |
| 或筋骨疼痛。 | 或者是筋骨疼痛。 | or aching of the sinews and bones. |
| 木鬼： | 如果是木鬼： | A Wood ghost: |
| 四肢不利， | 四肢不灵便， | the four limbs not free, |
| 风气肝胆， | 风气、肝胆的病， | wind-qi and illness of the liver and gall, |
| 左瘫右痪， | 左瘫右痪， | left and right palsy, |
| 口眼歪斜， | 口眼歪斜， | mouth and eyes awry, |
| 头疼发热， | 头疼发热， | headache and fever, |
| 三焦口渴； | 三焦口渴； | thirst in the Triple Burner; |
| 加朱雀， | 加了朱雀， | with the Vermilion Bird added, |
| 狂言乱语， | 说胡话、乱语， | wild and confused speech, |
| 阳症伤寒。 | 是阳症伤寒。 | a yang cold-damage illness. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4967 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4967*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水鬼： | 如果是水鬼： | A Water ghost: |
| 沉寒痼冷， | 沉寒痼冷， | deep cold and chronic chill, |
| 遗精白浊， | 遗精、白浊， | seminal emission and turbid urine, |
| 小便淋沥， | 小便淋沥， | dribbling urination, |
| 吐泻呕逆。 | 吐泻呕逆。 | vomiting, diarrhoea and retching. |
| 火鬼： | 如果是火鬼： | A Fire ghost: |
| 寒热往来， | 寒热往来， | alternate chills and fever, |
| 脾寒疟疾。 | 脾寒疟疾。 | spleen chill and malaria. |
| 土鬼： | 如果是土鬼： | An Earth ghost: |
| 乃脾胃发瘴， | 是脾胃发瘴， | spleen and stomach miasma, |
| 水肿虚浮， | 水肿虚浮， | water swelling and puffy weakness, |
| 瘟疫时气。 | 瘟疫时气。 | epidemic and seasonal qi. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4968 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4968*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若前卦火鬼，后卦水鬼， | 如果前一卦是火鬼、后一卦是水鬼， | If the earlier hexagram is a Fire ghost and the later a Water ghost, |
| 必前之症候为阳， | 前面的症候一定是阳症， | the earlier symptoms were surely yang, |
| 服冷药过多， | 服冷药过多， | and taking too much cold medicine |
| 变为阴症。 | 变成了阴症。 | has turned them into a yin condition. |
| 若午前占， | 如果午时以前占， | If the divination is made before noon, |
| 遇此两鬼， | 遇到这两个鬼， | and these two ghosts are met, |
| 当言勿服药； | 应当说不要服药； | one should say do not take medicine; |
| 过当， | 过了头， | if it is overdone, |
| 后必有变化。 | 以后必定有变化。 | there will surely be a change afterwards. |
| 火鬼于火旺月日占是也。 | 这说的是在火旺的月日占卦的情形。 | This speaks of divining in a month or day when Fire is strong. |

> **按**:这一段和黄金策疾病章里的「五行症候」（LY.ILL.HJC.14 到 HJC.20 一带）、易隐「五行鬼各主之症」（LY.ILL.YY.13）是同一份名单，每种五行的具体病名略有不同：天玄赋的金鬼是「肺腑病、咳嗽气、疮疖、血光、筋骨疼痛」，木鬼里有「三焦口渴」和「加朱雀狂言乱语阳症伤寒」，火鬼只有「寒热往来、脾寒疟疾」（易隐的火鬼阴是心目之疾、阳是疮痍疖毒虐热），土鬼是「脾胃发瘴、水肿虚浮、瘟疫时气」。「遗精白浊」「淋沥」「痼冷」「脾寒疟疾」是医病名，照字面译；「三焦」是中医说的上中下三焦，不作解释。「前卦火鬼、后卦水鬼」说的是同一个人先后占了两卦：前面是火鬼阳症，若已服了太多冷药，后一卦水鬼就是变成了阴症。「若午前占，遇此两鬼，当言勿服药」的「午前」是午时以前；末一句说火鬼那一条指的是在火旺的月日占卦。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。
>
> **Note:** This passage is the same list as the 'symptoms by the five phases' in the Huangjin Ce's chapter on illness (LY.ILL.HJC.14 to HJC.20) and the Yi Yin's 'the symptoms each phase of ghost governs' (LY.ILL.YY.13), with the specific illness names differing a little for each phase: in the Tianxuan Fu the Metal ghost is 'illness of the lungs and viscera, coughing and wheezing, sores and boils, bleeding, aching sinews and bones'; the Wood ghost has 'thirst in the Triple Burner' and 'with the Vermilion Bird added, wild and confused speech, a yang cold-damage illness'; the Fire ghost has only 'alternate chills and fever, spleen chill and malaria' (the Yi Yin's Fire ghost is, if yin, illness of the heart and eyes, and if yang, sores, boils and hot malaria); and the Earth ghost is 'spleen and stomach miasma, water swelling and puffy weakness, epidemic and seasonal qi'. 'Seminal emission and turbid urine', 'dribbling urination', 'chronic chill' and 'spleen chill and malaria' are names of illnesses and are translated literally; the 'Triple Burner' is the upper, middle and lower burners of Chinese medicine and is not explained. 'The earlier hexagram a Fire ghost and the later a Water ghost' speaks of one person divining two hexagrams in succession: the earlier is a yang condition with the Fire ghost, and if too much cold medicine has already been taken, the Water ghost of the later hexagram is the change to a yin condition. 'If divined before noon and these two ghosts are met, say do not take medicine': the 'before noon' is before the Wu double-hour; the last sentence says that the Fire ghost's case refers to divining in a month or day when Fire is strong. After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote.

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.02、LY.ILL.TXF.04、LY.ILL.HJC.14、LY.ILL.YY.13、LY.ILL.ZS.22、LY.ILL.HDY.05

---

## LY.ILL.YM.26 分一身、四体、五脏、七情：流于六爻分一身、摇于八宫分四体、属于五行分五脏、现于六神分七情；注引卜筮元龟、黄金策、海底眼、解云为证

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼落在哪一爻，分的是一身的哪一处；鬼动在哪一宫（哪一卦），分的是四肢百体的哪一部分；鬼属哪一行，分的是哪个脏；鬼上的是哪个六神，分的是哪种情绪（七情）。注引了四家为证：卜筮元龟按爻位，从初爻的脚到六爻的头；黄金策按八卦，乾头坤腹、巽腰震足、兑口艮背、坎耳离目；海底眼按五行和地支；解云按六神：白虎是兵祸丧痛、青龙是酒色风流、玄武是忧愁思虑、勾陈是夹带怀胎、朱雀是恼怒焦烦、螣蛇是惊惶恐怖。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** By which of the six lines the ghost flows through, the part of the whole body is told; by which of the eight palaces it stirs in, the four limbs and trunk; by which of the five phases it belongs to, which of the viscera; by which of the six spirits it appears in, which of the seven emotions. The note quotes four authorities: the Bushi Yuangui by line position, from the foot on the first line to the head on the sixth; the Huangjin Ce by trigram, Qian the head and Kun the belly, Xun the waist and Zhen the foot, Dui the mouth and Gen the back, Kan the ear and Li the eye; the Haidi Yan by the five phases and branches; and the explanation by the six spirits: the White Tiger is war, mourning and grief, the Azure Dragon wine, lust and dissipation, the Dark Tortoise worry and anxious thought, Hook Earth complication and pregnancy, the Vermilion Bird anger and vexation, the Coiling Snake fright and terror.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1228 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1228*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 流于六爻，以分其一身也，摇于八宫，以分其四体也，属于五行，以分其五脏也，现于六神，以分其七情也。 | 鬼流到哪一爻，用来分是一身的哪一处；鬼在哪一宫动，用来分是四肢百体的哪一部分；鬼属哪一行，用来分是哪个脏；鬼上的是哪个六神，用来分是哪种情绪（七情）。 | By which of the six lines it flows through, the parts of the one body are distinguished; by which of the eight palaces it stirs in, the four limbs and trunk are distinguished; by which of the five phases it belongs to, the five viscera are distinguished; by which of the six spirits it appears in, the seven emotions are distinguished. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1229 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1229*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卜筮元龟：鬼在初爻两足伤，二爻双腿患非常，三爻腰股常轻软，四爻心腹及胸堂，五爻脏腑司喉咽，六爻头上患为算戗（？）。 | 《卜筮元龟》说：鬼在初爻，两只脚受伤；二爻，两条腿有非同寻常的病；三爻，腰和大腿常常发软；四爻，心、腹和胸口；五爻，脏腑，管喉咙咽部；六爻，头上的病，算作戗（？）。 | The Bushi Yuangui says: the ghost on the first line, both feet are hurt; on the second line, the two legs have extraordinary trouble; on the third line, the waist and thighs are often weak and soft; on the fourth line, the heart, belly and chest; on the fifth line, the viscera and bowels, governing the throat; on the sixth line, trouble in the head, counted as 'qiang' (?). |
| 黄金策：乾为头兮坤为腹，巽为腰兮震为足，兑为口兮艮为背，坎为耳兮离为目。 | 《黄金策》说：乾是头，坤是腹；巽是腰，震是脚；兑是口，艮是背；坎是耳，离是目。 | The Huangjin Ce says: Qian is the head and Kun the belly; Xun the waist and Zhen the foot; Dui the mouth and Gen the back; Kan the ear and Li the eye. |
| 海底眼：喉病金兮木四枝，风邪疼痛起黄形，辰戌胃胸生呕逆，丑为肚腹未为脾，火动热极三焦咳，血腥眼目及疮痍，水鬼发寒因冷得，泄泻虚劳耳肾衰。 | 《海底眼》说：喉咙的病属金，木属四肢；风邪疼痛，起黄色的病相；辰、戌是胃和胸，生呕吐反逆；丑是肚腹，未是脾；火动热到极点，三焦咳嗽；血腥、眼睛以及疮疡；水鬼发寒，是因为受了冷；泄泻、虚劳，耳朵和肾衰弱。 | The Haidi Yan says: throat trouble belongs to Metal, and Wood to the four limbs; wind-evil brings pain and a yellow look; Chen and Xu are the stomach and chest, producing vomiting and retching; Chou is the belly and Wei the spleen; Fire moving is extreme heat and coughing of the three burners; blood, the eyes and sores; a Water ghost brings chills because of cold; diarrhoea, consumption, and weakness of the ears and kidneys. |
| 有为火鬼病于心、小肠命三焦，水鬼病于肾膀胱，金鬼病于肺大肠，木鬼病于肝胆，土鬼病于脾胃也。 | 又有：火鬼的病在心、小肠、命门和三焦；水鬼的病在肾和膀胱；金鬼的病在肺和大肠；木鬼的病在肝和胆；土鬼的病在脾和胃。 | There is also: a Fire ghost's illness is in the heart, small intestine, life gate and the three burners; a Water ghost's in the kidneys and bladder; a Metal ghost's in the lungs and large intestine; a Wood ghost's in the liver and gallbladder; an Earth ghost's in the spleen and stomach. |
| 解云：虎自兵丧哀痛由，龙贪酒色爱风流，忧愁思虑居元武，夹带怀胎谓值勾，脑（恼）怒焦烦朱雀噪，惊惶恐怖伴蛇游。） | 解说：白虎，是从兵祸、丧事、悲哀、痛苦而起；青龙，是贪酒好色、爱风流；忧愁思虑，归玄武；夹带、怀胎，说是遇上勾陈；恼怒焦烦，朱雀吵嚷；惊惶恐怖，有螣蛇相伴。 | The explanation says: the White Tiger's cause is war, mourning, grief and pain; the Azure Dragon is greed for wine and women and love of pleasure; worry and anxious thought dwell with the Dark Tortoise; complication and pregnancy are said to fall on Hook Earth; anger and vexation (the original writes 'brain', with 'vexation' in brackets), the Vermilion Bird is noisy; fright and terror go with the Coiling Snake. |

> **按**:这一条经文只有一句总纲，分身体的实际办法全在注里，注引了四家：《卜筮元龟》按爻位，《黄金策》按八卦（与 LY.ILL.HJC.04 相同），《海底眼》按五行地支，「解云」按六神。「六爻头上患为算戗（？）」的「算戗」二字，原文自己在后面标了问号，是转录者对这两个字没有把握，这里照录，不改。「有为火鬼病于心」的「有为」疑是「又为」之误，译作「又有」。「元武」是「玄武」避讳的写法。与易隐（LY.ILL.YY.02、YY.11–YY.13）、黄金策（LY.ILL.HJC.02–HJC.05）讲的是同一件事，各家配法略有出入，以各条为准。
>
> **Note:** In this entry the text gives only a single sentence of outline, and the actual ways of dividing the body are all in the note, which quotes four authorities: the Bushi Yuangui by line position, the Huangjin Ce by trigram (the same as LY.ILL.HJC.04), the Haidi Yan by the five phases and branches, and the 'explanation' by the six spirits. In 'the sixth line, trouble in the head, counted as qiang', the two characters 'suan qiang' are followed in the original by a question mark of the transcriber's own, showing doubt about them, and are copied as they stand. In the sentence about a Fire ghost's illness in the heart, the opening 'yǒu wéi' looks like an error for 'yòu wéi' (also), and is rendered 'there is also'. 'Yuan wu' is the taboo-avoiding form of 'Xuan wu' (Dark Tortoise). The same matter is treated by the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.02, YY.11–YY.13) and the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.02–HJC.05), with slightly different assignments in each; go by each entry.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.25、LY.ILL.YM.27、LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.03、LY.ILL.HJC.04、LY.ILL.HJC.05、LY.ILL.YY.02、LY.ILL.YY.11、LY.ILL.YY.12、LY.ILL.YY.13、LY.ILL.YY.17、LY.ILL.BSZZ.25

## LY.ILL.YM.31 问症发真假、患其成疾、养病于外、避暑：鬼实则是鬼虚则非；随墓助伤已成疾；用神坏其疾难疗；养病避暑忌随墓助伤、世折鬼动

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:有人问病症发作是不是真的，鬼爻实就是真的，鬼爻虚就不是。有人占担心会不会成病，遇到随墓、助伤，病就已经成了；用神如果坏了，这个病怎么能治好呢？占在外面养病的，遇到随墓、助伤，世爻受损、鬼动，不行；在这里避暑的，也是这个道理。注：官鬼空、破、散、绝叫做虚，旺相、有生扶叫做实；用神空、破、绝、散就会成病，旺相有生扶就不会成病。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If someone asks whether the outbreak of symptoms is real: when the ghost is full it is real, when the ghost is empty it is not. If someone divines whether a worry will become an illness, following into the tomb and helping to harm mean the illness has already formed; if the Subject is spoiled, how can the illness be cured? For one who divines convalescing away from home, following into the tomb, helping to harm, the Self broken and the ghost moving will not do; for one escaping the summer heat here, it is the same. The note says: the Pressure line Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is called empty, and thriving and strong, generated and supported, is called full; the Subject Void, broken, in Extinction or scattered, the illness forms, and thriving and strong, generated and supported, it does not form.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1237 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1237*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 有问症发是真，盖鬼实则是，鬼虚则非也。 | 有人问病症发作是真的吗，鬼爻实就是真的，鬼爻虚就不是。 | If someone asks whether the outbreak of symptoms is real: when the ghost is full it is real, when the ghost is empty it is not. |
| 有筮患其成疾，随墓助伤，已成疾矣； | 有人占担心会不会成病，遇到随墓、助伤，病就已经成了； | If someone divines whether a worry will become an illness, following into the tomb and helping to harm mean the illness has already formed; |
| 用神若坏，其疾奚能疗乎？ | 用神如果坏了，这个病怎么能治好呢？ | if the Subject is spoiled, how can the illness be cured? |
| 卜养病于外者，随墓助伤，世折鬼动不可也。 | 占在外面养病的，遇到随墓、助伤，世爻受损、鬼动，都不行。 | For one who divines convalescing away from home, following into the tomb, helping to harm, the Self broken and the ghost moving will not do. |
| 避暑于此者，亦由是也。 | 在这里避暑的，也是这个道理。 | for one escaping the summer heat here, it is the same. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1238 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1238*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼空破散绝谓之虚，旺相生扶谓之实，用神空破绝散则成疾，旺相生扶则不成疾。 | 官鬼空、破、散、绝叫做虚，旺相、有生扶叫做实；用神空、破、绝、散就会成病，旺相有生扶就不会成病。 | The Pressure line Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is called empty, and thriving and strong, generated and supported, is called full; the Subject Void, broken, in Extinction or scattered, the illness forms, and thriving and strong, generated and supported, it does not form. |

> **按**:这一条问的是病有没有真的发出来、会不会成病、能不能到外面去养，注里只补了一个判据：虚实看鬼，成不成看用神。「虚」是空、破、散、绝，「实」是旺相、有生扶。前面说的是「鬼实则是」，后面注里说的是「用神旺相则不成疾」，两处一个看鬼、一个看用神，并不矛盾：问症发是不是真，问的是鬼；问会不会成病，问的是人的用神。「避暑于此」的「此」字，原文没有交代是哪里，这里照译。
>
> **Note:** This entry asks whether the illness has really broken out, whether it will become an illness, and whether one may go away to convalesce, and the note adds only one criterion: emptiness or fullness is read from the ghost, and whether it forms from the Subject. 'Empty' is Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction; 'full' is thriving, strong, generated or supported. The text says 'the ghost full is real' and the note says 'the Subject thriving and strong, it does not form'; one looks at the ghost and the other at the Subject, and they do not contradict: asking whether the outbreak is real asks about the ghost, and asking whether it will become an illness asks about the person's Subject. The 'here' in 'escaping the heat here' is not identified in the original and is rendered as it stands.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.30、LY.ILL.YM.32、LY.ILL.YM.24、LY.ILL.HJC.85、LY.ILL.YY.15

---

## LY.ILL.YY.11 病症以官鬼为凭：鬼持身世命用、鬼动来刑害克冲身世命用、官鬼伏其下、或身世命用化官鬼，各随五行断；鬼属阴在内卦金肺木肝胆水肾火心小肠土脾胃大肠，鬼属阳在外卦金四肢骨节牙齿右耳、木筋骨左耳、水口皮痰血汗、土鼻腹背肌肉、火目胸手心脚底；八卦乾首坤腹震足巽股坎耳离目艮背指兑口舌

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:断病症以官鬼为凭据：鬼爻持在身、世、命爻、用爻上，或者鬼发动来刑、害、克、冲它们，或者官鬼伏在它们下面，或者它们发动变出官鬼，都各按官鬼的五行来断。鬼属阴爻、在内卦的：金是肺，木是肝胆，水是肾，子（水）是膀胱，火是心和小肠，土是脾、胃、大肠。鬼属阳爻、在外卦的：金是四肢、骨节、牙齿、右耳、小便，木是筋骨、左耳，水是口、嘴、皮、尿、痰涎、血、汗，土是鼻尖、腹背、肌肉，火是眼睛、胸、手心、脚底；另外辰、戌是头顶，丑、未是肩背。再按八卦：乾是头，坤是腹，震是脚，巽是大腿，坎是耳朵，离是眼睛，艮是背和手指，兑是口和舌头。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The symptoms are judged by the Pressure line: if the Pressure line holds the Body, the Self, the destiny line or the Subject line; or the ghost moves to punish, harm, control or clash them; or the Pressure line is hidden beneath them; or they move and change into Pressure: in every case judge by the element of Pressure. If the ghost is yin and in the inner trigram: Metal is the lungs, Wood the liver and gallbladder, Water the kidneys and Zi the bladder, Fire the heart and small intestine, Earth the spleen, stomach and large intestine. If the ghost is yang and in the outer trigram: Metal is the limbs, bone joints, teeth, right ear and urine; Wood the sinews and bones and the left ear; Water the mouth, lips, skin, urine, phlegm and spittle, blood and sweat; Earth the tip of the nose, belly and back, and flesh; Fire the eyes, chest, palms and soles; Chen and Xu are the crown of the head and Chou and Wei the shoulders and back. And by the eight trigrams: Qian is the head, Kun the belly, Zhen the feet, Xun the thighs, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Gen the back and fingers, and Dui the mouth and tongue.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2997–2999 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, lines 2997–2999*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以官鬼为凭。 | 断病症以官鬼为凭据。 | The symptoms are judged by the Pressure line. |
| 如鬼爻持身世，命爻用爻或鬼动来刑害克冲身世命用爻者，或官鬼伏于身世命用爻下者，或身世命用动化官鬼者，各随五行断之也。 | 比如鬼爻持在身、世、命爻、用爻上，或者鬼发动来刑、害、克、冲身、世、命、用爻，或者官鬼伏在身、世、命、用爻下面，或者身、世、命、用爻发动变出官鬼，都各按它的五行来断。 | For example, if the Pressure line holds the Body, the Self, the destiny line or the Subject line; or the ghost moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Body, Self, destiny or Subject line; or the Pressure line is hidden beneath the Body, Self, destiny or Subject line; or the Body, Self, destiny or Subject line moves and changes into Pressure: in every case judge by its element. |
| 鬼属阴，在内卦，则金为肺，木为肝胆，水为肾，子膀胱，火为心小肠，土为脾胃大肠也。 | 鬼属阴爻、在内卦的：金是肺，木是肝胆，水是肾，子（水）是膀胱，火是心和小肠，土是脾、胃、大肠。 | If the ghost is yin and in the inner trigram: Metal is the lungs, Wood the liver and gallbladder, Water the kidneys and Zi the bladder, Fire the heart and small intestine, and Earth the spleen, stomach and large intestine. |
| 鬼属阳，在外卦，则金为四肢骨节牙齿右耳小便也，木为筋骨左耳也，水为口嘴皮溺痰涎血汗也，土为鼻准腹背肌肉也，火为目胸手心脚底也，又辰戌为顶门，丑未为肩背也。 | 鬼属阳爻、在外卦的：金是四肢、骨节、牙齿、右耳、小便，木是筋骨、左耳，水是口、嘴、皮、尿、痰涎、血、汗，土是鼻尖、腹背、肌肉，火是眼睛、胸、手心、脚底；另外辰、戌是头顶，丑、未是肩背。 | If the ghost is yang and in the outer trigram: Metal is the limbs, bone joints, teeth, right ear and urine; Wood the sinews and bones and the left ear; Water the mouth, lips, skin, urine, phlegm and spittle, blood and sweat; Earth the tip of the nose, belly and back, and flesh; Fire the eyes, chest, palms and soles; and Chen and Xu are the crown of the head and Chou and Wei the shoulders and back. |
| 又八卦乾为首，坤为腹，震为足，巽为股，坎为耳，离为目，艮为背指，兑为口舌也。 | 又按八卦：乾是头，坤是腹，震是脚，巽是大腿，坎是耳朵，离是眼睛，艮是背和手指，兑是口和舌头。 | Again by the eight trigrams: Qian is the head, Kun the belly, Zhen the feet, Xun the thighs, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Gen the back and fingers, and Dui the mouth and tongue. |

> **按**:这一条是易隐病症段的总纲，把五行、八卦、内外卦阴阳三套办法叠在一起：先看鬼在哪个五行，再看它属阴属阳、在内在外，得出是哪个脏腑或身体部位。黄金策讲同一件事的地方见 LY.ILL.HJC.02（五行定脏腑）、LY.ILL.HJC.04（八宫定病位）。「水为口嘴皮溺痰涎血汗」里的「溺」是尿，金那一栏的「小便」也是尿，原文两处都写。
>
> **Note:** This is the general outline of the Yi Yin's section on symptoms and lays three systems over one another — the five elements, the eight trigrams, and yin-yang with inner and outer trigrams: first see which element the ghost is, then whether it is yin or yang and inner or outer, to arrive at which viscus or part of the body. Where the Huangjin Ce treats the same thing see LY.ILL.HJC.02 (the element fixes the viscus) and LY.ILL.HJC.04 (the palace fixes the site of the illness). In 'Water is the mouth, lips, skin, urine ...' the 'ni' is urine, and 'xiaobian' in the Metal column is also urine; the original writes both.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.77) 第 163–165 行:宫诸身乾为首，坤为腹，震为足，巽为股，坎为耳，离为目，艮为手，兑为口

相关:LY.ILL.YY.12、LY.ILL.YY.13、LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.04、LY.ILL.HJC.11

---

## LY.ILL.YY.12 按爻位分身体：初爻足、二爻股膝、三爻腹小腹腰臀肛门小便、四爻胸胃乳、五爻面项手（水口火目土鼻水左耳金右耳）、六爻头脑；遇鬼伏、鬼化、鬼动来伤之处必带疾

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:再按爻位来分：初爻是脚，二爻是大腿和膝，三爻是腹、小腹、腰、臀、肛门、小便，四爻是胸、胃、乳，五爻是脸、颈项、手（水是口，火是眼睛，土是鼻，水是左耳，金是右耳），六爻是头和脑。凡是哪一爻遇到鬼伏在下面、变出鬼、或者鬼发动来伤到它，那个部位一定留下毛病。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Again by the position of the lines: the first line is the feet, the second the thighs and knees, the third the belly, lower belly, waist, buttocks, anus and urination, the fourth the chest, stomach and breasts, the fifth the face, neck and hands (Water the mouth, Fire the eyes, Earth the nose, Water the left ear, Metal the right ear), and the sixth the head and brain. Wherever a line has a ghost hidden beneath it, or changes into a ghost, or a ghost moves to harm it, that place will surely be left with an ailment.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2999 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 2999*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又分宫，初爻，足也。 | 又按爻位来分：初爻是脚。 | Again by the position of the lines: the first line is the feet. |
| 二爻，股膝也。 | 二爻是大腿和膝。 | The second line is the thighs and knees. |
| 三爻，腹小腹腰臀肛门小便也。 | 三爻是腹、小腹、腰、臀、肛门、小便。 | The third line is the belly, lower belly, waist, buttocks, anus and urination. |
| 四爻，胸胃乳也。 | 四爻是胸、胃、乳。 | The fourth line is the chest, stomach and breasts. |
| 五爻，面项手。 | 五爻是脸、颈项、手。 | The fifth line is the face, neck and hands. |
| 水为口，火为目，土为鼻，水左耳，金右耳也。 | 水是口，火是眼睛，土是鼻，水是左耳，金是右耳。 | Water is the mouth, Fire the eyes, Earth the nose, Water the left ear and Metal the right ear. |
| 六爻头脑也。 | 六爻是头和脑。 | The sixth line is the head and brain. |
| 凡遇鬼伏鬼化鬼，或鬼动来伤者，必带疾也。 | 凡是（这一爻）遇到伏着鬼、变出鬼，或者有鬼发动来伤它的，一定留下毛病。 | Whenever a line has a ghost hidden beneath it or changes into a ghost, or a ghost moves to harm it, it will surely be left with an ailment. |

> **打架**:左耳属哪一行：同一段里，上一条（LY.ILL.YY.11）说木是筋骨、左耳，这里说「水左耳，金右耳」，两处对左耳的五行不一致；书里没有说明，应是传抄的差异。易隐别处（第 1447 行讲相貌处）是「木聋左耳，金虎右耳」，与这里说水的一处不同。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which element the left ear belongs to: within the same passage, the previous entry (LY.ILL.YY.11) says Wood is the sinews and bones and the left ear, and here it says 'Water the left ear, Metal the right ear', so the two disagree on the left ear's element; the book gives no explanation and it is likely a difference in transmission. Elsewhere the Yi Yin (line 1447, on appearance) has 'Wood deaf in the left ear, Metal and Tiger the right ear', which differs from this one place that says Water.

> **按**:这里的分法是从初爻（脚）一路往上到六爻（头脑），和上面鬼谷分爻（LY.ILL.YY.02）的左栏一致。「水为口，火为目，土为鼻，水左耳，金右耳」是接在五爻（脸）后面的，讲脸上的五官各归哪一行。
>
> **Note:** The division here runs from the first line (feet) up to the sixth (head and brain) and agrees with the left column of Guigu's division of the lines (LY.ILL.YY.02). 'Water the mouth, Fire the eyes, Earth the nose, Water the left ear, Metal the right ear' follows the fifth line (the face) and assigns the features of the face to the elements.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.11、LY.ILL.YY.02、LY.ILL.HJC.28、LY.ILL.HJC.29

---

## LY.ILL.YY.13 五行鬼各主之症：金鬼阴肺腑之疾阳骨节疼痛脓血淋漓；木鬼阴肝胆之疾阳四肢风气酸疼口眼歪斜；水鬼阴遗精盗汗肾竭血枯经闭阳呕吐泄泻；火鬼阴心目之疾阳疮痍疖毒虐热；土鬼阴虚浮气喘水蛊脾泄阳瘟疫时气，辰咽喉肿塞戌腹痛胃伤丑气促痿痺未翻胃噎膈痨嗽

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用爻遇到金鬼：属阴是肺腑的病，属阳是骨节疼痛、脓血淋漓；金鬼旺，是泻肚子再带一样生僻的病；金鬼衰，是血里生脓。遇到木鬼：属阴是肝胆的病，属阳是四肢风气酸疼、口眼歪斜、头疼口干；木动的，阳的痒、阴的疼；木鬼旺，是风病带热；木鬼衰，是在同一个字的病上再生出风来。遇到水鬼：属阴是遗精、盗汗、肾气枯竭，女的是血枯闭经，属阳是呕吐泄泻；水鬼旺，是呕吐带咳嗽；水鬼衰，是受寒腹泻兼虚弱。遇到火鬼：属阴是心和眼睛的病，属阳是疮痍疖毒、疟热呻吟；火鬼旺，先冷后热；火鬼衰，先热后冷。遇到土鬼：属阴是虚浮气喘、水蛊、脾泄，属阳是瘟疫时气；辰鬼是咽喉肿塞，戌鬼是腹痛胃伤，丑鬼是气促痿痹，未鬼是翻胃、噎膈、痨病咳嗽。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Subject line meets a Metal ghost: yin means a disease of the lungs and viscera, yang means aching bone joints and dripping pus and blood; a strong Metal ghost brings diarrhea together with a rare ailment, and a weak Metal ghost brings pus forming within the blood. Meeting a Wood ghost: yin means a disease of the liver and gallbladder, yang means wind-qi with aching limbs, a crooked mouth and eyes, headache and a dry mouth; when Wood moves, yang itches and yin aches; a strong Wood ghost brings wind sickness with heat, a weak one wind growing upon the same rare ailment. Meeting a Water ghost: yin means seminal emission, night sweats and exhausted kidneys, and in a woman dried-up blood and stopped menses; yang means vomiting and diarrhea; a strong Water ghost brings vomiting with cough, a weak one cold diarrhea with weakness. Meeting a Fire ghost: yin means a disease of the heart and eyes, yang means sores, boils and poison, malarial fever and groaning; a strong Fire ghost brings cold first and then heat, a weak one heat first and then cold. Meeting an Earth ghost: yin means puffy weakness and wheezing, water-swelling and spleen diarrhea, yang means epidemic and seasonal qi; a Chen ghost is a swollen blocked throat, a Xu ghost belly pain and a damaged stomach, a Chou ghost shortness of breath and limp numbness, and a Wei ghost regurgitation, choking in the gullet, consumption and cough.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2999 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 2999*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用逢金鬼者，阴则肺腑之疾，阳则骨节疼痛，脓血淋漓也。 | 用爻遇到金鬼的，属阴就是肺腑一类的病，属阳就是骨节疼痛、脓血淋漓。 | If the Subject line meets a Metal ghost, yin means a disease of the lungs and viscera, and yang means aching bone joints and dripping pus and blood. |
| 又金鬼旺，泻中带(此处之字上疒下去)。 | 另外金鬼旺的，是泻肚子，还带着（此处原书有一个字，上面是病字头、下面是个「去」字，是个生僻的病字，书里没有解释）。 | Also a strong Metal ghost: diarrhea together with (here the book has a rare character for an ailment, written with the sickness radical above and the character for 'go' below, which the book does not explain). |
| 金鬼衰，血内生脓也。 | 金鬼衰弱的，血里生脓。 | A weak Metal ghost: pus forms within the blood. |
| 用逢木鬼者，阴则肝胆之疾，阳则四肢疯气酸疼，口眼歪斜，头疼口燥也。 | 用爻遇到木鬼的，属阴是肝胆的病，属阳是四肢风气酸疼、口眼歪斜、头疼口干。 | If the Subject line meets a Wood ghost, yin means a disease of the liver and gallbladder, and yang means wind-qi with aching limbs, a crooked mouth and eyes, headache and a dry mouth. |
| 又木动者，阳痒而阴疼。 | 另外木发动的，属阳的痒，属阴的疼。 | Also when Wood moves, yang itches and yin aches. |
| 木官旺，疯中带热，木鬼衰(此处之字上疒下去)上生疯也。 | 木官旺的，是风病中带热；木鬼衰的，是在（又是那个上面病字头、下面「去」字的字）上再生出风来。 | A strong Wood Pressure line: wind sickness with heat; a weak Wood ghost: wind grows upon (the same rare ailment character, written with the sickness radical above and 'go' below). |
| 用逢水鬼者，阴则遗精盗汗，肾竭，女为血枯经闭，阳则呕吐泄泻也。 | 用爻遇到水鬼的，属阴是遗精、盗汗、肾气枯竭，女的是血枯、闭经，属阳是呕吐、泄泻。 | If the Subject line meets a Water ghost, yin means seminal emission, night sweats and exhausted kidneys, and in a woman dried-up blood and stopped menses; yang means vomiting and diarrhea. |
| 又水鬼旺，呕中带嗽。 | 另外水鬼旺的，呕吐中带着咳嗽。 | Also a strong Water ghost: vomiting together with cough. |
| 水鬼衰，寒泄兼虚也。 | 水鬼衰弱的，是受寒腹泻再加虚弱。 | A weak Water ghost: cold diarrhea together with weakness. |
| 用逢火鬼者，阴则心目之疾，阳则疮痍疖毒，虐热吟呻也。 | 用爻遇到火鬼的，属阴是心和眼睛的病，属阳是疮痍疖毒、疟热呻吟。 | If the Subject line meets a Fire ghost, yin means a disease of the heart and eyes, and yang means sores, boils and poison, malarial fever and groaning. |
| 又火鬼旺，先寒后热，火鬼衰先热后寒也。 | 另外火鬼旺的，先冷后热；火鬼衰的，先热后冷。 | Also a strong Fire ghost: cold first and then heat; a weak Fire ghost: heat first and then cold. |
| 用逢土鬼者，阴则虚浮气喘，水蛊脾泄，阳则瘟疫时气也。 | 用爻遇到土鬼的，属阴是虚浮气喘、水蛊、脾泄，属阳是瘟疫、时气。 | If the Subject line meets an Earth ghost, yin means puffy weakness and wheezing, water-swelling and spleen diarrhea, and yang means epidemic and seasonal qi. |
| 又辰鬼，则咽喉肿塞。 | 另外辰鬼，是咽喉肿塞。 | Also a Chen ghost: a swollen, blocked throat. |
| 戌鬼，则腹痛胃伤。 | 戌鬼，是腹痛、胃伤。 | A Xu ghost: belly pain and a damaged stomach. |
| 丑鬼，则气促痿痺。 | 丑鬼，是气喘急促、痿痹。 | A Chou ghost: shortness of breath, limpness and numbness. |
| 未鬼则翻胃噎膈痨嗽也。 | 未鬼是翻胃、噎膈、痨病咳嗽。 | A Wei ghost: regurgitation, choking in the gullet, consumption and cough. |

> **按**:原书里有两处写着「（此处之字上疒下去）」，是整理者的注：那个字上面是病字头、下面是「去」，一个生僻的病字，整理者没有打出来，病名不明，译文照写不猜。「虐热」应是疟热（疟疾发热），原书写作「虐」。「水蛊」是腹部胀水的病，「膈」「噎膈」是吞咽梗阻，「痿痺」是肢体软弱、麻木疼痛，都按字面。这一条和黄金策的五行症候（LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.04）是同一个路子，但易隐更细：每一行分阴阳，又分旺衰，土另分辰戌丑未四种。
>
> **Note:** The original has two places marked '(the character here: sickness radical above, go below)', the transcriber's note: a rare character for an ailment which the transcriber could not enter, so the name of the ailment is unknown and the translation writes it as it stands and does not guess. 'Malarial fever' (nuere) is a writing of 'nue' with the wrong character in the original. 'Water-swelling' (shuigu) is a disease with a belly swollen with fluid, 'yege' is obstruction in swallowing, and 'weibi' is limpness of the limbs with numbness and pain, all rendered literally. This entry is on the same road as the Huangjin Ce's element symptoms (LY.ILL.HJC.02 and LY.ILL.HJC.04) but the Yi Yin is finer: each element is divided into yin and yang and into strong and weak, and Earth further into Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.11、LY.ILL.YY.14、LY.ILL.HJC.02、LY.ILL.HJC.04

---

## LY.ILL.YY.14 寒热与阴阳症：水鬼化火、火鬼化水是往来寒热；内鬼冲外、外鬼冲内是内外感伤；鬼内动下元之症、外动上焦之症；世鬼出现阳症、伏藏阴症；阴鬼化阳阴症变阳、阳鬼化阴阳症变阴

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:水鬼变火、火鬼变水的，是忽冷忽热；内卦的鬼冲外卦、外卦的鬼冲内卦的，是内外都受了伤。鬼在内卦发动，是下元的病症；鬼在外卦发动，是上焦的病症。世爻上的鬼出现，是阳症；世爻上的鬼伏藏，是阴症。阴鬼变阳，阴症变成阳症；阳鬼变阴，阳症变成阴症。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Water ghost changing into Fire, or a Fire ghost into Water, means alternating chills and fever; an inner ghost clashing the outer, or an outer ghost the inner, means injury both within and without. A ghost moving in the inner trigram is a disorder of the lower origin; a ghost moving in the outer trigram is a disorder of the upper burner. A ghost at the Self appearing openly is a yang condition, and one at the Self hidden is a yin condition. A yin ghost changing into yang: a yin condition turns yang; a yang ghost changing into yin: a yang condition turns yin.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2999 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 2999*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水鬼化火，火鬼化水者，往来寒热也。 | 水鬼变火、火鬼变水的，是忽冷忽热（寒热往来）。 | A Water ghost changing into Fire, or a Fire ghost into Water, means alternating chills and fever. |
| 内鬼冲外，外鬼冲内者，内外感伤也。 | 内卦的鬼冲外卦、外卦的鬼冲内卦的，是内外都受了伤。 | An inner ghost clashing the outer, or an outer ghost the inner, means injury both within and without. |
| 鬼内动，下元之症。 | 鬼在内卦发动，是下元的病症。 | A ghost moving in the inner trigram is a disorder of the lower origin. |
| 鬼外动，上焦之症。 | 鬼在外卦发动，是上焦的病症。 | A ghost moving in the outer trigram is a disorder of the upper burner. |
| 世鬼出现，阳症。 | 世爻上的鬼出现，是阳症。 | A ghost at the Self appearing openly is a yang condition. |
| 世鬼伏藏，阴症。 | 世爻上的鬼伏藏，是阴症。 | A ghost at the Self hidden is a yin condition. |
| 阴鬼化阳，阴症变阳。 | 阴鬼变阳，阴症变成阳症。 | A yin ghost changing into yang: a yin condition turns yang. |
| 阳鬼化阴，阳症变阴。 | 阳鬼变阴，阳症变成阴症。 | A yang ghost changing into yin: a yang condition turns yin. |

> **按**:「下元」按字面是下部的根本，指下焦、肾一带；「上焦」是胸膈以上。这一条和黄金策讲的「水化火火化水往来寒热」（LY.ILL.HJC.14）、「上冲下下冲上内外感伤」（LY.ILL.HJC.15）是同一个说法，易隐再添了世鬼出现与伏藏分阳症阴症、阴阳互变两条。
>
> **Note:** 'Lower origin' is literally the root of the lower part and means the lower burner and the kidneys; the 'upper burner' is the region above the chest and diaphragm. This entry is the same saying as the Huangjin Ce's 'Water into Fire and Fire into Water: alternating chills and fever' (LY.ILL.HJC.14) and 'upper clashing lower and lower clashing upper: injury within and without' (LY.ILL.HJC.15); the Yi Yin adds two things: a ghost at the Self, open or hidden, marks a yang or yin condition, and yin and yang turning into each other.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.13、LY.ILL.YY.15、LY.ILL.HJC.14、LY.ILL.HJC.15

---

## LY.ILL.YY.20 饮食：卦内无财或财空绝或四墓持用饮食不纳；禄入绝乡不进食而死；鬼动化财饮食仍进；财化鬼兄因食增病；应动合用而化财伤用忌食馈来之物；丑鬼忌牛肉、卯兔、未羊、酉鸡、戌犬、亥猪；水鬼忌鱼腥冷咸、木鬼忌果核酸、金鬼忌葱蒜姜辣、火鬼忌煎炒苦、土鬼忌米面瓜蔬甘

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里没有财，或者财爻空、绝，或者四个墓库的爻持着用爻的，吃不下东西；禄（财）落入绝地的，吃不下饭而死；鬼发动变出财的，饮食还能吃；财变出鬼、兄的，是因为吃东西使病加重；应爻发动合用爻、又变出财来伤用爻的，忌讳吃人家送来的东西。忌口：丑鬼忌牛肉，卯鬼忌兔肉，未鬼忌羊肉，酉鬼忌鸡肉，戌鬼忌狗肉，亥鬼忌猪肉；水鬼忌鱼腥、冷的、咸的东西，木鬼忌带核的果子、酸的东西，金鬼忌葱、蒜、姜、辣的东西，火鬼忌煎炒烤炙、香味浓的、苦的东西，土鬼忌米面、茄子、芋头、瓜菜、甜的东西。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If there is no Wealth in the hexagram, or Wealth is Void or in Extinction, or the four tombs hold the Subject line, food and drink are not taken in; if the emolument (Wealth) falls into a place of Extinction, he dies without taking food; if the ghost moves and changes into Wealth, food and drink can still be taken; if Wealth changes into a ghost or Peer, the illness worsens because of eating; if the Other line moves to combine with the Subject line and changes into Wealth that harms it, gifts of food sent over must be avoided. Foods to avoid: a Chou ghost must avoid beef, a Mao ghost rabbit, a Wei ghost mutton, a You ghost chicken, a Xu ghost dog, and a Hai ghost pork; a Water ghost must avoid fishy, cold and salty things, a Wood ghost fruit with stones and sour things, a Metal ghost onions, garlic, ginger and hot pungent things, a Fire ghost fried, roasted and strongly scented or bitter things, and an Earth ghost rice and wheat, aubergine, taro, melons and vegetables and sweet things.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3009 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3009*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦内无财，或财空绝，或四墓持用者，饮食不纳也。 | 卦里没有财，或者财爻空、绝，或者辰戌丑未四个墓的爻持着用爻的，吃不下东西。 | If there is no Wealth in the hexagram, or Wealth is Void or in Extinction, or the four tombs hold the Subject line, food and drink are not taken in. |
| 禄入绝乡者，不进食而死也。 | 禄（财）落入绝地的，吃不下饭而死。 | If the emolument (Wealth) falls into a place of Extinction, he dies without taking food. |
| 鬼动化财者，饮食仍进也。 | 鬼发动变出财的，饮食还能吃。 | If the ghost moves and changes into Wealth, food and drink can still be taken. |
| 财化鬼兄者，因食增病也。 | 财变出鬼、兄的，是因为吃东西使病加重。 | If Wealth changes into a ghost or Peer, the illness worsens because of eating. |
| 应动合用，而化财伤用者，忌食馈来之物也。 | 应爻发动合用爻，又变出财来伤用爻的，忌讳吃人家送来的东西。 | If the Other line moves to combine with the Subject line and changes into Wealth that harms it, gifts of food sent over must be avoided. |
| 又丑鬼忌牛肉，卯鬼忌兔肉，未鬼忌羊肉，酉鬼忌鸡肉，戌鬼忌犬肉，亥鬼忌猪肉。 | 又，丑鬼忌牛肉，卯鬼忌兔肉，未鬼忌羊肉，酉鬼忌鸡肉，戌鬼忌狗肉，亥鬼忌猪肉。 | Also a Chou ghost must avoid beef, a Mao ghost rabbit, a Wei ghost mutton, a You ghost chicken, a Xu ghost dog, and a Hai ghost pork. |
| 又水鬼忌鱼腥冷物咸物，木鬼忌果核酸物，金鬼忌葱蒜姜辣辛物，火鬼忌煿炙煎炒香味苦物，土鬼忌米面茄芋瓜蔬甘物也。 | 又，水鬼忌鱼腥、冷的、咸的东西；木鬼忌带核的果子、酸的东西；金鬼忌葱、蒜、姜、辣的东西；火鬼忌煎炒烤炙、香味浓的、苦的东西；土鬼忌米面、茄子、芋头、瓜菜、甜的东西。 | Also a Water ghost must avoid fishy, cold and salty things; a Wood ghost fruit with stones and sour things; a Metal ghost onions, garlic, ginger and hot pungent things; a Fire ghost fried, roasted and strongly scented or bitter things; and an Earth ghost rice and wheat, aubergine, taro, melons and vegetables and sweet things. |

> **按**:「禄」在这里指财爻（前面讲死卦时说「财为禄命」，LY.ILL.YY.06）。「四墓」是辰戌丑未四个墓库的地支。忌口这一段是把五行、地支和食物对起来，书里没有另讲为什么。黄金策讲饮食的地方见 LY.ILL.HJC.21（卦内无财则饮食不纳）。
>
> **Note:** 'Emolument' (lu) here means the Wealth line (in the hexagram-images that judge death the book says 'Wealth is the salary and destiny', LY.ILL.YY.06). The 'four tombs' are the four branches Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei, the tomb-storehouses. The passage on foods to avoid matches elements, branches and foods and the book does not say why. Where the Huangjin Ce treats food and drink see LY.ILL.HJC.21 ('no Wealth in the hexagram, food is not taken in').

相关:LY.ILL.YY.19、LY.ILL.YY.21、LY.ILL.HJC.21、LY.ILL.HJC.40

## LY.ILL.YY.36 十六变卦(京房)：外戒吉凶从外来、内戒祸福从内起；骸骨卦生则羸瘦死不葬埋；棺槨卦病必死亡；血脉卦主血疾漏下；绝命卦事多反复；游魂肌肉卦精神恍惚；归魂塚墓卦坟墓吉而事可成

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:每一宫都有十六个变卦，占的人遇到变入本宫卦的，灾福应验十分。外戒卦，吉凶从外面来；内戒卦，祸福从里面起；骸骨卦，活着的人羸弱消瘦，死了的人不得埋葬；棺槨卦，病人必定死亡；血脉卦，主血疾和漏下；绝命卦，事情多反复，为人孤独、和世俗不合；游魂、肌肉卦，精神恍惚，像做梦、像痴呆；归魂、坟墓卦，坟墓吉，事可以成。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Each palace has sixteen changed hexagrams, and when the diviner meets a change into a hexagram of the own palace, calamity and fortune are borne out to the full. An Outer Warning hexagram: good and ill come from outside; an Inner Warning hexagram: calamity and fortune arise from within; a Bones hexagram: the living are frail and thin, the dead are not buried; a Coffin hexagram: in illness the person surely dies; a Blood Vessels hexagram means blood disease and leakage from below; an End of Life hexagram: affairs mostly reverse and repeat, and the person is solitary and out of tune with the customs; a Wandering Soul or Flesh hexagram: the spirit is dazed, as in a dream or as if foolish; a Returning Soul or Grave hexagram: graves are auspicious and the matter can succeed.

**《易隐·十六变卦》原文** — 《易隐》第 92 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "The sixteen changed hexagrams", base text; Yi Yin, line 92*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 京房曰， | 京房这样说： | Jing Fang says: |
| 自初至五不动复下飞四往复用飞， | 从初爻变到五爻，上爻不动，再往下飞变第四爻，往复用「飞」的办法； | changing from the first line to the fifth, the top line not moving, then flying down to change the fourth, going back and forth using the flying; |
| 上飞下飞还本体， | 往上飞、往下飞，还回本体， | flying up and flying down, returning to the original body, |
| 便是十六变卦例。 | 这便是十六变卦的规矩。 | this is the rule of the sixteen changed hexagrams. |
| 如乾宫一变姤， | 比如乾宫，一变是姤， | Take the Qian palace: the first change is Gou, |
| 至五变剥， | 到五变是剥， | to the fifth change, Bo; |
| 上爻为宗庙， | 上爻是宗庙， | the top line is the ancestral temple, |
| 永不变。 | 永远不变。 | and never changes. |
| 复下飞四晋为游魂， | 再下飞第四爻，晋是游魂； | then flying down to the fourth line, Jin is the Wandering Soul; |
| 下飞三旅为外戒， | 下飞第三爻，旅是外戒； | flying down to the third, Lü is the Outer Warning; |
| 下飞二鼎为内戒， | 下飞第二爻，鼎是内戒； | flying down to the second, Ding is the Inner Warning; |
| 下飞初大有为归魂。 | 下飞初爻，大有是归魂。 | flying down to the first, Dayou is the Returning Soul. |
| 复上飞二离为绝命， | 再上飞第二爻，离是绝命； | then flying up to the second, Li is the End of Life; |
| 复上飞三噬嗑为血脉， | 再上飞第三爻，噬嗑是血脉； | then flying up to the third, Shihe is the Blood Vessels; |
| 上飞四颐为肌肉， | 上飞第四爻，颐是肌肉； | flying up to the fourth, Yi (Nourishment) is the Flesh; |
| 上飞五益为骸骨， | 上飞第五爻，益是骸骨； | flying up to the fifth, Yi (Increase) is the Bones; |
| 复下飞四无妄为棺槨， | 再下飞第四爻，无妄是棺槨； | then flying down to the fourth, Wuwang is the Coffin; |
| 下飞三同人为坟墓， | 下飞第三爻，同人是坟墓； | flying down to the third, Tongren is the Grave; |
| 下飞二乾复还本体。 | 下飞第二爻，乾，又回到本体。 | flying down to the second, Qian, back to the original body. |

**《易隐·十六变卦》原文** — 《易隐》第 92 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "The sixteen changed hexagrams", base text; Yi Yin, line 92*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡十六变八宫皆仿此， | 凡十六变，八个宫都照这样； | The sixteen changes are made likewise in all eight palaces; |
| 占者遇变入本宫卦者，灾福应十分。 | 占的人遇到变入本宫卦的，灾福应验十分。 | when the diviner meets a change into a hexagram of the own palace, calamity and fortune are borne out to the full. |
| 外戒卦，吉凶从外来。 | 外戒卦，吉凶从外面来。 | An Outer Warning hexagram: good and ill come from outside. |
| 内戒卦，祸福从内起。 | 内戒卦，祸福从里面起。 | An Inner Warning hexagram: calamity and fortune arise from within. |
| 骸骨卦，生则赢瘦，死不葬埋。 | 骸骨卦，活着羸弱消瘦，死了不得埋葬。 | A Bones hexagram: alive, frail and thin; dead, not buried. |
| 棺槨卦，病必死亡。 | 棺槨卦，病人必定死亡。 | A Coffin hexagram: in illness the person surely dies. |
| 血脉卦，主血疾漏下。 | 血脉卦，主血疾和漏下。 | A Blood Vessels hexagram means blood disease and leakage from below. |
| 绝命卦，事多反复，为人孤独，不谐于俗。 | 绝命卦，事情多反复，为人孤独，和世俗不合。 | An End of Life hexagram: affairs mostly reverse and repeat, the person is solitary, and out of tune with the customs. |
| 游魂肌肉卦，精神恍惚，如梦如癡。 | 游魂、肌肉卦，精神恍惚，像做梦、像痴呆。 | A Wandering Soul or Flesh hexagram: the spirit is dazed, as in a dream or as if foolish. |
| 归魂塚墓卦，坟墓吉，而事可成也。 | 归魂、坟墓卦，坟墓吉，事可以成。 | A Returning Soul or Grave hexagram: for graves, auspicious, and the matter can succeed. |

> **按**:这是京房的「十六变卦」：每一宫从本宫卦起，一爻一爻地变（「飞」就是变一爻），依次得到十六个卦，其中几个有专名（游魂、外戒、内戒、归魂、绝命、血脉、肌肉、骸骨、棺槨、坟墓）。头一段是乾宫的走法：乾变姤、遁、否、观、剥（五变），上爻不动；再从剥变第四爻得晋（游魂），变第三爻得旅（外戒），变第二爻得鼎（内戒），变初爻得大有（归魂）；再从大有变第二爻得离（绝命），变第三爻得噬嗑（血脉），变第四爻得颐（肌肉），变第五爻得益（骸骨），再变第四爻得无妄（棺槨），变第三爻得同人（坟墓），变第二爻回到乾。「自初至五不动」按「从初到五逐爻变、上爻不动」读，因为下文说「上爻为宗庙，永不变」。后一段是这些名称的断语，只有「棺槨卦，病必死亡」和「骸骨卦」「血脉卦」直接说到病；「游魂肌肉卦精神恍惚」和卜筮元龟的「游魂恍惚」是同一个说法。「塚墓」即冢墓，按 Grave 译。「遇变入本宫卦者」的「本宫卦」是本卦所属那一宫里的卦。断易天机里的卜筮元龟一段用了这些名称，见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.13。
>
> **Note:** This is Jing Fang's 'sixteen changed hexagrams': each palace, starting from the palace hexagram, changes one line at a time ('fei', flying, means changing one line) and gives sixteen hexagrams in turn, several of which have their own names (Wandering Soul, Outer Warning, Inner Warning, Returning Soul, End of Life, Blood Vessels, Flesh, Bones, Coffin, Grave). The first passage gives the walk for the Qian palace: Qian changes to Gou, Dun, Pi, Guan and Bo (five changes), the top line not moving; then from Bo changing the fourth line gives Jin (Wandering Soul), the third line Lü (Outer Warning), the second line Ding (Inner Warning), the first line Dayou (Returning Soul); then from Dayou changing the second line gives Li (End of Life), the third line Shihe (Blood Vessels), the fourth line Yi (Nourishment; Flesh), the fifth line Yi (Increase; Bones), then the fourth line again gives Wuwang (Coffin), the third line Tongren (Grave), and the second line returns to Qian. 'Zi chu zhi wu bu dong' is read as 'changing line by line from the first to the fifth, the top line not moving', because the text goes on to say 'the top line is the ancestral temple and never changes'. The second passage gives the judgments for these names, and only 'a Coffin hexagram: in illness the person surely dies', and the Bones and Blood Vessels hexagrams, speak of illness directly; 'a Wandering Soul or Flesh hexagram: the spirit is dazed' is the same saying as the 'Wandering Soul dazed' of the Bushi Yuangui. 'Zhong mu' (a tomb mound) is rendered Grave. The 'hexagram of the own palace' of 'a change into a hexagram of the own palace' is a hexagram in the palace to which the original hexagram belongs. The passage of the Bushi Yuangui in the Duanyi Tianji uses these names, see LY.ILL.DYTJ.13.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.13、LY.ILL.DYTJ.10、LY.ILL.CA.04
